package document

import (
	"bytes"
)

// Mutation qualification policies, NOT original format limits. Read-only Parse
// retains WP02's 16MiB and WP15's MaxTokens; admission is deliberately narrower.
const (
	MaxMutationBytes  = 65536
	MaxMutationTokens = 16384
	MaxBoldPairs      = 2
)

const (
	RevisionMismatch    Category = "RevisionMismatch"
	MutationLimit       Category = "MutationLimit"
	UnsupportedPrefix   Category = "UnsupportedPrefix"
	UnsupportedResource Category = "UnsupportedResource"
	UnsupportedMetadata Category = "UnsupportedMetadata"
	UnsupportedBody     Category = "UnsupportedBody"
	InvalidASCII        Category = "InvalidASCII"
)

// Revision is an opaque comparable identity, not a content hash. Even separately
// parsed equal bytes have distinct revisions. Its zero value identifies no image.
type Revision struct{ state *revisionState }

func (d *Document) Revision() Revision {
	if !d.valid() {
		return Revision{}
	}
	return Revision{d.state}
}
func (c Cursor) Revision() Revision {
	if !c.state.valid() {
		return Revision{}
	}
	return Revision{c.state}
}

// OriginalExport copies the immutable initially loaded image across all edits.
// New returns explicit NoLoadedOriginal for its entire lifetime. Export instead
// returns this revision's native candidate. Neither performs IO.
func (d *Document) OriginalExport() ([]byte, error) {
	if !d.valid() {
		return nil, failure(InvalidDocument, -1, noSpan)
	}
	if d.state.birth.kind == newBirth {
		return nil, failure(NoLoadedOriginal, -1, noSpan)
	}
	return d.state.birth.originalImage.Export(), nil
}

// EditResult preserves the receiver and incoming cursor on every refusal. No
// partial revision is published. Changed describes a native body change; a
// same-byte typeover only moves its cursor and does not invent metadata dirtiness.
type EditResult struct {
	Document *Document
	Cursor   Cursor
	Changed  bool
	receipt  *asciiEditReceipt
}

// The receipt is completed before publication and never points at exported
// mutable handles. History can validate actual ASCII transitions without trusting
// fabricated/rebound public result fields or retaining document ancestry chains.
type asciiEditReceipt struct {
	before, after         *revisionState
	cursor                Cursor
	changed               bool
	replaced, inputLength int
}

// CheckMutation checks the full reviewed WP23 prefix/resource/paired-body schema.
// Native parsing alone, a matching prefix size or a fixture digest is insufficient.
func (d *Document) CheckMutation() error { _, e := d.mutationAdmission(); return e }

// InsertASCII receives explicit insert semantics; session mode/key delivery is
// outside this package. Input is WP printable ASCII identity, not Unicode text.
func (d *Document) InsertASCII(c Cursor, text []byte) (EditResult, error) {
	return d.editASCII(c, text, false)
}

// TypeoverASCII is repeated original character typeover: replace the next ASCII
// token, insert before a code, append at EOF; advance one resulting token gap per
// character. Encountering a code keeps subsequent characters before that code.
func (d *Document) TypeoverASCII(c Cursor, text []byte) (EditResult, error) {
	return d.editASCII(c, text, true)
}

func (d *Document) editASCII(c Cursor, text []byte, over bool) (EditResult, error) {
	unchanged := EditResult{Document: d, Cursor: c}
	if !d.valid() {
		return unchanged, failure(InvalidDocument, -1, noSpan)
	}
	if c.state != d.state {
		return unchanged, failure(RevisionMismatch, c.gap, noSpan)
	}
	// Cursor fields are private, but retain explicit checks before indexing.
	if c.gap < 0 || c.gap > d.TokenCount() {
		return unchanged, failure(InvalidPosition, c.gap, noSpan)
	}
	_, e := d.mutationAdmission()
	if e != nil {
		return unchanged, e
	}
	if len(text) > MaxMutationTokens {
		return unchanged, failure(MutationLimit, c.gap, noSpan)
	}
	for i, b := range text {
		if b < 0x20 || b > 0x7e {
			return unchanged, failure(InvalidASCII, i, noSpan)
		}
	}
	if len(text) == 0 {
		unchanged.receipt = &asciiEditReceipt{before: d.state, after: d.state, cursor: c}
		return unchanged, nil
	}
	raw := d.state.image.Export()
	start := int(d.state.gaps[c.gap])
	end := start
	replaced := 0
	if over {
		for replaced < len(text) && c.gap+replaced < d.TokenCount() {
			span := d.span(c.gap + replaced)
			if identify(raw[span.Offset:span.Offset+span.Length]) != ASCII {
				break
			}
			end += span.Length
			replaced++
		}
	}
	// Subtraction precedes addition, with input text already bounded.
	size := len(raw) - (end - start) + len(text)
	count := d.TokenCount() - replaced + len(text)
	if size > MaxMutationBytes || count > MaxMutationTokens {
		return unchanged, failure(MutationLimit, c.gap, noSpan)
	}
	bodyStart := int(d.state.gaps[0])
	body := make([]byte, 0, size-bodyStart)
	body = append(body, raw[bodyStart:start]...)
	body = append(body, text...)
	body = append(body, raw[end:]...)
	r, e := d.publishBody(c, body, count, c.gap+len(text), false)
	if e == nil {
		r.receipt = &asciiEditReceipt{before: d.state, after: r.Document.state, cursor: r.Cursor, changed: r.Changed, replaced: replaced, inputLength: len(text)}
	}
	return r, e
}

// The sole semantic candidate publisher. Only domain commands call it, after
// whole-token edits. forceFormat is authorized for confirmed blocked-pair repair;
// no-edit/decline/preview paths never call it. It preserves the owned input prefix
// and applies only checked indexed packet6 bit3, never a template or relocation.
func (d *Document) publishBody(c Cursor, body []byte, tokens, gap int, forceFormat bool) (EditResult, error) {
	unchanged := EditResult{Document: d, Cursor: c}
	if !d.valid() {
		return unchanged, failure(InvalidDocument, -1, noSpan)
	}
	if c.state != d.state {
		return unchanged, failure(RevisionMismatch, c.gap, noSpan)
	}
	flags, e := d.mutationAdmission()
	if e != nil {
		return unchanged, e
	}
	start := int(d.state.gaps[0])
	if tokens < 0 || tokens > MaxMutationTokens || len(body) > MaxMutationBytes-start {
		return unchanged, failure(MutationLimit, gap, noSpan)
	}
	if gap < 0 || gap > tokens {
		return unchanged, failure(InvalidPosition, gap, noSpan)
	}
	raw := d.state.image.Export()
	changed := !bytes.Equal(raw[start:], body)
	cursor := c
	cursor.gap = gap
	if !changed && (!forceFormat || raw[flags]&8 != 0) {
		return EditResult{Document: d, Cursor: cursor}, nil
	}
	candidate := make([]byte, 0, start+len(body))
	candidate = append(candidate, raw[:start]...)
	candidate = append(candidate, body...)
	candidate[flags] |= 8
	next, e := parseRevision(candidate, d.state.birth)
	if e != nil {
		return unchanged, e
	}
	if next.TokenCount() != tokens {
		return unchanged, failure(InvalidDocument, -1, noSpan)
	}
	if e = next.CheckMutation(); e != nil {
		return unchanged, e
	}
	cursor.state = next.state
	return EditResult{Document: next, Cursor: cursor, Changed: changed}, nil
}

package document

import (
	"bytes"
	"encoding/binary"
	"github.com/lynncoleart/wordling/internal/wp51"
	"sort"
)

// Qualified resource field values transcribed from approved WP23 field-ledger.json
// (72 complete fields plus index schemas), SDK51 WP3–18 and WP103–105.
// These are individual closed resource/metadata values, not a serialized prefix
// template or a fixture whitelist. Input ordering/locations are preserved. The
// body pointer, packet references and span ownership are validated separately.
// Hash variants are literal caches; no hash/font/counter algorithm is invented.
type qualifiedField struct {
	name   string
	offset int
	value  string
}

var qualifiedPacketFields = map[uint16][]qualifiedField{
	7: {
		{"font name string pool", 0, "\x43\x6f\x75\x72\x69\x65\x72\x20\x31\x30\x63\x70\x69\x00"},
	},
	15: {
		{"font instance ID", 0, "\x00\x00"},
		{"proportional-spacing cache ID", 2, "\xff\xff"},
		{"top shoulder height", 4, "\x89\x00"},
		{"bottom shoulder height", 6, "\x3f\x00"},
		{"printed character width", 8, "\x78\x00"},
		{"optimal space width", 10, "\x78\x00"},
		{"normal space width", 12, "\x78\x00"},
		{"horizontal motion units", 14, "\x0a\x00"},
		{"horizontal fractional numerator", 16, "\x01\x00"},
		{"font string pool offset", 18, "\x00\x00"},
		{"font flags", 20, "\x00"},

		{"descriptor: cell height", 23, "\xf4\x01"},

func (d *Document) mutationAdmission() (int, error) {
	if !d.valid() {
		return 0, failure(InvalidDocument, -1, noSpan)
	}
	info := d.state.image.Inspect()
	if info.Prefix.Length+info.Body.Length > MaxMutationBytes || d.TokenCount() > MaxMutationTokens {
		return 0, failure(MutationLimit, -1, info.Body)
	}
	if len(info.Blocks) != 2 || len(info.Packets) != 5 {
		return 0, failure(UnsupportedPrefix, -1, info.Prefix)
	}
	raw := d.state.image.Export()
	// The approved reader checks native identity, arithmetic, bounds, cycles and
	// delimiters. Consume its actual packet/block spans, never reparse a codec.
	first, last := info.Blocks[0], info.Blocks[1]
	if first.Data.Offset != 16 || first.Data.Length != 50 || last.Data.Length != 50 || first.Count != 5 || last.Count != 5 || first.Next != uint32(last.Data.Offset) || last.Next != 0 {
		return 0, failure(UnsupportedPrefix, -1, info.Prefix)
	}
	// Reader deliberately ignores unused slots/terminator payload; mutation may not.
	if !bytes.Equal(raw[last.Data.Offset+20:last.Data.Offset+50], make([]byte, 30)) {
		return 0, failure(UnsupportedPrefix, -1, last.Data)
	}
	types := [...]uint16{7, 15, 12, 6, 8}
	// WP42's complete pre-print field/extent proof qualifies one additional
	// linked index order. Physical payload offsets still come from Inspect.
	reordered := info.Packets[0].Type == 6
	if reordered {
		types = [...]uint16{6, 7, 15, 12, 8}
	}
	sizes := map[uint16]int{7: 14, 15: 86, 12: 90, 6: 16, 8: 2}
	spans := []wp51.Span{{Offset: 0, Length: 16}, first.Data, last.Data}
	flags := -1
	var pool, font []byte
	for i, p := range info.Packets {
		expectedIndex := first.Data.Offset + 10*(i+1)
		if i == 4 {
			expectedIndex = last.Data.Offset + 10
		}
		if p.Type != types[i] || p.Data.Length != sizes[p.Type] || p.Index != (wp51.Span{Offset: expectedIndex, Length: 10}) {
			return 0, failure(UnsupportedPrefix, -1, p.Index)
		}
		spans = append(spans, p.Data)
		packet := raw[p.Data.Offset : p.Data.Offset+p.Data.Length]
		category := UnsupportedResource
		if p.Type == 6 {
			category = UnsupportedMetadata
			flags = p.Data.Offset
		}
		if p.Type == 8 {
			category = UnsupportedPrefix
		}
		for _, f := range qualifiedPacketFields[p.Type] {
			actual := packet[f.offset : f.offset+len(f.value)]
			allowed := string(actual) == f.value
			if p.Type == 15 && f.offset == 21 && !reordered {
				allowed = allowed || string(actual) == "\x11\x72"
			}
			if p.Type == 6 && f.offset == 0 {
				allowed = actual[0] == 0 || actual[0] == 8
			}
			if !allowed {
				return 0, failure(category, -1, wp51.Span{Offset: p.Data.Offset + f.offset, Length: len(f.value)})
			}
		}
		if p.Type == 7 {
			pool = packet
		}
		if p.Type == 15 {
			font = packet
		}
		if p.Type == 8 && p.Data.Offset+p.Data.Length != info.Body.Offset {
			return 0, failure(UnsupportedPrefix, -1, p.Data)
		}
	}
	// All prefix bytes are owned exactly once, including physically relocated
	// packets. This derives prefix length; it never assumes B=324 or flags=256.
	sort.Slice(spans, func(i, j int) bool { return spans[i].Offset < spans[j].Offset })
	end := 0
	for _, span := range spans {
		if span.Offset != end {
			return 0, failure(UnsupportedPrefix, -1, span)
		}
		end += span.Length
	}
	if end != info.Body.Offset {
		return 0, failure(UnsupportedPrefix, -1, info.Prefix)
	}
	pointer := int(binary.LittleEndian.Uint16(font[18:20]))
	if pointer >= len(pool) || bytes.IndexByte(pool[pointer:], 0) < 0 {
		return 0, failure(UnsupportedResource, -1, info.Prefix)
	}
	// One font row. AFC FF=no chain, FE=unavailable; other values must index it.
	for _, ref := range font[50:86] {
		if ref != 254 && ref != 255 && ref >= 1 {
			return 0, failure(UnsupportedResource, -1, info.Prefix)
		}
	}
	if font[62] != 255 {
		return 0, failure(UnsupportedResource, -1, info.Prefix)
	}
	bold := false
	pairs := 0
	for i := 0; i < d.TokenCount(); i++ {
		span := d.span(i)
		switch identify(raw[span.Offset : span.Offset+span.Length]) {
		case ASCII:
		case BoldOn:
			if bold || pairs >= MaxBoldPairs {
				return 0, failure(UnsupportedBody, i, span)
			}
			bold = true
			pairs++
		case BoldOff:
			if !bold {
				return 0, failure(UnsupportedBody, i, span)
			}
			bold = false
		default:
			return 0, failure(UnsupportedBody, i, span)
		}
	}
	if bold {
		return 0, failure(UnsupportedBody, d.TokenCount(), info.Body)
	}
	return flags, nil
}


=== ACTUAL_APPROVED_H71_SEMANTIC_SOURCE cd983c05401a90c1f0c912c60ab6ca23d8bd437b:internal/document/history.go ===
package document

import "github.com/lynncoleart/wordling/internal/wp51"

// Transient implementation policies, not original disk/memory capacity limits.
const (
	HistoryCapacity         = 3
	MaxHistoryFragmentBytes = MaxMutationBytes
)

type HistoryBoundary uint8

const (
	SavedBoundary HistoryBoundary = iota + 1
	RightBoundary
)

// History wraps private immutable state. NewHistory explicitly creates a fresh
// transient context; Parse, retrieve and clearing a document NEVER reset history.
// Cross-document continuation is unqualified and refuses rather than clearing it.
type History struct{ state *historyState }
type HistoryRevision struct{ state *historyState }
type historyState struct {
	document                *revisionState
	entries                 [HistoryCapacity]historyEntry
	count                   int
	groupOpen, groupUnknown bool
	effectUnqualified       bool
	groupGap                int
	seedView                bool
	seedStage               seedQualification
	observed                *historyObservation
}
type historyEntry struct {
	fragment fragment
	kind     DeletionKind
	commands int
}
type fragment struct {
	native      string
	kinds       []Kind
	codeIndices []uint16
}

func (f fragment) offset(index int) int {
	offset := index
	for _, code := range f.codeIndices {
		if int(code) < index {
			offset += 2
		}
	}
	return offset
}
func (d *Document) fragment(start, end int) fragment {
	span := wp51.Span{Offset: int(d.state.gaps[start]), Length: int(d.state.gaps[end] - d.state.gaps[start])}
	raw, _ := d.state.image.Bytes(span)
	f := fragment{native: string(raw), kinds: make([]Kind, end-start)}
	for i := start; i < end; i++ {
		s := d.span(i)
		local := s.Offset - span.Offset
		k := identify(raw[local : local+s.Length])
		f.kinds[i-start] = k
		if k == BoldOn || k == BoldOff {
			f.codeIndices = append(f.codeIndices, uint16(i-start))
		}
	}
	return f
}
func joinFragments(a, b fragment) fragment {
	f := fragment{native: a.native + b.native, kinds: make([]Kind, len(a.kinds)+len(b.kinds))}
	copy(f.kinds, a.kinds)
	copy(f.kinds[len(a.kinds):], b.kinds)
	f.codeIndices = append([]uint16(nil), a.codeIndices...)
	for _, index := range b.codeIndices {
		f.codeIndices = append(f.codeIndices, uint16(int(index)+len(a.kinds)))
	}
	return f
}
func NewHistory(d *Document) (History, error) {
	if !d.valid() {
		return History{}, failure(InvalidDocument, -1, noSpan)
	}
	if e := d.CheckMutation(); e != nil {
		return History{}, e
	}
	return History{&historyState{document: d.state}}, nil
}
func (h History) Revision() HistoryRevision { return HistoryRevision{h.state} }

// Qualified distinguishes enabled history behavior from preserved earlier
// entries after an edit whose immediate history effect has not been observed.
func (h History) Qualified() bool { return h.state != nil && !h.state.effectUnqualified }

// CountKnown reports whether stored fragment count also models actual stack
// count. Bounded observations deliberately withhold this stronger knowledge.
func (h History) CountKnown() bool {
	return h.state != nil && h.state.observed == nil && !h.state.effectUnqualified
}

// Count is stored fragment knowledge; CountKnown distinguishes an actual stack
// model from bounded WP40 observations with deliberately unknown hidden count.
func (h History) Count() int {
	if h.state == nil {
		return 0
	}
	return h.state.count
}
func (h History) check(d *Document) error {
	if h.state == nil {
		return failure(InvalidHistory, -1, noSpan)
	}
	if h.state.document != d.state {
		return failure(HistoryMismatch, -1, noSpan)
	}
	if h.state.effectUnqualified {
		return failure(UnsupportedHistoryEffect, -1, noSpan)
	}
	return nil
}

// AfterEdit advances only a real immutable ASCII receipt. Native editing remains
// admitted independently of unobserved history effects: prior fragments survive,
// but subsequent history/deletion commands explicitly refuse after typeover
// replacement or insertion with existing history. The known Restore/reopen/X
// sequence remains admitted; no post-X history observation is invented.
func (h History) AfterEdit(r EditResult) (History, error) {
	if h.state == nil {
		return h, failure(InvalidHistory, -1, noSpan)
	}
	a := r.receipt
	if a == nil || !r.Document.valid() || r.Document.state != a.after || r.Cursor != a.cursor || r.Changed != a.changed {
		return h, failure(InvalidEditResult, -1, noSpan)
	}
	if a.before != h.state.document {
		return h, failure(HistoryMismatch, -1, noSpan)
	}
	if a.inputLength == 0 {
		return h, nil
	}
	next := *h.state
	next.document = a.after
	next.seedStage = seedUnqualified
	next.groupOpen = false
	next.groupUnknown = false
	if a.replaced > 0 || next.count > 0 {
		next.effectUnqualified = true
	}
	return History{&next}, nil
}

// EntryInfo is a copied inspection value; Native is original WP bytes, not labels.
type EntryInfo struct {
	Native           []byte
	Kind             DeletionKind
	Commands, Tokens int
	Qualified        bool
}

func (h History) Entry(index int) (EntryInfo, error) {
	if h.state == nil {
		return EntryInfo{}, failure(InvalidHistory, -1, noSpan)
	}
	if index < 0 || index >= h.state.count {
		return EntryInfo{}, failure(InvalidPosition, index, noSpan)
	}
	entry := h.state.entries[index]
	return EntryInfo{[]byte(entry.fragment.native), entry.kind, entry.commands, len(entry.fragment.kinds), h.Qualified()}, nil
}

// Separate records only the observed successful Save and Right grouping barriers.
// The dispatcher must report actual intervening commands; it may not reinterpret
// arbitrary keys/timing as these barriers or silently clear an existing stack.
func (h History) Separate(boundary HistoryBoundary) (History, error) {
	if h.state == nil {
		return h, failure(InvalidHistory, -1, noSpan)
	}
	if h.state.effectUnqualified || h.state.observed != nil {
		return h, failure(UnsupportedHistoryEffect, -1, noSpan)
	}
	if boundary != SavedBoundary && boundary != RightBoundary {
		return h, failure(UnsupportedHistoryBoundary, -1, noSpan)
	}
	next := *h.state
	next.groupOpen = false
	next.groupUnknown = false
	if boundary == RightBoundary && (next.seedStage == seedBrowsed || next.seedStage == seedRightSeparated) {
		next.seedStage = seedRightSeparated
	} else {
		next.seedStage = seedUnqualified
	}
	return History{&next}, nil
}

// InterruptGrouping records a non-deleting interleaving whose grouping effect
// is unqualified (for example a view command between deletions). Entries and
// document state remain known; continuation refuses until an observed Save/Right
// separator. This is not a generic key router or a guessed new-deletion rule.
func (h History) InterruptGrouping() (History, error) {
	if h.state == nil {
		return h, failure(InvalidHistory, -1, noSpan)
	}
	if h.state.effectUnqualified || h.state.observed != nil {
		return h, failure(UnsupportedHistoryEffect, -1, noSpan)
	}
	next := *h.state
	next.groupUnknown = next.groupUnknown || next.groupOpen
	next.groupOpen = false
	next.seedStage = seedUnqualified
	return History{&next}, nil
}
func (h History) record(s deletionState, f fragment, after *revisionState) (History, error) {
	if len(f.native) > MaxHistoryFragmentBytes || len(f.kinds) > MaxMutationTokens || len(f.codeIndices) > 2*MaxBoldPairs {
		return h, failure(MutationLimit, -1, noSpan)
	}
	next := *h.state
	next.document = after
	next.seedStage = seedUnqualified
	if s.kind == CharacterDeletion && s.target == 0 && h.state.count == 0 && len(f.kinds) == 1 && f.kinds[0] == ASCII && wp40Shape(after) {
		next.seedStage = seedAwaitingBrowse
		next.seedView = s.cursor.reveal
	}
	next.groupUnknown = false
	consecutive := s.kind == CharacterDeletion && s.direction == ForwardDelete && next.groupOpen && s.cursor.gap == next.groupGap && next.count > 0 && next.entries[0].kind == CharacterDeletion
	if consecutive {
		old := next.entries[0]
		if len(old.fragment.native) > MaxHistoryFragmentBytes-len(f.native) || len(old.fragment.kinds) > MaxMutationTokens-len(f.kinds) {
			return h, failure(MutationLimit, -1, noSpan)
		}
		old.fragment = joinFragments(old.fragment, f)
		old.commands++
		next.entries[0] = old
	} else {
		for i := HistoryCapacity - 1; i > 0; i-- {
			next.entries[i] = next.entries[i-1]
		}
		next.entries[0] = historyEntry{f, s.kind, 1}
		if next.count < HistoryCapacity {
			next.count++
		}
	}
	next.groupOpen = s.kind == CharacterDeletion && s.direction == ForwardDelete
	next.groupGap = s.target
	return History{&next}, nil
}

// Preview captures actual history/document state, not a mutable public handle.
// It is a read-only overlay at the current cursor, never an inserted document.
type Preview struct{ state *previewState }
type previewState struct {
	history *historyState
	cursor  Cursor
	index   int
	steps   int
}

func (p Preview) Active() bool {
	return p.state != nil && p.state.history != nil && p.state.history.count > 0
}
func (p Preview) Index() int {
	if !p.Active() {
		return -1
	}
	return p.state.index
}
func (p Preview) Cursor() Cursor {
	if p.state == nil {
		return Cursor{}
	}
	return p.state.cursor
}
func (p Preview) Prompt() string {
	if !p.Active() {
		return ""
	}
	return "Undelete: 1 Restore; 2 Previous Deletion: 0"
}
func (h History) Open(c Cursor) (Preview, error) {
	if h.state == nil {
		return Preview{}, failure(InvalidHistory, -1, noSpan)
	}
	if h.state.effectUnqualified {
		return Preview{}, failure(UnsupportedHistoryEffect, -1, noSpan)
	}
	if h.state.observed != nil && (c.gap != h.state.observed.gap || c.reveal != h.state.observed.reveal) {
		return Preview{}, failure(UnsupportedHistoryEffect, c.gap, noSpan)
	}
	if c.state != h.state.document {
		return Preview{}, failure(RevisionMismatch, c.gap, noSpan)
	}
	if h.state.count == 0 {
		return Preview{}, nil
	}
	return Preview{&previewState{history: h.state, cursor: c}}, nil
}
func (p Preview) Previous() (Preview, error) {
	if !p.Active() {
		return p, failure(HistoryEmpty, -1, noSpan)
	}
	next := *p.state
	if next.history.observed != nil {
		if next.steps >= 3 {
			return p, failure(UnsupportedHistoryEffect, -1, noSpan)
		}
		next.index++
		next.steps++
	} else {
		next.index = (next.index + 1) % next.history.count
		next.steps++
	}
	return Preview{&next}, nil
}
func (h History) CancelPreview(p Preview) (History, error) {
	if !p.Active() {
		return h, failure(HistoryEmpty, -1, noSpan)
	}
	if h.state != p.state.history {
		return h, failure(HistoryMismatch, -1, noSpan)
	}
	next := *h.state
	if next.observed != nil {
		if p.state.steps != 3 {
			return h, failure(UnsupportedHistoryEffect, -1, noSpan)
		}
		observation := *next.observed
		observation.browsed = true
		next.observed = &observation
	}
	if next.seedStage == seedAwaitingBrowse && p.state.steps == 3 && p.state.cursor.gap == 0 && p.state.cursor.reveal == next.seedView {
		next.seedStage = seedBrowsed
	} else if next.seedStage != seedRightSeparated {
		next.seedStage = seedUnqualified
	}
	// No post-preview continued-delete grouping probe exists. Preserve entries;
	// refuse that unqualified interleaving until a known Save/Right separator.
	next.groupUnknown = next.groupUnknown || next.groupOpen
	next.groupOpen = false
	return History{&next}, nil
}
func (d *Document) Restore(h History, p Preview) (Deletion, error) {
	unchanged := unchangedDeletion(d, p.Cursor(), h)
	if !p.Active() {
		return unchanged, failure(HistoryEmpty, -1, noSpan)
	}
	if e := d.checkDeletion(p.Cursor(), h); e != nil {
		return unchanged, e
	}
	if p.state.history != h.state {
		return unchanged, failure(HistoryMismatch, -1, noSpan)
	}
	if h.state.observed != nil && (h.state.observed.restored || !h.state.observed.browsed || p.state.index != 0) {
		return unchanged, failure(UnsupportedHistoryEffect, -1, noSpan)
	}
	f := h.state.entries[p.fragmentIndex()].fragment
	if len(f.kinds) > MaxMutationTokens-d.TokenCount() {
		return unchanged, failure(MutationLimit, p.Cursor().gap, noSpan)
	}
	raw := d.state.image.Export()
	start := int(d.state.gaps[0])
	at := int(d.state.gaps[p.Cursor().gap])
	bodyLength := len(raw) - start
	if len(f.native) > MaxMutationBytes-start-bodyLength {
		return unchanged, failure(MutationLimit, p.Cursor().gap, noSpan)
	}
	body := make([]byte, 0, bodyLength+len(f.native))
	body = append(body, raw[start:at]...)
	body = append(body, f.native...)
	body = append(body, raw[at:]...)
	edit, e := d.publishBody(p.Cursor(), body, d.TokenCount()+len(f.kinds), p.Cursor().gap+len(f.kinds), false)
	if e != nil {
		return unchanged, e
	}
	next := *h.state
	next.document = edit.Document.state
	if next.observed != nil {
		observation := *next.observed
		observation.restored = true
		observation.gap = edit.Cursor.gap
		observation.browsed = false
		next.observed = &observation
	}
	next.groupOpen = false
	next.groupUnknown = false
	// EC204 proves one attributed Restore, not its later stack lifetime. Keep
	// prior bytes but refuse subsequent queries; plain/opening-repair retention
	// is independently observed and remains enabled.
	if h.state.entries[p.fragmentIndex()].kind == AttributedDeletion {
		next.effectUnqualified = true
	}
	return Deletion{&deletionState{before: d.state, after: edit.Document.state, beforeHistory: h.state, afterHistory: &next, cursor: edit.Cursor, kind: Restoration, applied: true, bodyChanged: edit.Changed, nativeChanged: d.state != edit.Document.state}}, nil
}

type OverlayOrigin uint8

const (
	DocumentToken OverlayOrigin = iota
	DeletionFragment
)

// Fragment spans are absent (Offset=-1): they are not current native-file bytes.
// FragmentStart/End identify virtual selection; the UI's [Block] marker is not a
// native token and must never be serialized into either document or history.
type OverlayElement struct {
	Origin     OverlayOrigin
	TokenIndex int
	Kind       Kind
	Span       wp51.Span
	Raw        []byte
	Text       string
}
type Overlay struct {
	Elements                                           []OverlayElement
	FragmentStart, FragmentEnd, CursorGap, TotalTokens int
	Reveal                                             bool
}

func (p Preview) Present(start, count int, reveal bool) (Overlay, error) {
	if !p.Active() {
		return Overlay{}, failure(HistoryEmpty, -1, noSpan)
	}
	d := &Document{state: p.state.history.document}
	f := p.state.history.entries[p.fragmentIndex()].fragment
	gap := p.state.cursor.gap
	total := d.TokenCount() + len(f.kinds)
	if start < 0 || start > total || count < 0 || count > total-start {
		return Overlay{}, failure(InvalidPosition, start, noSpan)
	}
	if count > MaxWindowTokens {
		return Overlay{}, failure(WindowLimit, start, noSpan)
	}
	out := Overlay{Elements: make([]OverlayElement, count), FragmentStart: gap, FragmentEnd: gap + len(f.kinds), CursorGap: gap, TotalTokens: total, Reveal: reveal}
	for i := start; i < start+count; i++ {
		var e OverlayElement
		if i >= gap && i < gap+len(f.kinds) {
			index := i - gap
			e = OverlayElement{Origin: DeletionFragment, TokenIndex: index, Kind: f.kinds[index], Span: noSpan, Raw: []byte(f.native[f.offset(index):f.offset(index+1)])}
		} else {
			index := i
			if i >= gap+len(f.kinds) {
				index -= len(f.kinds)
			}
			token, _ := d.Token(index)
			e = OverlayElement{Origin: DocumentToken, TokenIndex: index, Kind: token.Kind, Span: token.Span, Raw: token.Raw}
		}
		if e.Kind == ASCII {
			e.Text = string(e.Raw)
		} else if reveal {
			if e.Kind == BoldOn {
				e.Text = "[BOLD]"
			} else {
				e.Text = "[bold]"
			}
		}
		out.Elements[i-start] = e
	}
	return out, nil
}


=== ACTUAL_APPROVED_H71_SEMANTIC_SOURCE cd983c05401a90c1f0c912c60ab6ca23d8bd437b:internal/document/history_observation.go ===
package document

// This is a finite history-knowledge state inside the existing History, not an
// inferred Add/Skip/Clear rule. Four actual positions can be indistinguishable;
// stored fragments do not assert the original hidden entry count or ordering.
type seedQualification uint8

const (
	seedUnqualified seedQualification = iota
	seedAwaitingBrowse
	seedBrowsed
	seedRightSeparated
)

type historyObservation struct {
	closing, restored, browsed bool
	gap                        int
	reveal                     bool
}

// The qualification class fixes command order, view and token shape, while all
// character identities/fragments come from the actual immutable native values.
// Character values are not control commands or prerecorded-screen selectors.
func wp40Shape(state *revisionState) bool {
	d := &Document{state: state}
	if !d.valid() || d.TokenCount() != 6 {
		return false
	}
	for i, k := range []Kind{ASCII, BoldOn, ASCII, ASCII, BoldOff, ASCII} {
		token, _ := d.Token(i)
		if token.Kind != k {
			return false
		}
	}
	return true
}
func (h History) wp40Target(s deletionState) bool {
	if h.state == nil || h.state.observed != nil || h.state.seedStage != seedRightSeparated || h.state.seedView != s.cursor.reveal || !wp40Shape(h.state.document) {
		return false
	}
	if h.state.count != 1 || len(h.state.entries[0].fragment.kinds) != 1 || h.state.entries[0].fragment.kinds[0] != ASCII || h.state.entries[0].commands != 1 {
		return false
	}
	return s.kind == PairDeletion || (s.kind == ClosingRepair && s.blockStart == 4 && s.blockEnd == 5 && s.cursor.reveal)
}
func (h History) observeTarget(s deletionState, closing fragment, after *revisionState) History {
	next := *h.state
	next.document = after
	next.groupOpen = false
	next.groupUnknown = false
	next.seedStage = 0
	gap := s.target
	if s.kind == ClosingRepair {
		gap = s.blockEnd
	} else if s.target == s.off {
		gap--
	}
	next.observed = &historyObservation{closing: s.kind == ClosingRepair, gap: gap, reveal: s.cursor.reveal}
	if s.kind == ClosingRepair {
		next.entries[1] = next.entries[0]
		next.entries[0] = historyEntry{fragment: closing, kind: ClosingRepair, commands: 1}
		next.count = 2 // number of distinct stored observed fragments, NOT stack size.
	}
	return History{&next}
}
func (p Preview) fragmentIndex() int {
	if p.state.history.observed != nil {
		if p.state.history.observed.closing {
			return p.state.index % 2
		}
		return 0
	}
	return p.state.index
}


=== ACTUAL_APPROVED_H71_SEMANTIC_SOURCE cd983c05401a90c1f0c912c60ab6ca23d8bd437b:internal/document/document.go ===
// Package document exposes immutable native revisions and shared normal/Reveal
// token gaps. Semantic writes require the narrower WP23 mutation admission.
package document

import (
	"encoding/binary"
	"fmt"

	"github.com/lynncoleart/wordling/internal/wp51"
)

// Safety policies, not original application limits. The gap index is at most
// (MaxTokens+1)*4 bytes; a presentation allocates at most MaxWindowTokens elements.
const (
	MaxTokens       = 1 << 20
	MaxWindowTokens = 4096
)

type Category string

const (
	InvalidDocument  Category = "InvalidDocument"
	InvalidPosition  Category = "InvalidPosition"
	InvalidDirection Category = "InvalidDirection"
	TokenLimit       Category = "TokenLimit"
	WindowLimit      Category = "WindowLimit"
	UnsupportedToken Category = "UnsupportedToken"
)

// Failure identifies a document refusal. Native-reader errors pass through with
// their original *wp51.Failure type. A negative index/span denotes no token.
type Failure struct {
	Category   Category
	TokenIndex int
	Span       wp51.Span
}

func (e *Failure) Error() string {
	return fmt.Sprintf("document: %s at token %d, span %d+%d", e.Category, e.TokenIndex, e.Span.Offset, e.Span.Length)
}
func failure(k Category, index int, span wp51.Span) error { return &Failure{k, index, span} }

var noSpan = wp51.Span{Offset: -1}

type Kind uint8

const (
	Opaque Kind = iota
	ASCII
	BoldOn
	BoldOff
)

// Token is an independent inspection value. Raw always retains the exact native
// bytes, even for an opaque token whose movement/presentation is unsupported.
type Token struct {
	Kind Kind
	Span wp51.Span
	Raw  []byte
}

// Document must be constructed by Parse or New. It retains one immutable dependency
// image and a compact private absolute-gap index, with no mutable document state.
type Document struct{ state *revisionState }

// A caller may copy or replace the exported Document wrapper. Cursor and
// Revision capture this private immutable state, never that mutable wrapper.
// No state pointer, image pointer or gap slice is exposed to callers.
type revisionState struct {
	image *wp51.Image
	gaps  []uint32
	birth *birthLineage
}

func (s *revisionState) valid() bool {
	return s != nil && s.image != nil && s.birth != nil && len(s.gaps) > 0
}

// Parse delegates all native validity decisions to the approved reader. The
// following indexing pass only locates already-validated token spans; it does not
// decode opaque payloads, nested text, paired-code relationships or edit safety.
func Parse(input []byte) (*Document, error) { return parseRevision(input, nil) }

// One native parser/indexer. A nil birth means an explicit Loaded acquisition;
// internal validation and New supply their captured immutable lineage.
func parseRevision(input []byte, birth *birthLineage) (*Document, error) {
	image, err := wp51.Parse(input)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	info := image.Inspect()
	if info.OuterTokenCount > MaxTokens {
		return nil, failure(TokenLimit, -1, info.Body)
	}
	body, _ := image.Bytes(info.Body)
	gaps := make([]uint32, info.OuterTokenCount+1)
	offset := 0
	for i := 0; i < info.OuterTokenCount; i++ {
		gaps[i] = uint32(info.Body.Offset + offset)
		offset += framedWidth(body[offset:])
	}
	gaps[len(gaps)-1] = uint32(info.Body.Offset + len(body))
	if birth == nil {
		birth = &birthLineage{kind: loadedBirth, originalImage: image}
	}
	return &Document{state: &revisionState{image: image, gaps: gaps, birth: birth}}, nil
}

// These SDK WP51 outer-width facts are used only after wp51.Parse has checked
// every delimiter/length/bound. Reusing their span lengths is not another native
// validator or permission to interpret payloads. In particular C0 remains opaque.
func framedWidth(raw []byte) int {
	lead := raw[0]
	if lead < 0xc0 {
		return 1
	}
	if lead < 0xd0 {
		return [...]int{4, 9, 11, 3, 3, 5, 6, 7, 4, 5, 6, 6, 8, 10, 10, 12}[lead-0xc0]
	}
	return int(binary.LittleEndian.Uint16(raw[2:4])) + 4
}
func identify(raw []byte) Kind {
	if len(raw) == 1 && raw[0] >= 0x20 && raw[0] <= 0x7e {
		return ASCII
	}
	if len(raw) == 3 && raw[1] == 0x0c && raw[0] == raw[2] {
		if raw[0] == 0xc3 {
			return BoldOn
		}
		if raw[0] == 0xc4 {
			return BoldOff
		}
	}
	return Opaque
}
func (d *Document) valid() bool { return d != nil && d.state.valid() }
func (d *Document) TokenCount() int {
	if !d.valid() {
		return 0
	}
	return len(d.state.gaps) - 1
}
func (d *Document) Export() ([]byte, error) {
	if !d.valid() {
		return nil, failure(InvalidDocument, -1, noSpan)
	}
	return d.state.image.Export(), nil
}
func (d *Document) span(index int) wp51.Span {
	return wp51.Span{Offset: int(d.state.gaps[index]), Length: int(d.state.gaps[index+1] - d.state.gaps[index])}
}
func (d *Document) Token(index int) (Token, error) {
	if !d.valid() {
		return Token{}, failure(InvalidDocument, index, noSpan)
	}
	if index < 0 || index >= d.TokenCount() {
		return Token{}, failure(InvalidPosition, index, noSpan)
	}
	span := d.span(index)
	raw, _ := d.state.image.Bytes(span)
	return Token{Kind: identify(raw), Span: span, Raw: raw}, nil
}

// Cursor is a value: view and movement operations return a new cursor, preserving
// the caller's previous cursor on failure. Its document remains immutable.
type Cursor struct {
	state  *revisionState
	gap    int
	reveal bool
}

// CursorAt constructs a valid token-gap coordinate, not an original GoTo/Home
// command. Zero and TokenCount are the body boundaries, never byte positions.
func (d *Document) CursorAt(gap int) (Cursor, error) {
	if !d.valid() {
		return Cursor{}, failure(InvalidDocument, -1, noSpan)
	}
	if gap < 0 || gap > d.TokenCount() {
		return Cursor{}, failure(InvalidPosition, gap, noSpan)
	}
	return Cursor{state: d.state, gap: gap}, nil
}
func (c Cursor) Gap() int                    { return c.gap }
func (c Cursor) Reveal() bool                { return c.reveal }
func (c Cursor) WithReveal(show bool) Cursor { c.reveal = show; return c }
func (c Cursor) Offset() (int, error) {
	if !c.state.valid() {
		return 0, failure(InvalidDocument, -1, noSpan)
	}
	return int(c.state.gaps[c.gap]), nil
}
func (c Cursor) Move(direction int) (Cursor, error) {
	d := &Document{state: c.state}
	if !c.state.valid() {
		return c, failure(InvalidDocument, -1, noSpan)
	}
	if direction != -1 && direction != 1 {
		return c, failure(InvalidDirection, c.gap, noSpan)
	}
	target := c.gap + direction
	if target < 0 || target > d.TokenCount() {
		return c, failure(InvalidPosition, target, noSpan)
	}
	index := c.gap
	if direction < 0 {
		index--
	}
	token, _ := d.Token(index)
	if token.Kind == Opaque {
		return c, failure(UnsupportedToken, index, token.Span)
	}
	c.gap = target
	return c, nil
}

// Element preserves source/token identity in both views, including the empty
// normal text of Bold codes. Text is derived display, never native serialization.
type Element struct {
	TokenIndex int
	Kind       Kind
	Span       wp51.Span
	Text       string
}
type Presentation struct {
	StartGap, EndGap, CursorGap int
	Reveal                      bool
	Elements                    []Element
}

// Present renders a bounded token window, refusing an opaque token before
// returning any elements. It does not wrap, count terminal cells or infer layout.
func (c Cursor) Present(start, count int) (Presentation, error) {
	d := &Document{state: c.state}
	if !d.valid() {
		return Presentation{}, failure(InvalidDocument, -1, noSpan)
	}
	if start < 0 || start > d.TokenCount() || count < 0 || count > d.TokenCount()-start {
		return Presentation{}, failure(InvalidPosition, start, noSpan)
	}
	if count > MaxWindowTokens {
		return Presentation{}, failure(WindowLimit, start, noSpan)
	}
	// Acquire only the requested contiguous window, not the full body. Indexes
	// already identify valid complete-token spans, so this remains input bounded.
	window := wp51.Span{Offset: int(d.state.gaps[start]), Length: int(d.state.gaps[start+count] - d.state.gaps[start])}
	raw, _ := d.state.image.Bytes(window)
	for i := start; i < start+count; i++ {
		span := d.span(i)
		local := span.Offset - window.Offset
		if identify(raw[local:local+span.Length]) == Opaque {
			return Presentation{}, failure(UnsupportedToken, i, span)
		}
	}
	p := Presentation{StartGap: start, EndGap: start + count, CursorGap: c.gap, Reveal: c.reveal, Elements: make([]Element, count)}
	for i := start; i < start+count; i++ {
		span := d.span(i)
		local := span.Offset - window.Offset
		token := raw[local : local+span.Length]
		kind := identify(token)
		text := ""
		if kind == ASCII {
			text = string(token)
		} else if c.reveal {
			if kind == BoldOn {
				text = "[BOLD]"
			} else {
				text = "[bold]"
			}
		}
		p.Elements[i-start] = Element{TokenIndex: i, Kind: kind, Span: span, Text: text}
	}
	return p, nil
}


=== ACTUAL_APPROVED_H71_SEMANTIC_SOURCE cd983c05401a90c1f0c912c60ab6ca23d8bd437b:internal/document/new.go ===
package document

import (
	"encoding/binary"
	"github.com/lynncoleart/wordling/internal/wp51"
)

// SerializationProfile selects independently authored closed native metadata.
// It does not supply a font/printer resource or a Linux layout implementation.
type SerializationProfile uint8

const HistoricalUS51DosTextStandardPrinter SerializationProfile = 1

// NewConfig deliberately accepts only a closed profile selection. It has no raw
// bytes, field map, cache/default override, resource handle, clock or IO provider.
type NewConfig struct{ Profile SerializationProfile }

const (
	UnsupportedNewProfile Category = "UnsupportedNewProfile"
	NoLoadedOriginal      Category = "NoLoadedOriginal"
	InvalidNewEncoding    Category = "InvalidNewEncoding"
	// Construction policy bound, not an original file-format limit.
	MaxNewPrefixBytes = 1024
)

type birthKind uint8

const (
	loadedBirth birthKind = iota + 1
	newBirth
)

type birthLineage struct {
	kind          birthKind
	originalImage *wp51.Image
}

// Birth is a comparable immutable identity separate from current Revision.
// Equal native bytes do not imply equal birth. Zero identifies no document.
// The captured private lineage cannot be retargeted by replacing a Document.
type Birth struct{ lineage *birthLineage }

func (b Birth) IsNew() bool    { return b.lineage != nil && b.lineage.kind == newBirth }
func (b Birth) IsLoaded() bool { return b.lineage != nil && b.lineage.kind == loadedBirth }
func (d *Document) Birth() Birth {
	if !d.valid() {
		return Birth{}
	}
	return Birth{d.state.birth}
}

// New encodes one explicitly selected historical serialization profile, then
// delegates native validation/indexing and mutation qualification to the same
// private paths used by Loaded revisions. No original fixture/resource is read.
func New(config NewConfig) (*Document, error) {
	if config.Profile != HistoricalUS51DosTextStandardPrinter {
		return nil, failure(UnsupportedNewProfile, -1, noSpan)
	}
	raw, err := encodeHistoricalProfile()
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	d, err := parseRevision(raw, &birthLineage{kind: newBirth})
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if err = d.CheckMutation(); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	return d, nil
}

// Fields below are individually authored from the reviewed schema.json
// (fa2047...), SDK WP3–18, Appendix A8/A9 (PDF507/508) and WP43 selected-profile
// observations. They are not extracted from a native/SET/PRS blob. Encoders keep
// the documented representation; these caches are not general hash algorithms.
type wpu uint16
type psu uint16
type inch3600 uint16

type historicalDosTextInitialization struct {
	screenWidth                    wpu
	printerCounter                 uint8
	displayPitchRaw, resolutionRaw uint16
}

type nativeDescriptor struct {
	cell                                       inch3600
	width                                      wpu
	cap, x, descender                          psu
	italic                                     int16
	typefaceTraits                             uint32 // 24-bit bitset; high reserved bit zero
	flags50, typefaceNameCache, displayQuality uint8
	fontNameCache                              uint16
	characterSets                              [2]uint16
}

// A bounded little-endian field encoder. Construction checks extents before
// allocating the candidate; no public input can select extra rows or payloads.
type newEncoder struct {
	bytes []byte
	err   bool
}

func (w *newEncoder) add(b ...byte) {
	if w.err || len(b) > MaxNewPrefixBytes-len(w.bytes) {
		w.err = true
		return
	}
	w.bytes = append(w.bytes, b...)
}
func (w *newEncoder) u8(v uint8)   { w.add(v) }
func (w *newEncoder) u16(v uint16) { w.add(byte(v), byte(v>>8)) }
func (w *newEncoder) u32(v uint32) { w.add(byte(v), byte(v>>8), byte(v>>16), byte(v>>24)) }
func (w *newEncoder) u24(v uint32) {
	if v >= 1<<23 {
		w.err = true
		return
	}
	w.add(byte(v), byte(v>>8), byte(v>>16))
}
func (w *newEncoder) nul(s string, width int) {
	if len(s) >= width || width > MaxNewPrefixBytes-len(w.bytes) {
		w.err = true
		return
	}
	for _, c := range []byte(s) {
		if c < 0x20 || c > 0x7e {
			w.err = true
			return
		}
		w.u8(c)
	}
	for i := len(s); i < width; i++ {
		w.u8(0)
	}
}
func encodeDescriptor(w *newEncoder, d nativeDescriptor) {
	w.u16(uint16(d.cell))
	w.u16(uint16(d.width))
	w.u16(uint16(d.cap))
	w.u16(uint16(d.x))
	w.u16(uint16(d.descender))
	w.u16(uint16(d.italic))
	w.u24(d.typefaceTraits)
	w.u8(d.flags50)
	w.u8(d.typefaceNameCache)
	w.u8(d.displayQuality)
	w.u16(d.fontNameCache)
	w.u16(d.characterSets[0])
	w.u16(d.characterSets[1])
}
func cachedDosStamp(year, month, day, hour, minute, second int) (uint16, uint16, bool) {
	if year < 1980 || year > 2107 || month < 1 || month > 12 || day < 1 || day > 31 || hour < 0 || hour > 23 || minute < 0 || minute > 59 || second < 0 || second > 58 || second%2 != 0 {
		return 0, 0, false
	}
	days := [...]int{0, 31, 28, 31, 30, 31, 30, 31, 31, 30, 31, 30, 31}
	if year%4 == 0 && (year%100 != 0 || year%400 == 0) {
		days[2] = 29
	}
	if day > days[month] {
		return 0, 0, false
	}
	return uint16((year-1980)<<9 | month<<5 | day), uint16(hour<<11 | minute<<5 | second/2), true
}
func encodeHistoricalProfile() ([]byte, error) {
	// Explicit scoped policy120WPU/01/raw0/raw0. No metric/DPI/percentage or
	// universal default/counter rule follows from these DOS text observations.
	init := historicalDosTextInitialization{screenWidth: 120, printerCounter: 1, displayPitchRaw: 0, resolutionRaw: 0}
	pool := newEncoder{}
	poolLocalName := len(pool.bytes)
	pool.nul("Courier 10cpi", 14)
	font := newEncoder{}
	font.u16(0)      // font instance, SDK SET57h+0
	font.u16(0xffff) // no proportional-spacing cache, +2
	font.u16(137)
	font.u16(63) // shoulder heights PSU, +4/+6
	font.u16(120)
	font.u16(120)
	font.u16(120) // printed/optimal/normal widths WPU
	font.u16(10)
	font.u16(1)                     // motion units / fractional numerator
	font.u16(uint16(poolLocalName)) // bounded pool-local name reference, +18
	font.u8(0)                      // font flags
	font.u16(0x24e7)                // selected descriptor-match cache, not computed
	encodeDescriptor(&font, nativeDescriptor{cell: 500, width: 120, cap: 5630, x: 4150, descender: 1880, italic: 0, typefaceTraits: 0x110400, flags50: 0x40, typefaceNameCache: 0xc9, displayQuality: 0, fontNameCache: 0x3893, characterSets: [2]uint16{1, 1}})
	font.u16(600)
	font.u8(0x40) // 5.1 point size in1/3600inch and traits
	for i := 0; i < 16; i++ {
		if i == 15 {
			font.u8(0xfe)
		} else {
			font.u8(0xff)
		}
	} // attribute AFC, unavailable final attribute
	for i := 0; i < 16; i++ {
		font.u8(0xff)
	} // character-set AFC, no chained rows
	for i := 0; i < 4; i++ {
		font.u8(0xff)
	} // orientation AFC
	printer := newEncoder{}
	printer.nul("Standard Printer", 37)
	printer.nul("STANPRIN.PRS", 13)
	encodeDescriptor(&printer, nativeDescriptor{cell: 475, width: 120, cap: 7700, x: 5900, descender: 2700, italic: 0, typefaceTraits: 0x110400, flags50: 0x40, typefaceNameCache: 0xc9, displayQuality: 0, fontNameCache: 0xcf87, characterSets: [2]uint16{1, 1}})
	printer.u16(0)
	printer.u16(0)
	printer.u16(0)
	printer.u16(0)   // resource minima top/bottom/left/right
	printer.u8(0x18) // qualified selection/init/hzone/orientation flags
	date, clock, ok := cachedDosStamp(1992, 3, 9, 12, 4, 20)
	if !ok {
		return nil, failure(InvalidNewEncoding, -1, noSpan)
	}
	printer.u16(date)
	printer.u16(clock) // cached resource stamp, never host/FAT time
	printer.u16(600)
	printer.u8(0x40) // point-size/typeface tail in native12 (different SET54h order)
	state := newEncoder{}
	state.u8(0)
	state.u8(0x23)
	state.u16(0x7c) // document flags, text/graphics quality, redline
	state.u16(uint16(init.screenWidth))
	state.u16(0) // scoped screen width/binding WPU
	state.u8(init.printerCounter)
	state.u8(0) // scoped counter/reserved
	state.u16(init.displayPitchRaw)
	state.u16(init.resolutionRaw)
	state.u16(0) // raw encodings/reserved
	graphics := newEncoder{}
	graphics.u16(0) // no graphics payload
	packets := []struct {
		kind uint16
		data *newEncoder
	}{{7, &pool}, {15, &font}, {12, &printer}, {6, &state}, {8, &graphics}}
	const headerBytes = 16
	const indexBytes = 10
	const entriesPerBlock = 5
	blockBytes := entriesPerBlock * indexBytes
	lastBlock := headerBytes + blockBytes
	for _, p := range packets[:4] {
		if p.data.err {
			return nil, failure(InvalidNewEncoding, -1, noSpan)
		}
		lastBlock += len(p.data.bytes)
	}
	bodyStart := lastBlock + blockBytes + len(graphics.bytes)
	if graphics.err || bodyStart > MaxNewPrefixBytes || bodyStart > MaxMutationBytes {
		return nil, failure(InvalidNewEncoding, -1, noSpan)
	}
	w := newEncoder{bytes: make([]byte, 0, bodyStart)}
	w.u8(0xff)
	w.u8('W')
	w.u8('P')
	w.u8('C')
	w.u32(uint32(bodyStart))
	w.u8(1)
	w.u8(0x0a)
	w.u8(0)
	w.u8(1)
	w.u16(0)
	w.u16(0) // identity/version/encryption/reserved
	w.u16(0xfffb)
	w.u16(entriesPerBlock)
	w.u16(uint16(blockBytes))
	w.u32(uint32(lastBlock))
	offset := headerBytes + blockBytes
	for _, p := range packets[:4] {
		w.u16(p.kind)
		w.u32(uint32(len(p.data.bytes)))
		w.u32(uint32(offset))
		offset += len(p.data.bytes)
	}
	for _, p := range packets[:4] {
		w.add(p.data.bytes...)
	}
	w.u16(0xfffb)
	w.u16(entriesPerBlock)
	w.u16(uint16(blockBytes))
	w.u32(0)
	w.u16(8)
	w.u32(uint32(len(graphics.bytes)))
	w.u32(uint32(lastBlock + blockBytes))
	for i := 2 * indexBytes; i < blockBytes; i++ {
		w.u8(0)
	} // wholly zero unused slots
	w.add(graphics.bytes...)
	if w.err || len(w.bytes) != bodyStart || binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(w.bytes[4:8]) != uint32(len(w.bytes)) {
		return nil, failure(InvalidNewEncoding, -1, noSpan)
	}
	return w.bytes, nil
}


=== ACTUAL_APPROVED_H71_SEMANTIC_SOURCE cd983c05401a90c1f0c912c60ab6ca23d8bd437b:internal/document/deletion.go ===
package document

// Original command identities; these are semantic commands, not terminal keys.
type DeleteDirection uint8

const (
	ForwardDelete DeleteDirection = iota + 1
	Backspace
)

type DeleteAnswer uint8

const (
	Yes DeleteAnswer = iota + 1
	No
	F1
	Escape
)

type DeletionKind uint8

const (
	NoDeletion DeletionKind = iota
	CharacterDeletion
	PairDeletion
	OpeningRepair
	ClosingRepair
	AttributedDeletion
	Restoration
)
const (
	UnsupportedDeletion        Category = "UnsupportedDeletion"
	UnsupportedHistoryEffect   Category = "UnsupportedHistoryEffect"
	InvalidHistory             Category = "InvalidHistory"
	HistoryMismatch            Category = "HistoryMismatch"
	InvalidDeletionState       Category = "InvalidDeletionState"
	InvalidAnswer              Category = "InvalidAnswer"
	InvalidEditResult          Category = "InvalidEditResult"
	InvalidPreview             Category = "InvalidPreview"
	HistoryEmpty               Category = "HistoryEmpty"
	UnsupportedHistoryBoundary Category = "UnsupportedHistoryBoundary"
)

// Deletion is an immutable command result/question. Accessors return copied
// handles or values; no accepted object retains an exported mutable wrapper.
type Deletion struct{ state *deletionState }
type deletionState struct {
	before, after                                       *revisionState
	beforeHistory, afterHistory                         *historyState
	cursor                                              Cursor
	kind                                                DeletionKind
	direction                                           DeleteDirection
	target, on, off                                     int
	blockStart, blockEnd                                int
	pending, block, applied, bodyChanged, nativeChanged bool
}

func (r Deletion) Document() *Document {
	if r.state == nil {
		return nil
	}
	return &Document{state: r.state.after}
}
func (r Deletion) Cursor() Cursor {
	if r.state == nil {
		return Cursor{}
	}
	return r.state.cursor
}
func (r Deletion) History() History {
	if r.state == nil {
		return History{}
	}
	return History{state: r.state.afterHistory}
}
func (r Deletion) Pending() bool       { return r.state != nil && r.state.pending }
func (r Deletion) BlockActive() bool   { return r.state != nil && r.state.block }
func (r Deletion) Applied() bool       { return r.state != nil && r.state.applied }
func (r Deletion) BodyChanged() bool   { return r.state != nil && r.state.bodyChanged }
func (r Deletion) NativeChanged() bool { return r.state != nil && r.state.nativeChanged }
func (r Deletion) Kind() DeletionKind {
	if r.state == nil {
		return NoDeletion
	}
	return r.state.kind
}
func (r Deletion) BlockRange() (int, int, bool) {
	if !r.BlockActive() {
		return 0, 0, false
	}
	return r.state.blockStart, r.state.blockEnd, true
}
func (r Deletion) Prompt() string {
	if !r.Pending() {
		return ""
	}
	if r.state.block {
		return "Delete Block? No (Yes)"
	}
	if r.state.target == r.state.on {
		return "Delete [BOLD]? No (Yes)"
	}
	return "Delete [bold]? No (Yes)"
}
func unchangedDeletion(d *Document, c Cursor, h History) Deletion {
	var state *revisionState
	if d != nil {
		state = d.state
	}
	return Deletion{&deletionState{before: state, after: state, beforeHistory: h.state, afterHistory: h.state, cursor: c}}
}
func (d *Document) checkDeletion(c Cursor, h History) error {
	if !d.valid() {
		return failure(InvalidDocument, -1, noSpan)
	}
	if c.state != d.state {
		return failure(RevisionMismatch, c.gap, noSpan)
	}
	if e := h.check(d); e != nil {
		return e
	}
	return d.CheckMutation()
}
func (d *Document) pairAt(index int) (int, int) {
	on := -1
	raw := d.state.image.Export()
	for i := 0; i < d.TokenCount(); i++ {
		s := d.span(i)
		switch identify(raw[s.Offset : s.Offset+s.Length]) {
		case BoldOn:
			on = i
		case BoldOff:
			if on == index || i == index {
				return on, i
			}
			on = -1
		}
	}
	return -1, -1
}

// Delete interprets only a complete admitted ASCII/Bold token. Normal Bold
// deletion returns its exact code question; Reveal bypasses that question.
// Unsupported history effects remain atomic prerequisites, never guessed entries.
func (d *Document) Delete(c Cursor, direction DeleteDirection, h History) (Deletion, error) {
	unchanged := unchangedDeletion(d, c, h)
	if e := d.checkDeletion(c, h); e != nil {
		return unchanged, e
	}
	if direction != ForwardDelete && direction != Backspace {
		return unchanged, failure(InvalidDirection, c.gap, noSpan)
	}
	target := c.gap
	if direction == Backspace {
		target--
	}
	if target < 0 || target >= d.TokenCount() {
		return unchanged, failure(InvalidPosition, target, noSpan)
	}
	token, _ := d.Token(target)
	s := *unchanged.state
	s.direction = direction
	s.target = target
	if token.Kind == ASCII {
		s.kind = CharacterDeletion
		return d.completeDeletion(s, h)
	}
	if token.Kind != BoldOn && token.Kind != BoldOff {
		return unchanged, failure(UnsupportedDeletion, target, token.Span)
	}
	s.kind = PairDeletion
	s.on, s.off = d.pairAt(target)
	if s.on < 0 {
		return unchanged, failure(UnsupportedBody, target, token.Span)
	}
	if !c.reveal {
		s.pending = true
		return Deletion{&s}, nil
	}
	return d.completeDeletion(s, h)
}

// DeleteBlock qualifies forward selections of exactly one opening boundary, one
// closing boundary, or a complete attributed span in Reveal Codes. Empty,
// reversed, normal-view and other partial selections are explicitly unqualified.
func (d *Document) DeleteBlock(anchor, end Cursor, h History) (Deletion, error) {
	unchanged := unchangedDeletion(d, end, h)
	if e := d.checkDeletion(end, h); e != nil {
		return unchanged, e
	}
	if anchor.state != d.state {
		return unchanged, failure(RevisionMismatch, anchor.gap, noSpan)
	}
	if !anchor.reveal || !end.reveal || anchor.gap >= end.gap {
		return unchanged, failure(UnsupportedDeletion, anchor.gap, noSpan)
	}
	s := *unchanged.state
	s.blockStart = anchor.gap
	s.blockEnd = end.gap
	s.block = true
	s.pending = true
	s.on, s.off = d.pairAt(anchor.gap)
	if s.on < 0 {
		return unchanged, failure(UnsupportedDeletion, anchor.gap, noSpan)
	}
	switch {
	case anchor.gap == s.on && end.gap == s.on+1:
		s.kind = OpeningRepair
	case anchor.gap == s.off && end.gap == s.off+1:
		s.kind = ClosingRepair
	case anchor.gap == s.on && end.gap == s.off+1:
		s.kind = AttributedDeletion
	default:
		return unchanged, failure(UnsupportedDeletion, anchor.gap, noSpan)
	}
	return Deletion{&s}, nil
}
func (d *Document) AnswerDeletion(h History, pending Deletion, answer DeleteAnswer) (Deletion, error) {
	unchanged := unchangedDeletion(d, pending.Cursor(), h)
	if pending.state == nil || !pending.Pending() {
		return unchanged, failure(InvalidDeletionState, -1, noSpan)
	}
	if e := d.checkDeletion(pending.Cursor(), h); e != nil {
		return unchanged, e
	}
	if pending.state.beforeHistory != h.state || pending.state.before != d.state {
		return unchanged, failure(HistoryMismatch, -1, noSpan)
	}
	if answer < Yes || answer > Escape {
		return pending, failure(InvalidAnswer, -1, noSpan)
	}
	s := *pending.state
	if answer == Yes {
		return d.completeDeletion(s, h)
	}
	if h.state.count > 0 {
		return pending, failure(UnsupportedHistoryEffect, -1, noSpan)
	}
	s.pending = false
	if !s.block {
		if s.direction == ForwardDelete {
			s.cursor.gap++
		} else {
			s.cursor.gap--
		}
	}
	next := *h.state
	next.groupOpen = false
	s.afterHistory = &next
	return Deletion{&s}, nil
}

// CancelBlock is the separately observed F1 cleanup after a declined block
// question. It is not a global Escape/No/cancel-exit rule or an Undelete action.
func (d *Document) CancelBlock(h History, r Deletion) (Deletion, error) {
	unchanged := unchangedDeletion(d, r.Cursor(), h)
	if e := d.checkDeletion(r.Cursor(), h); e != nil {
		return unchanged, e
	}
	if r.state == nil || r.Pending() || !r.BlockActive() || r.state.after != d.state || r.state.afterHistory != h.state {
		return unchanged, failure(InvalidDeletionState, -1, noSpan)
	}
	s := *r.state
	s.block = false
	s.kind = NoDeletion
	s.before = s.after
	s.beforeHistory = h.state
	next := *h.state
	next.groupOpen = false
	s.afterHistory = &next
	return Deletion{&s}, nil
}
func (d *Document) completeDeletion(s deletionState, h History) (Deletion, error) {
	unchanged := Deletion{&s}
	if h.state.groupUnknown {
		return unchanged, failure(UnsupportedHistoryBoundary, -1, noSpan)
	}
	// Approved WP40 qualifies only its structural command context and bounded
	// observations; no general record/skip/clear/count rule is inferred.
	if (s.kind == PairDeletion || s.kind == ClosingRepair) && !h.wp40Target(s) {
		return unchanged, failure(UnsupportedHistoryEffect, s.target, noSpan)
	}
	if h.state.count > 0 && (s.kind == OpeningRepair || s.kind == AttributedDeletion) {
		return unchanged, failure(UnsupportedHistoryEffect, s.target, noSpan)
	}
	if h.state.observed != nil || (s.kind == CharacterDeletion && s.direction == Backspace) {
		return unchanged, failure(UnsupportedHistoryEffect, s.target, noSpan)
	}
	raw := d.state.image.Export()
	bodyStart := int(d.state.gaps[0])
	body := make([]byte, 0, len(raw)-bodyStart)
	gap := s.cursor.gap
	tokens := d.TokenCount()
	var remembered fragment
	switch s.kind {
	case CharacterDeletion:
		span := d.span(s.target)
		body = append(body, raw[bodyStart:span.Offset]...)
		body = append(body, raw[span.Offset+span.Length:]...)
		remembered = d.fragment(s.target, s.target+1)
		tokens--
		gap = s.target
	case PairDeletion:
		on, off := d.span(s.on), d.span(s.off)
		body = append(body, raw[bodyStart:on.Offset]...)
		body = append(body, raw[on.Offset+on.Length:off.Offset]...)
		body = append(body, raw[off.Offset+off.Length:]...)
		tokens -= 2
		gap = s.target
		if s.target == s.off {
			gap--
		}
	case ClosingRepair:
		body = append(body, raw[bodyStart:]...)
		remembered = joinFragments(d.fragment(s.on, s.on+1), d.fragment(s.off, s.off+1))
		gap = s.blockEnd
	case OpeningRepair:
		body = append(body, raw[bodyStart:]...)
		remembered = joinFragments(d.fragment(s.off, s.off+1), d.fragment(s.on, s.on+1))
		gap = s.blockEnd
	case AttributedDeletion:
		start, end := int(d.state.gaps[s.blockStart]), int(d.state.gaps[s.blockEnd])
		body = append(body, raw[bodyStart:start]...)
		body = append(body, raw[end:]...)
		remembered = d.fragment(s.blockStart, s.blockEnd)
		tokens -= s.blockEnd - s.blockStart
		gap = s.blockStart
	default:
		return unchanged, failure(UnsupportedDeletion, -1, noSpan)
	}
	edit, e := d.publishBody(s.cursor, body, tokens, gap, (s.kind == OpeningRepair || s.kind == ClosingRepair))
	if e != nil {
		return unchanged, e
	}
	var next History
	if s.kind == PairDeletion || s.kind == ClosingRepair {
		next = h.observeTarget(s, remembered, edit.Document.state)
	} else {
		next, e = h.record(s, remembered, edit.Document.state)
	}
	if e != nil {
		return unchanged, e
	}
	s.after = edit.Document.state
	s.cursor = edit.Cursor
	s.afterHistory = next.state
	s.pending = false
	s.block = false
	s.applied = true
	s.bodyChanged = edit.Changed
	s.nativeChanged = s.before != s.after
	return Deletion{&s}, nil
}
