package editor

import (
	"errors"
	"github.com/lynncoleart/wordling/internal/document"
	"github.com/lynncoleart/wordling/internal/storage"
)

func copyDirectory(p *storage.Directory) *storage.Directory {
	if p == nil {
		return nil
	}
	v := *p
	return &v
}
func copySource(p *storage.Document) *storage.Document {
	if p == nil {
		return nil
	}
	v := *p
	return &v
}
func copyDestination(p *storage.Destination) *storage.Destination {
	if p == nil {
		return nil
	}
	v := *p
	return &v
}
func copyNative(p *document.Document) *document.Document {
	if p == nil {
		return nil
	}
	v := *p
	return &v
}
func copyIntent(p *Intent) *Intent {
	if p == nil {
		return nil
	}
	v := *p
	v.Directory = copyDirectory(p.Directory)
	v.Document = copySource(p.Document)
	v.Destination = copyDestination(p.Destination)
	v.Candidate = append([]byte(nil), p.Candidate...)
	return &v
}
func copyResult(r Result) Result {
	r.Document = copySource(r.Document)
	r.Macro = copyMacro(r.Macro)
	r.Destination = copyDestination(r.Destination)
	// Host-owned arbitrary diagnostic causes cannot be universally deep-cloned.
	switch e := r.Err.(type) {
	case *storage.Failure:
		if e != nil {
			v := *e
			r.Err = &v
		}
	case *document.Failure:
		if e != nil {
			v := *e
			r.Err = &v
		}
	case *Refusal:
		if e != nil {
			v := *e
			r.Err = &v
		}
	}
	return r
}
func (s Session) dirty() bool {
	if s.newPhase == NewUnpublished {
		return s.native.Revision() != s.genesis
	}
	if s.newPhase == AwaitPublish || s.newPhase == AwaitFirstRead || s.newPhase == PublishedUnbound || s.newPhase == PublicationUncertain {
		return true
	}
	return s.native != nil && s.native.Revision() != s.saved
}
func editingCommand(c Command) bool { return c == Text || c == Insert || c == Right || c == Left }

// History and Preview are copied handles over the dependency's private immutable
// state. These handles preserve pre-interleaving evidence; current command
// permission is reported by Snapshot, not by an earlier handle. Unknown
// CountKnown never becomes empty, including after clear/retrieve.
func (s Session) History() document.History { return s.history }
func (s Session) Preview(start, count int) (document.Overlay, error) {
	return s.preview.Present(start, count, s.cursor.Reveal())
}

func (s Session) applyDeletion(r document.Deletion, c Command) Session {
	old := s.native.Revision()
	s.native = r.Document()
	s.cursor = r.Cursor()
	s.history = r.History()
	s.deletion = r
	s.selection = r.BlockActive()
	if s.native.Revision() != old {
		s.revision++
		s.lastSave = NotSaved
	}
	s.provenance = c
	if r.Pending() {
		s.mode = DeleteQuestion
	} else {
		s.mode = Editing
	}
	return s
}
func (s Session) edit(e Event) (Session, *Intent, error) {
	if s.native == nil {
		return s, nil, refuse("command requires a loaded document")
	}
	if s.mode == DeleteQuestion {
		var a document.DeleteAnswer
		switch e.Command {
		case Yes:
			a = document.Yes
		case No:
			a = document.No
		case F1:
			a = document.F1
		case Escape:
			a = document.Escape
		default:
			return s, nil, refuse("answer required for exact deletion question")
		}
		r, err := s.native.AnswerDeletion(s.history, s.deletion, a)
		if err != nil {
			return s, nil, err
		}
		return s.applyDeletion(r, e.Command), nil, nil
	}
	if s.mode == HistoryPreview {
		switch e.Command {
		case Previous:
			p, err := s.preview.Previous()
			if err != nil {
				return s, nil, err
			}
			s.preview = p
		case F1:
			h, err := s.history.CancelPreview(s.preview)
			if err != nil {
				return s, nil, err
			}
			s.history = h
			s.preview = document.Preview{}
			s.mode = Editing
		case Restore:
			r, err := s.native.Restore(s.history, s.preview)
			if err != nil {
				return s, nil, err
			}
			s = s.applyDeletion(r, e.Command)
			s.preview = document.Preview{}
		default:
			return s, nil, refuse("only original Undelete 1/2/F1 are qualified")
		}
		return s, nil, nil
	}
	if s.selection && e.Command == F1 && s.deletion.BlockActive() {
		r, err := s.native.CancelBlock(s.history, s.deletion)
		if err != nil {
			return s, nil, err
		}
		return s.applyDeletion(r, e.Command), nil, nil
	}
	switch e.Command {
	case Text:
		if s.selection {
			return s, nil, refuse("typing in block is unqualified")
		}
		var r document.EditResult
		var err error
		if s.over {
			r, err = s.native.TypeoverASCII(s.cursor, []byte(e.Text))
		} else {
			r, err = s.native.InsertASCII(s.cursor, []byte(e.Text))
		}
		if err != nil {
			return s, nil, err
		}
		h := s.history
		if s.historyValid {
			h, err = h.AfterEdit(r)
			if err != nil {
				return s, nil, err
			}
		}
		old := s.native.Revision()
		s.native = copyNative(r.Document)
		s.cursor = r.Cursor
		s.history = h
		if s.native.Revision() != old {
			s.revision++
			s.lastSave = NotSaved
		}
	case Insert:
		if s.selection {
			return s, nil, refuse("mode toggle during selection unqualified")
		}
		s.over = !s.over
		if s.historyValid && s.history.Qualified() {
			h, err := s.history.InterruptGrouping()
			if err != nil {
				return s, nil, err
			}
			s.history = h
		}
	case Block:
		if !s.historyValid || s.selection || !s.cursor.Reveal() {
			return s, nil, refuse("only forward Reveal block qualification")
		}
		s.selection = true
		s.anchor = s.cursor
		s.deletion = document.Deletion{}
	case Delete, Backspace:
		if !s.historyValid {
			return s, nil, refuse("history is not qualified for this document lifecycle")
		}
		var r document.Deletion
		var err error
		if s.selection {
			if e.Command != Backspace {
				return s, nil, refuse("only observed selected-block Backspace is qualified")
			}
			r, err = s.native.DeleteBlock(s.anchor, s.cursor, s.history)
		} else {
			d := document.ForwardDelete
			if e.Command == Backspace {
				d = document.Backspace
			}
			r, err = s.native.Delete(s.cursor, d, s.history)
		}
		if err != nil {
			return s, nil, err
		}
		return s.applyDeletion(r, e.Command), nil, nil
	case F1:
		if s.native.Birth().IsNew() && s.native.Revision() == s.genesis && s.history.Count() == 0 {
			return s, nil, refuse("startup empty Undelete is not qualified")
		}
		if s.selection {
			return s, nil, refuse("block cancellation requires declined block question")
		}
		if !s.historyValid {
			return s, nil, refuse("history is unknown, not empty")
		}
		p, err := s.history.Open(s.cursor)
		if err != nil {
			return s, nil, err
		}
		if p.Active() {
			s.preview = p
			s.mode = HistoryPreview
		}
	default:
		return s, nil, refuse("unsupported editing command")
	}
	s.provenance = e.Command
	return s, nil, nil
}

// Filename/exit/prefix interleavings do not silently join deletion groups.
// Already unqualified history is retained; native safety is independent.
func (s Session) interrupted() (document.History, error) {
	if !s.historyValid || !s.history.Qualified() {
		return s.history, nil
	}
	return s.history.InterruptGrouping()
}

// saveHistoryInterleaving separates known filename/publication behavior from
// optional hidden-history knowledge. Only Save receives this bounded waiver;
// other interleavings continue to use the dependency's exact guards.
func (s Session) saveHistoryInterleaving() (Session, error) {
	if !s.historyValid {
		return s, nil
	}
	h, err := s.history.InterruptGrouping()
	if err == nil {
		s.history = h
		return s, nil
	}
	var failure *document.Failure
	if !s.history.CountKnown() && errors.As(err, &failure) && failure.Category == document.UnsupportedHistoryEffect {
		// Bounded paired-code observations admit immediate history only; an
		// already-unqualified real history stays unavailable as well.
		// F10 is independently known, but its post-interleaving history is not.
		s.historyValid = false
		return s, nil
	}
	return s, err
}


=== ACTUAL_APPROVED_H71_SEMANTIC_SOURCE cd983c05401a90c1f0c912c60ab6ca23d8bd437b:internal/editor/new_binding.go ===
package editor

import (
	"bytes"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"fmt"

	"github.com/lynncoleart/wordling/internal/document"
	"github.com/lynncoleart/wordling/internal/storage"
)

// NewPhase describes Linux host safety, not additional DOS prompts.
type NewPhase string

const (
	NewUnpublished       NewPhase = "NewUnpublished"
	AwaitPublish         NewPhase = "AwaitPublish"
	AwaitFirstRead       NewPhase = "AwaitFirstRead"
	PublishedUnbound     NewPhase = "PublishedUnbound"
	PublicationUncertain NewPhase = "PublicationUncertain"
	NewAnchored          NewPhase = "NewAnchored"
)
const (
	PublishNewBuffer IntentKind = "PublishNewBuffer"
	BindNewSource    IntentKind = "BindNewSource"
)

// RequestKey is an opaque issued-request identity for once-per-request host
// scheduling. It confers neither IO authority nor transaction idempotence.
type RequestKey struct{ proof *firstRequest }

func (k RequestKey) Valid() bool { return k.proof != nil }
func (i Intent) RequestKey() RequestKey {
	if !i.firstProof.matches(&i) {
		return RequestKey{}
	}
	return RequestKey{i.firstProof}
}

type firstRequest struct {
	transition          *transitionIdentity
	buffer              *bufferIdentity
	birth               document.Birth
	native              document.Revision
	operation, revision uint64
	screen              uint8
	kind                IntentKind
	directory           storage.Directory
	target              storage.Destination
	name, display       string
	candidate           []byte
	publication         *firstPublication
}
type firstPublication struct {
	request   *firstRequest
	result    Result
	possible  bool
	candidate string
}
type firstObservation struct {
	request   *firstRequest
	outcome   storage.Outcome
	source    *storage.Document
	err       *sealedError
	possible  bool
	candidate string
}

// sealedError owns diagnostic values. Unwrap returns fresh typed failures so
// errors.As on a public diagnostic cannot mutate the private observation.
type sealedError struct {
	message  string
	failures []storage.Failure
}

func (e *sealedError) Error() string { return e.message }
func (e *sealedError) Unwrap() []error {
	out := make([]error, len(e.failures))
	for i, f := range e.failures {
		v := f
		out[i] = &v
	}
	return out
}
func freezeFirstError(err error, invoked bool) (*sealedError, bool, string) {
	if err == nil {
		return nil, false, ""
	}
	out := &sealedError{}
	possible, candidate := false, ""
	budget := 64
	var walk func(error, bool)
	walk = func(e error, covered bool) {
		if e == nil {
			return
		}
		budget--
		if budget < 0 {
			possible = true
			return
		}
		if f, ok := e.(*storage.Failure); ok && f != nil {
			v := *f
			v.Cause = nil
			out.failures = append(out.failures, v)
			out.message += fmt.Sprintf("storage: %s during %s (%q); ", v.Kind, v.Stage, v.Name)
			if candidate == "" {
				candidate = v.Candidate
			}
			switch v.Kind {
			case storage.InvalidRequest, storage.UnsafePath, storage.ReadFailed, storage.InputLimit, storage.NativeRefused, storage.StaleSource, storage.DestinationChanged, storage.OverwriteRequired, storage.UnsupportedReplacement, storage.CandidateFailed, storage.PublicationFailed, storage.CleanupFailed:
			default:
				possible = true
			}
			possible = possible || v.DestinationMayExist
			walk(f.Cause, true)
			return
		}
		if many, ok := e.(interface{ Unwrap() []error }); ok {
			for _, child := range many.Unwrap() {
				walk(child, covered)
			}
			return
		}
		if one, ok := e.(interface{ Unwrap() error }); ok {
			walk(one.Unwrap(), covered)
			return
		}
		if !covered {
			possible = true
		}
		out.message += e.Error() + "; "
	}
	walk(err, false)
	if !invoked {
		possible = false
	}
	return out, possible, candidate
}
func (p *firstRequest) matches(i *Intent) bool {
	if p == nil || i == nil || i.firstProof != p || i.Directory == nil {
		return false
	}
	if i.Kind != p.kind || i.Operation != p.operation || i.Revision != p.revision || i.NativeRevision != p.native || i.Screen != p.screen || *i.Directory != p.directory || i.Name != p.name || i.Document != nil || i.Decision != storage.NoOverwrite || i.MacroRun != 0 || i.MacroRequest != 0 || i.MacroParent != ([32]byte{}) || i.Repeat != 0 || i.macroProof != nil {
		return false
	}
	if !bytes.Equal(i.Candidate, p.candidate) {
		return false
	}
	if p.kind == PublishNewBuffer {
		return i.Destination != nil && *i.Destination == p.target
	}
	return i.Destination == nil
}
func (s Session) firstIntent(kind IntentKind, directory *storage.Directory, name string, pub *firstPublication) (Session, *Intent, error) {
	if s.native == nil || !s.native.Birth().IsNew() {
		return s, nil, refuse("first publication requires actual New birth")
	}
	raw, err := s.native.Export()
	if err != nil {
		return s, nil, err
	}
	if s.sequence == ^uint64(0) || s.revision == 0 {
		return s, nil, refuse("first request counter exhausted or invalid")
	}
	s.sequence++
	p := &firstRequest{transition: &transitionIdentity{1}, buffer: s.bufferID, birth: s.native.Birth(), native: s.native.Revision(), operation: s.sequence, revision: s.revision, screen: s.screen, kind: kind, directory: *directory, name: name, display: s.draft, candidate: append([]byte(nil), raw...), publication: pub}
	var target *storage.Destination
	if kind == PublishNewBuffer {
		if s.target == nil || s.target.Exists() {
			return s, nil, refuse("requires observed absent target")
		}
		p.target = *s.target
		target = copyDestination(s.target)
		s.newPhase = AwaitPublish
	} else {
		s.newPhase = AwaitFirstRead
		p.display = pub.request.display
	}
	i := &Intent{firstProof: p, Operation: p.operation, Revision: p.revision, Kind: kind, Directory: copyDirectory(directory), Name: name, Destination: target, Candidate: append([]byte(nil), raw...), NativeRevision: p.native, Screen: p.screen}
	s.transition = p.transition
	s.pending = copyIntent(i)
	s.resume = Editing
	s.mode = Waiting
	return s, copyIntent(i), nil
}
func firstResult(i *Intent, kind IntentKind) (Result, *firstRequest) {
	r := Result{Kind: kind}
	if i == nil {
		r.Err = refuse("nil first request")
		return r, nil
	}
	r.Operation, r.Revision = i.Operation, i.Revision
	p := i.firstProof
	if !p.matches(i) || p.kind != kind {
		r.Err = refuse("first request wrapper differs from accepted authority")
		return r, nil
	}
	return r, p
}

// ExecuteNewPublication performs actual41 IO outside Reduce, on the captured
// opened capability. Public wrapper values can refuse but never redirect IO.
func ExecuteNewPublication(i *Intent) Result {
	r, p := firstResult(i, PublishNewBuffer)
	if p == nil {
		return r
	}
	id := storage.PublicationID{Operation: p.operation, Revision: p.revision}
	prepared, err := p.directory.PrepareNewPublication(&p.target, p.candidate, id)
	invoked := false
	if err == nil {
		invoked = true
		r.Outcome, err = p.directory.PublishNew(prepared, id)
	}
	frozen, possible, candidate := freezeFirstError(err, invoked)
	if r.Outcome.State == storage.Published && err != nil {
		possible = true
	}
	o := &firstObservation{request: p, outcome: r.Outcome, err: frozen, possible: possible, candidate: candidate}
	r.firstObservation = o
	if frozen != nil {
		r.Err = frozen
	}
	return r
}

// ExecuteFirstRead observes the same retained capability/name. It cannot
// publish, re-Observe, choose another root or replace the live native object.
func ExecuteFirstRead(i *Intent) Result {
	r, p := firstResult(i, BindNewSource)
	if p == nil {
		return r
	}
	source, err := p.directory.Read(p.name)
	frozen, _, candidate := freezeFirstError(err, false)
	o := &firstObservation{request: p, source: copySource(source), err: frozen, candidate: candidate}
	r.firstObservation = o
	r.Document = copySource(source)
	if frozen != nil {
		r.Err = frozen
	}
	return r
}
func firstReceipt(r Result, applied, possible bool, candidate string) Receipt {
	return Receipt{Result: copyResult(r), Applied: applied, DestinationMayExist: possible, Candidate: candidate}
}
func cloneFirstReceipt(r Receipt) Receipt { r.Result = copyResult(r.Result); return r }
func (s Session) completeFirst(r Result) (Session, *Intent, error) {
	i := s.pending
	p := i.firstProof
	o := r.firstObservation
	invalid := func() (Session, *Intent, error) {
		return s, nil, refuse("first completion lacks exact current private observation")
	}
	if p == nil || !p.matches(i) || p.transition != s.transition || p.buffer != s.bufferID || p.birth != s.native.Birth() || p.native != s.native.Revision() || p.revision != s.revision || p.screen != s.screen || o == nil || o.request != p || r.Operation != p.operation || r.Revision != p.revision || r.Kind != p.kind || r.Macro != nil || r.Destination != nil || r.macroProof != nil || r.macroObservation != nil || r.Outcome != o.outcome {
		return invalid()
	}
	if o.err == nil {
		if r.Err != nil {
			return invalid()
		}
	} else {
		e, ok := r.Err.(*sealedError)
		if !ok || e != o.err {
			return invalid()
		}
	}
	if (r.Document == nil) != (o.source == nil) {
		return invalid()
	}
	if r.Document != nil && *r.Document != *o.source {
		return invalid()
	}
	s.pending = nil
	s.mode = Editing
	s = s.advanced()
	s.last = firstReceipt(r, true, o.possible, o.candidate)
	if p.kind == PublishNewBuffer {
		if o.err != nil {
			s.notice = o.err.Error()
			s.lastSave = SaveFailed
			s.newPhase = NewUnpublished
			if o.possible {
				s.newPhase = PublicationUncertain
				s.lastSave = SaveUncertain
				s.first = &firstPublication{p, copyResult(r), true, o.candidate}
			}
			return s, nil, nil
		}
		outcome := o.outcome
		if outcome.State != storage.Published || outcome.Name != p.name || outcome.DirectoryID != p.directory.ID() || outcome.Identity.ID.Inode == 0 || outcome.Identity.Size != int64(len(p.candidate)) || outcome.Identity.SHA256 != sha256.Sum256(p.candidate) {
			s.newPhase = PublicationUncertain
			s.lastSave = SaveUncertain
			s.notice = "Invalid publication observation; destination effect unresolved"
			s.first = &firstPublication{p, copyResult(r), true, o.candidate}
			return s, nil, refuse(s.notice)
		}
		s.first = &firstPublication{p, copyResult(r), false, ""}
		s.lastSave = NotSaved
		return s.firstIntent(BindNewSource, &p.directory, p.name, s.first)
	}
	// No Parse: acquisition provides only the permanent first guard.
	pub := p.publication
	if pub == nil || s.first != pub {
		return invalid()
	}
	source := o.source
	if o.err != nil || source == nil || source.Name() != p.name || source.DirectoryID() != p.directory.ID() || source.Identity() != pub.result.Outcome.Identity || !bytes.Equal(source.Export(), p.candidate) {
		s.newPhase = PublishedUnbound
		s.lastSave = SaveUncertain
		s.notice = "Published but first rooted Read did not bind; explicit Read-only retry required"
		return s, nil, nil
	}
	s.source = copySource(source)
	s.newPhase = NewAnchored
	s.saved = p.native
	s.name = pub.request.display
	s.publishedName = s.name
	s.publishedRevision = p.native
	s.published = firstReceipt(pub.result, true, false, "")
	s.lastSave = NewNamePublished
	s.notice = ""
	if s.historyValid {
		h, err := s.history.Separate(document.SavedBoundary)
		if err != nil {
			s.historyValid = false
		} else {
			s.history = h
		}
	}
	return s, nil, nil
}
func (s Session) retryFirstRead() (Session, *Intent, error) {
	if s.newPhase != PublishedUnbound || s.first == nil || s.pending != nil {
		return s, nil, refuse("no published-unbound receipt to retry")
	}
	p := s.first.request
	return s.firstIntent(BindNewSource, &p.directory, p.name, s.first)
}


=== ACTUAL_APPROVED_H71_SEMANTIC_SOURCE cd983c05401a90c1f0c912c60ab6ca23d8bd437b:internal/tui/model.go ===
package tui

import (
	"fmt"
	"strings"

	tea "charm.land/bubbletea/v2"
	"github.com/charmbracelet/x/ansi"
	"github.com/lynncoleart/wordling/internal/document"
	"github.com/lynncoleart/wordling/internal/editor"
	"github.com/lynncoleart/wordling/internal/input"
	"github.com/lynncoleart/wordling/internal/storage"
)

// CancelPendingMsg is an explicit host operation, never an original key alias.
// Disk IO continues; commands are held until the late receipt is reconciled.
type CancelPendingMsg struct{}

// RetryFirstReadMsg is explicit host safety, not an original-key alias.
// It cannot publish or acquire a replacement directory.
type RetryFirstReadMsg struct{}
type operation struct {
	id, revision uint64
	kind         editor.IntentKind
}
type Model struct {
	ascii             asciiQualification
	menu              menuDisplay
	runtime           *Runtime
	session           editor.Session
	width, height     int
	notice, reconcile string
	unsettled         []operation
	macro             *macroFlow
	macroTicket       *macroTicket
	macroCPU          bool
	macroStatus       MacroStatus
	macroFrozen       *macroPane
	repeatEntry       bool
	uiAuthority       *uiTransition
	macroDeferred     *macroIOReceipt
	firstPending      *firstTicket
}
type State struct {
	MenuBar          MenuBar
	RevealInspection RevealInspection

	Session                   editor.Snapshot
	Width, Height             int
	Notice, Reconciliation    string
	PendingIO                 int
	PhysicalDeliveryQualified bool
	Macro                     MacroStatus
	Profile                   string
	NativeProfile             string
}

func (m Model) State() State {
	profile := ""
	if m.runtime != nil {
		profile = m.runtime.profile
	}
	return State{MenuBar: m.menuState(), RevealInspection: m.inspectionState(), NativeProfile: NativeProfile, Profile: profile, Session: m.session.Snapshot(), Width: m.width, Height: m.height, Notice: m.notice, Reconciliation: m.reconcile, PendingIO: len(m.unsettled) + firstPendingCount(m.firstPending), Macro: m.macroStatus}
}

// Session returns an immutable value for measurements/inspection. Never fork it
// to execute independent intents: this Model owns the sole authoritative chain.
func (m Model) Session() editor.Session { return m.session }
func (m Model) Init() tea.Cmd           { return nil }
func (m Model) reduce(event editor.Event) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
	previousMode := m.session.Snapshot().Mode
	next, intent, err := m.session.Reduce(event)
	m.session = next
	if err == nil && next.Snapshot().Mode == editor.MacroName && previousMode != editor.MacroName {
		m.macroStatus = MacroStatus{}
		m.macroFrozen = nil
	}
	m.notice = ""
	if err != nil {
		m.notice = err.Error()
	}
	if intent != nil {
		if firstKind(intent.Kind) {
			return m.queueFirst(*intent)
		}
		m.unsettled = append(append([]operation(nil), m.unsettled...), operation{intent.Operation, intent.Revision, intent.Kind})
		return m, m.runtime.command(*intent)
	}
	if next.Snapshot().Mode == editor.Exited {
		return m, tea.Quit
	}
	return m, nil
}
func (m Model) Update(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
	if m.runtime == nil {
		m.notice = "Uninitialized host runtime"
		return m, nil
	}
	token, valid := m.runtime.claimUI(m.uiAuthority)
	if !valid {
		m.notice = "Stale UI transition refused; authentic model retains authority"
		return m, nil
	}
	m.uiAuthority = token
	next, cmd := m.update(msg)
	model := next.(Model)
	model.invalidateMenu()
	model.prepareASCIIViewport()
	return model, cmd
}
func (m Model) update(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
	switch msg.(type) {
	case firstReply, RetryFirstReadMsg, macroIOReply, macroReply, macroDuplicateMsg, macroReadRefusedMsg, HostStopMacroMsg, duplicateDispatchMsg, resultMsg, CancelPendingMsg:
		m.menu = menuDisplay{}
	}
	switch v := msg.(type) {
	case tea.EnvMsg, tea.ColorProfileMsg:
		// Bubble Tea startup administration is not delivered user input.
		return m, nil
	case firstReply:
		return m.firstResult(v.ticket)
	case RetryFirstReadMsg:
		if m.firstPending != nil || len(m.unsettled) > 0 || m.macro != nil {
			m.notice = "Actual IO still pending; Read retry refused"
			return m, nil
		}
		return m.reduce(editor.Event{Command: editor.RetryFirstRead})
	case macroIOReply:
		return m.macroIO(v.receipt)
	case macroReply:
		return m.macroReply(v)
	case macroDuplicateMsg:
		m.notice = "Duplicate macro dispatch refused; authentic continuation retained"
		return m, nil
	case macroReadRefusedMsg:
		return m.macroReadRefused(v)
	case HostStopMacroMsg:
		return m.hostStopMacro(v.Reason)
	}
	if _, ok := msg.(duplicateDispatchMsg); ok {
		m.notice = "Duplicate IO dispatch refused; no additional operation executed"
		return m, nil
	}
	if reply, ok := msg.(resultMsg); ok {
		result := reply.result
		if firstKind(result.Kind) {
			m.notice = "Unbound first-save payload refused; authentic receipt retained"
			return m, nil
		}
		if result.Kind == editor.ReadMacro {
			m.notice = "Unbound macro IO payload refused; authentic receipt retained"
			return m, nil
		}
		remaining := make([]operation, 0, len(m.unsettled))

		for _, op := range m.unsettled {
			if op == (operation{result.Operation, result.Revision, result.Kind}) {

			} else {
				remaining = append(remaining, op)
			}
		}
		m.unsettled = remaining
		if warning := Reconciliation(result); warning != "" {
			m.reconcile = warning
		}
		pending := m.session.Snapshot().Pending
		attached := pending != nil && pending.Operation == result.Operation && pending.Revision == result.Revision && pending.Kind == result.Kind
		if !attached && result.Err == nil && result.Outcome.State == storage.Published {
			m.reconcile = "Detached save published a destination; filesystem reconciliation required"
		}
		return m.reduce(editor.Event{Command: editor.Complete, Result: &result})
	}
	if _, ok := msg.(CancelPendingMsg); ok {
		return m.reduce(editor.Event{Command: editor.CancelOperation})
	}
	event := input.Translate(msg)
	if event.Kind == input.Resize {
		m.menu = menuDisplay{}
		m.width = max(1, min(event.Width, 4096))
		m.height = max(1, min(event.Height, 4096))
		return m, nil
	}
	if event.Kind != input.Press {
		switch event.Kind {
		case input.Paste, input.PasteStart, input.PasteEnd, input.Unknown:
			m.notice = "Unsupported event: " + string(event.Kind) + " (" + event.SourceType + ")"
		}
		return m, nil
	}
	m.invalidateMenu()
	if next, handled := m.menuPress(*event.Key); handled {
		return next, nil
	}
	if m.reconcile != "" {
		m.notice = "Resolve filesystem uncertainty before further commands"
		return m, nil
	}
	if (len(m.unsettled) > 0 || m.firstPending != nil) && m.session.Snapshot().Mode != editor.Waiting {
		m.notice = "Detached IO still pending; awaiting its actual receipt"
		return m, nil
	}
	k := event.Key
	if k.IsRepeat {
		m.notice = "Unsupported repeated key event"
		return m, nil
	}
	// Contextual Linux verification retry, not an original WP command claim.
	if k.Code == tea.KeyF10 && k.Mod == 0 && m.session.Snapshot().NewPhase == editor.PublishedUnbound {
		return m.reduce(editor.Event{Command: editor.RetryFirstRead})
	}
	if next, cmd, handled := m.macroKey(k.Code, k.Mod, k.Text); handled {
		return next, cmd
	}
	command, ok := resolve(*k, m.session.Snapshot().Mode)
	if !ok {
		m.notice = "Unsupported key (physical delivery unqualified)"
		return m, nil
	}
	return m.reduce(command)
}
func resolve(k input.Key, mode editor.Mode) (editor.Event, bool) {
	// Delivered text keeps its identity. No q/CtrlQ/CtrlS command aliases.
	if k.Mod&^(tea.ModShift|tea.ModCapsLock) == 0 && k.Text != "" {
		switch mode {
		case editor.RetrieveName, editor.SaveName, editor.MacroName, editor.Editing, editor.Waiting:
			return editor.Event{Command: editor.Text, Text: k.Text}, true
		case editor.ReplaceQuestion, editor.ExitSaveQuestion, editor.ExitQuestion, editor.DeleteQuestion:
			if k.Text == "y" || k.Text == "Y" {
				return editor.Event{Command: editor.Yes}, true
			}
			if k.Text == "n" || k.Text == "N" {
				return editor.Event{Command: editor.No}, true
			}
		case editor.HistoryPreview:
			if k.Text == "1" {
				return editor.Event{Command: editor.Restore}, true
			}
			if k.Text == "2" {
				return editor.Event{Command: editor.Previous}, true
			}
		}
	}
	event := editor.Event{}
	switch {
	case k.Code == tea.KeyF10 && k.Mod == tea.ModAlt:
		event.Command = editor.Macro
	case k.Code == tea.KeyF3 && k.Mod == tea.ModShift:
		event.Command = editor.SwitchDocument
	case k.Code == tea.KeyF10 && k.Mod == tea.ModShift:
		event.Command = editor.Retrieve
	case k.Code == tea.KeyF3 && k.Mod == tea.ModAlt:
		event.Command = editor.Reveal
	case k.Code == tea.KeyF4 && k.Mod == tea.ModAlt:
		event.Command = editor.Block
	case k.Mod != 0:
		return event, false
	default:
		switch k.Code {
		case tea.KeyF10:
			event.Command = editor.Save
		case tea.KeyF1:
			event.Command = editor.F1
		case tea.KeyF7:
			event.Command = editor.F7
		case tea.KeyEscape:
			event.Command = editor.Escape
		case tea.KeyHome:
			event.Command = editor.Home
		case tea.KeyUp:
			event.Command = editor.Up
		case tea.KeyRight:
			event.Command = editor.Right
		case tea.KeyLeft:
			event.Command = editor.Left
		case tea.KeyBackspace:
			event.Command = editor.Backspace
		case tea.KeyDelete:
			event.Command = editor.Delete
		case tea.KeyInsert:
			event.Command = editor.Insert
		case tea.KeyEnter:
			event.Command = editor.Enter
		default:
			return event, false
		}
	}
	return event, true
}

// panes uses only bounded authoritative document projections. It never interprets native bytes,
// wraps original pages, computes printed positions or serializes view text.
func (m Model) panes() (normal, reveal string, x int, err error) {
	f := m.visibleFrame()
	return f.normal, f.reveal, f.x, f.err
}
func (m Model) legacyPanes() (normal, reveal string, x int, err error) {
	d := m.session.Document()
	if d == nil {
		return "", "", 0, nil
	}
	s := m.session.Snapshot()
	gap := s.Gap
	if gap > document.MaxWindowTokens {
		return "", "", 0, fmt.Errorf("cursor beyond bounded %d-token presentation", document.MaxWindowTokens)
	}
	count := min(d.TokenCount(), document.MaxWindowTokens)
	p, err := m.session.Present(0, count)
	if err != nil {
		return "", "", 0, err
	}
	elements := p.Elements
	blockStart, blockEnd := -1, -1
	if s.Selection {
		blockStart, blockEnd = s.SelectionStart, s.SelectionEnd
	}
	if s.Mode == editor.HistoryPreview {
		info, e := m.session.Preview(0, 0)
		if e != nil {
			return "", "", 0, e
		}
		if info.TotalTokens > document.MaxWindowTokens {
			return "", "", 0, fmt.Errorf("preview exceeds bounded %d-token presentation", document.MaxWindowTokens)
		}
		overlay, e := m.session.Preview(0, info.TotalTokens)
		if e != nil {
			return "", "", 0, e
		}
		elements = make([]document.Element, len(overlay.Elements))
		for i, v := range overlay.Elements {
			elements[i] = document.Element{TokenIndex: i, Kind: v.Kind, Text: v.Text}
		}
		blockStart, blockEnd = overlay.FragmentStart, overlay.FragmentEnd
	}
	var n strings.Builder
	var lower []revealRun
	nativeActive := !s.Selection && s.Mode != editor.HistoryPreview
	nx, rx := 0, 0
	for i := 0; i <= len(elements); i++ {
		if i == blockStart {
			n.WriteString("[Block]")
			lower = append(lower, revealRun{text: "[Block]", role: revealBase})
			if i < gap {
				nx += 7
				rx += 7
			}
		}
		if i == blockEnd {
			n.WriteString("[Block]")
			lower = append(lower, revealRun{text: "[Block]", role: revealBase})
			if i < gap {
				nx += 7
				rx += 7
			}
		}
		if i == len(elements) {
			break
		}
		e := elements[i]
		rt := e.Text
		switch e.Kind {
		case document.BoldOn:
			n.WriteString("\x1b[1m")
			rt = "[BOLD]"
		case document.BoldOff:
			n.WriteString("\x1b[22m")
			rt = "[bold]"
		case document.ASCII:
			n.WriteString(e.Text)
			if i < gap {
				nx += len(e.Text)
			}
		default:
			return "", "", 0, fmt.Errorf("unsupported token presentation")
		}
		role := revealBase
		if e.Kind == document.BoldOn || e.Kind == document.BoldOff {
			role = revealCode
		}
		// Overlay elements deliberately have no native span. Only an element
		// returned by the authoritative native Present can own active cells.
		if nativeActive && e.TokenIndex == p.CursorGap && e.Span.Length > 0 {
			role = revealActive
		}
		lower = append(lower, revealRun{text: rt, role: role})
		if i < gap {
			rx += len(rt)
		}
	}
	if nativeActive && p.CursorGap == d.TokenCount() {
		lower = append(lower, revealRun{text: " ", role: revealActive})
	}
	// Terminal cell scrolling is view-only, never original pagination or Pos.
	ns, rs := max(0, nx-max(1, m.width/2)), max(0, rx-max(1, m.width/2))
	x = nx - ns
	return ansi.TruncateLeft(n.String()+"\x1b[0m", ns, ""), renderReveal(lower, rs, m.width), x, nil
}
func clip(s string, width int) string {
	out := ansi.Truncate(s, width, "")
	if strings.Contains(s, "\x1b[") {
		out += "\x1b[0m"
	}
	return out
}
func (m Model) View() tea.View {
	s := m.session.Snapshot()
	frame := m.visibleFrame()
	if frame.anchors {
		return m.anchorView(frame, s.Reveal)
	}
	normal, reveal, x, err := frame.normal, frame.reveal, frame.x, frame.err
	lines := []string{"Wordling 5.2 | bounded native editing", normal}
	if err != nil {
		lines[1] = "Unsupported document presentation: " + err.Error()
	}
	if s.Reveal {
		banner := "Press Reveal Codes to restore screen"
		if frame.inspection.Partial {
			banner = "Partial Reveal inspection | HOST ?N = unsupported owner"
		}
		lines = append(lines, banner, reveal)
	}
	prompt := s.Prompt
	if m.repeatEntry {
		prompt = "Repeat Value = "
	} else if s.Mode == editor.Editing && s.MacroRepeat == 3 {
		prompt = "Repeat Value = 3"
	}
	prompt = m.menuPrompt(s, prompt)
	lines = append(lines, prompt, s.Notice)
	if line := m.macroLine(); line != "" {
		lines = append(lines, line)
	}
	if s.Screen == 2 {
		lines = append(lines, "Doc2")
	}
	if line := firstStateLine(s); line != "" {
		lines = append(lines, line)
	}
	if s.Mode == editor.Waiting {
		lines = append(lines, "Filesystem operation pending")
	}
	if m.notice != "" {
		lines = append(lines, m.notice)
	}
	if m.reconcile != "" {
		lines = append(lines, m.reconcile)
	}
	history := "unknown"
	if s.HistoryCountKnown {
		history = fmt.Sprint(s.HistoryCount)
	}
	typing := ""
	if s.Typeover {
		typing = "Typeover "
	}
	profileLabel := "local-flat: ASCII basenames only; physical key delivery UNQUALIFIED"
	if m.runtime != nil && m.runtime.profile == MacroCOUT {
		profileLabel = "macro-cout: exact C:\\OUT\\ and flat names; physical key delivery UNQUALIFIED"
	}
	lines = append(lines, fmt.Sprintf("%s | %s | gap %d | %s", s.Mode, s.Name, s.Gap, s.SaveState),
		fmt.Sprintf("%sDirty=%t Block=%t revision=%d history=%s qualified=%t", typing, s.Dirty, s.Selection, s.Revision, history, s.HistoryQualified), profileLabel)
	if m.menuState().Visible {
		lines[0] = renderMenuBar()
	}
	for i := range lines {
		lines[i] = clip(lines[i], m.width)
	}
	if len(lines) > m.height {
		lines = lines[:m.height]
	}
	v := tea.NewView(strings.Join(lines, "\n"))
	v.AltScreen = true
	v.KeyboardEnhancements = tea.KeyboardEnhancements{ReportEventTypes: true, ReportAlternateKeys: true, ReportAllKeysAsEscapeCodes: true, ReportAssociatedText: true}
	if (s.Mode == editor.Editing || s.Mode == editor.DeleteQuestion || s.Mode == editor.HistoryPreview || s.Mode == editor.Waiting) && err == nil && m.height > 1 {
		y := 1
		if y < m.height {
			v.Cursor = tea.NewCursor(min(x, m.width-1), y)
		}
	}
	// Prompt caret refers to the original reducer draft, not document position.
	if (s.Mode == editor.RetrieveName || s.Mode == editor.SaveName || s.Mode == editor.MacroName) && len(lines) > 2 {
		prefix := "Document to be retrieved: "
		if s.Mode == editor.SaveName {
			prefix = "Document to be saved: "
		} else if s.Mode == editor.MacroName {
			prefix = "Macro: "
		}
		y := 2
		if s.Reveal {
			y = 4
		}
		if y < m.height {
			v.Cursor = tea.NewCursor(min(len(prefix)+s.Caret, m.width-1), y)
		}
	}
	if (m.repeatEntry || s.Mode == editor.Editing && s.MacroRepeat == 3) && m.height > 2 {
		x := len("Repeat Value = ")
		if !m.repeatEntry {
			x++
		}
		v.Cursor = tea.NewCursor(min(x, m.width-1), 2)
	}
	return v
}

// anchorEligible observes existing source/UI owners. It grants no command or IO
// authority. The pristine menu uses its qualified legacy base on BOTH toggles.
func (m Model) anchorEligible(s editor.Snapshot, frame macroPane) bool {
	if m.width != 80 || m.height != 25 || m.runtime == nil || frame.err != nil || frame.inspection.Partial ||
		s.Mode != editor.Editing || s.Selection || s.Typeover || !s.HistoryCountKnown || s.Screen != 1 || s.MacroRepeat != 1 ||
		s.Pending != nil || s.Prompt != "" || s.Notice != "" || m.repeatEntry || m.notice != "" || m.reconcile != "" ||
		len(m.unsettled) != 0 || m.firstPending != nil || m.macro != nil || m.macroTicket != nil || m.macroCPU ||
		m.macroDeferred != nil || m.macroStatus.Active || m.macroStatus.WorkerPending || m.macroStatus.DisplaySuppressed ||
		s.NewPhase == editor.PublishedUnbound || s.NewPhase == editor.PublicationUncertain ||
		m.menuReason() == "" || m.menu.diagnostic != "" {
		return false
	}
	m.runtime.mu.Lock()
	defer m.runtime.mu.Unlock()
	return !m.runtime.stopping && m.runtime.macro == nil && m.uiAuthority != nil && m.uiAuthority == m.runtime.uiAuthority
}

// A fixed bounded display composition, not original page/ruler/status layout.
// Frozen frames retain this choice even when later live state/size is different.
func (m Model) anchorView(frame macroPane, reveal bool) tea.View {
	lines := make([]string, 25)
	lines[0] = revealBase + frame.normal
	if reveal {
		lines[13] = frame.reveal
		lines[24] = renderReveal([]revealRun{
			{text: "Press ", role: revealBase},
			{text: "Reveal Codes", role: revealCode},
			{text: " to restore screen" + strings.Repeat(" ", 44), role: revealBase},
		}, 0, 80)
	}
	for i := range lines {
		lines[i] = clip(lines[i], m.width)
	}
	lines = lines[:min(len(lines), m.height)]
	v := tea.NewView(strings.Join(lines, "\n"))
	v.AltScreen = true
	v.KeyboardEnhancements = tea.KeyboardEnhancements{ReportEventTypes: true, ReportAlternateKeys: true, ReportAllKeysAsEscapeCodes: true, ReportAssociatedText: true}
	v.Cursor = tea.NewCursor(min(frame.x, m.width-1), 0)
	return v
}


=== ACTUAL_APPROVED_H71_SEMANTIC_SOURCE cd983c05401a90c1f0c912c60ab6ca23d8bd437b:internal/tui/reveal_inspection.go ===
package tui

import (
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"strconv"

	"github.com/lynncoleart/wordling/internal/document"
	"github.com/lynncoleart/wordling/internal/editor"
)

// MaxInspectionRenderBytes bounds expanded HOST markers, independently of E's
// native/text limits. This is host policy, not an original WP resource metric.
const MaxInspectionRenderBytes = 64 << 10

// RevealInspection is copied diagnostic data for the VISIBLE partial pane.
// It grants no native presentation, movement, mutation or IO authority.
type RevealInspection struct {
	Available, Partial            bool
	Revision                      document.Revision
	StartGap, EndGap, TotalTokens int
	TrueEOF                       bool
	Elements                      []document.Element
}

func copyInspection(x RevealInspection) RevealInspection {
	x.Elements = append([]document.Element(nil), x.Elements...)
	return x
}

// Merely reading State/View must not claim or advance UI authority.
func (m Model) inspectionEligible(s editor.Snapshot) bool {
	if m.runtime == nil || s.Mode != editor.Editing || !s.Reveal || s.Selection || s.Pending != nil ||
		m.repeatEntry || m.notice != "" || m.reconcile != "" || len(m.unsettled) != 0 || m.firstPending != nil ||
		m.macro != nil || m.macroTicket != nil || m.macroCPU || m.macroDeferred != nil ||
		m.macroStatus.Active || m.macroStatus.WorkerPending || m.macroStatus.DisplaySuppressed {
		return false
	}
	m.runtime.mu.Lock()
	defer m.runtime.mu.Unlock()
	return !m.runtime.stopping && m.uiAuthority != nil && m.runtime.uiAuthority == m.uiAuthority
}

func (m Model) visibleFrame() macroPane {
	// Select frozen pixels AND diagnostics before inspecting any live native data.
	if m.macroStatus.DisplaySuppressed && m.macroFrozen != nil {
		frame := *m.macroFrozen
		if !m.session.Snapshot().Reveal {
			frame.inspection = RevealInspection{}
		}
		return frame
	}
	s := m.session.Snapshot()
	if frame, ok := m.asciiViewportFrame(s); ok {
		return frame
	}
	var frame macroPane
	if m.inspectionEligible(s) {
		frame = m.liveInspectionFrame(s)
	} else {
		frame = m.legacyFrame()
	}
	frame.anchors = m.anchorEligible(s, frame)
	return frame
}

// State is an observation, not an implicit normal/virtual Present request.
func (m Model) inspectionState() RevealInspection {
	if m.macroStatus.DisplaySuppressed && m.macroFrozen != nil {
		if !m.session.Snapshot().Reveal {
			return RevealInspection{}
		}
		return copyInspection(m.macroFrozen.inspection)
	}
	s := m.session.Snapshot()
	if m.ascii.checked && m.ascii.whole && m.ascii.revision == s.NativeRevision && m.ordinaryViewEligible(s) {
		return RevealInspection{}
	}
	if !m.inspectionEligible(s) {
		return RevealInspection{}
	}
	return copyInspection(m.liveInspectionFrame(s).inspection)
}
func (m Model) liveInspectionFrame(s editor.Snapshot) macroPane {
	session := m.session
	d := session.Document()
	if d == nil {
		return m.legacyFrame()
	}
	count := min(d.TokenCount(), document.MaxWindowTokens)
	catalogue, err := document.InspectRevealCatalogue(d, s.NativeRevision, 0, count)
	if err != nil {
		return macroPane{err: err}
	}
	// E authenticates/bounds the SAME captured session/window before Present's
	// contiguous raw copy. The session is immutable; no caller wrapper is reused.
	frame := m.legacyFrame()
	var failure *document.Failure
	if !errors.As(frame.err, &failure) || failure.Category != document.UnsupportedToken {
		return frame
	}
	lower, err := inspectionRuns(catalogue)
	if err != nil {
		return macroPane{err: err}
	}
	frame.reveal = renderReveal(lower, 0, m.width)
	frame.inspection = RevealInspection{Available: true, Partial: true, Revision: catalogue.Revision,
		StartGap: catalogue.StartGap, EndGap: catalogue.EndGap, TotalTokens: catalogue.TotalTokens,
		TrueEOF: catalogue.TrueEOF, Elements: catalogue.Elements}
	return frame
}
func (m Model) legacyFrame() macroPane {
	normal, reveal, x, err := m.legacyPanes()
	return macroPane{normal: normal, reveal: reveal, x: x, err: err}
}

// Preflight lengths BEFORE run/marker/result construction. Elements come only
// from the approved E API; H neither reads native bytes nor reclassifies owners.
func inspectionRuns(c document.CatalogueInspection) ([]revealRun, error) {
	if len(c.Elements) > document.MaxWindowTokens {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("inspection render owner limit")
	}
	size := 0
	for _, e := range c.Elements {
		n := len(e.Text)
		if e.Kind == document.Opaque && e.Text == "" {
			if e.TokenIndex < 0 {
				return nil, fmt.Errorf("inspection invalid owner ordinal")
			}
			n = 2
			for value := e.TokenIndex; value >= 10; value /= 10 {
				n++
			}
		}
		if n > MaxInspectionRenderBytes-size {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("inspection expanded render limit")
		}
		size += n
	}
	runs := make([]revealRun, 0, len(c.Elements))
	for _, e := range c.Elements {
		text, role := e.Text, revealBase
		if e.Kind == document.Opaque && text == "" {
			text = "?" + strconv.Itoa(e.TokenIndex)
		} else if e.Kind != document.ASCII {
			role = revealCode
		}
		runs = append(runs, revealRun{text: text, role: role})
	}
	return runs, nil
}


=== ACTUAL_APPROVED_H71_SEMANTIC_SOURCE cd983c05401a90c1f0c912c60ab6ca23d8bd437b:internal/tui/ascii_viewport.go ===
package tui

import (
	"strings"

	"github.com/lynncoleart/wordling/internal/document"
	"github.com/lynncoleart/wordling/internal/editor"
)

// A single copied display fact, not native authority or a payload cache. The
// opaque revision is the authentic owner key; equal bytes do not compare equal.
// Invalidating the slot releases its one previous revision. Persistent Model
// values still own their respective immutable observations.
type asciiQualification struct {
	revision       document.Revision
	total          int
	checked, whole bool
}

const maxASCIIViewportOwners = 16384

func (m Model) ordinaryViewEligible(s editor.Snapshot) bool {
	if m.runtime == nil || s.Mode != editor.Editing || s.Selection || s.Typeover || !s.HistoryCountKnown || s.Screen != 1 || s.MacroRepeat != 1 ||
		s.Pending != nil || s.Prompt != "" || s.Notice != "" || m.repeatEntry || m.notice != "" || m.reconcile != "" ||
		len(m.unsettled) != 0 || m.firstPending != nil || m.macro != nil || m.macroTicket != nil || m.macroCPU || m.macroFrozen != nil ||
		m.macroDeferred != nil || m.macroStatus.Active || m.macroStatus.WorkerPending || m.macroStatus.DisplaySuppressed ||
		s.NewPhase == editor.PublishedUnbound || s.NewPhase == editor.PublicationUncertain || m.menuReason() == "" || m.menu.diagnostic != "" {
		return false
	}
	m.runtime.mu.Lock()
	defer m.runtime.mu.Unlock()
	return !m.runtime.stopping && m.runtime.macro == nil && m.uiAuthority != nil && m.uiAuthority == m.runtime.uiAuthority
}

// Only the returned authentic Update chain prepares the fact, after dispatch.
// View/State and stale Update returns never enter this path.
func (m *Model) prepareASCIIViewport() {
	s := m.session.Snapshot()
	if !m.ordinaryViewEligible(s) {
		m.ascii = asciiQualification{}
		return
	}
	d := m.session.Document()
	if d == nil || d.TokenCount() <= document.MaxWindowTokens {
		m.ascii = asciiQualification{}
		return
	}
	if m.ascii.checked && m.ascii.revision == s.NativeRevision {
		return
	}
	m.ascii = qualifyASCII(d, s.NativeRevision)
}
func qualifyASCII(d *document.Document, revision document.Revision) asciiQualification {
	total := d.TokenCount()
	fact := asciiQualification{revision: revision, total: total, checked: true}
	// Preflight total before requesting any catalogue allocation. This is only a
	// HOST view limit, never a native open/edit limit.
	if total <= document.MaxWindowTokens || total > maxASCIIViewportOwners {
		return fact
	}
	rawBytes, textBytes := 0, 0
	previousEnd := -1
	for start := 0; start < total; start += document.MaxWindowTokens {
		count := min(document.MaxWindowTokens, total-start)
		c, err := document.InspectRevealCatalogue(d, revision, start, count)
		if err != nil || c.Revision != revision || c.StartGap != start || c.EndGap != start+count || c.TotalTokens != total || c.TrueEOF != (start+count == total) || len(c.Elements) != count {
			return fact
		}
		for i, e := range c.Elements {
			if e.TokenIndex != start+i || e.Kind != document.ASCII || e.Span.Length != 1 || len(e.Text) != 1 || e.Text[0] < 32 || e.Text[0] > 126 || e.Span.Offset < 0 || (previousEnd >= 0 && e.Span.Offset != previousEnd) {
				return fact
			}
			rawBytes += e.Span.Length
			textBytes += len(e.Text)
			previousEnd = e.Span.Offset + e.Span.Length
			if rawBytes > 1<<20 || textBytes > 128<<10 {
				return fact
			}
		}
	}
	fact.whole = true
	return fact
}
func (m Model) asciiViewportFrame(s editor.Snapshot) (macroPane, bool) {
	f := m.ascii
	if !f.checked || !f.whole || f.revision != s.NativeRevision || !m.ordinaryViewEligible(s) {
		return macroPane{}, false
	}
	start := max(0, s.Gap-m.width/2)
	count := min(m.width, f.total-start)
	if start < 0 || start > s.Gap || s.Gap > f.total || count < 0 || count > document.MaxWindowTokens || (s.Gap < f.total && s.Gap >= start+count) {
		return macroPane{}, false
	}
	p, err := m.session.Present(start, count)
	if err != nil {
		return macroPane{err: err}, true
	}
	var normal strings.Builder
	runs := make([]revealRun, 0, len(p.Elements)+1)
	for _, e := range p.Elements {
		normal.WriteString(e.Text)
		role := revealBase
		if e.TokenIndex == p.CursorGap && e.Span.Length > 0 {
			role = revealActive
		}
		runs = append(runs, revealRun{text: e.Text, role: role})
	}
	if p.CursorGap == f.total {
		runs = append(runs, revealRun{text: " ", role: revealActive})
	}
	// Already an absolute owner window: render from local zero, never scroll again.
	return macroPane{normal: normal.String() + "\x1b[0m", reveal: renderReveal(runs, 0, m.width), x: s.Gap - start, anchors: m.width == 80 && m.height == 25}, true
}


=== ACTUAL_APPROVED_H71_SEMANTIC_SOURCE cd983c05401a90c1f0c912c60ab6ca23d8bd437b:internal/tui/new_model.go ===
package tui

import (
	tea "charm.land/bubbletea/v2"
	"github.com/lynncoleart/wordling/internal/editor"
	"github.com/lynncoleart/wordling/internal/storage"
)

func firstPendingCount(t *firstTicket) int {
	if t != nil {
		return 1
	}
	return 0
}
func (m Model) queueFirst(i editor.Intent) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
	if m.firstPending != nil {
		m.notice = "Previous first-save IO still owns its receipt"
		return m, nil
	}
	t, c := m.runtime.firstCommand(i)
	m.firstPending = t
	return m, c
}
func (m Model) firstResult(t *firstTicket) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
	if t == nil || m.firstPending != t {
		m.notice = "Foreign, duplicate or stale first-save reply refused"
		return m, nil
	}
	result, refusal, ok := m.runtime.deliverFirst(t)
	if !ok {
		m.notice = "First-save receipt lacks current host authority"
		return m, nil
	}
	m.firstPending = nil
	if refusal != nil {
		// No helper ran, so no genuine editor observation exists. Do not forge one.
		// Reducer cancellation conservatively retains uncertainty/read-only status.
		next, _, _ := m.session.Reduce(editor.Event{Command: editor.CancelOperation})
		m.session = next
		m.notice = refusal.Error()
		return m, nil
	}
	next, intent, err := m.session.Reduce(editor.Event{Command: editor.Complete, Result: &result})
	m.session = next
	m.notice = ""
	if err != nil {
		m.notice = err.Error()
	}
	phase := next.Snapshot().NewPhase
	if phase == editor.PublicationUncertain {
		m.reconcile = "Save outcome is unknown. Saving and normal exit are disabled; inspect filesystem."
	} else if next.Snapshot().Last.Applied == false && result.Outcome.State == storage.Published {
		m.reconcile = "A detached save created a file; the current buffer was not saved. Inspect filesystem."
	}
	if intent != nil {
		return m.queueFirst(*intent)
	}
	return m, nil
}
func firstStateLine(s editor.Snapshot) string {
	switch s.NewPhase {
	case editor.NewUnpublished:
		return "New: unsaved"
	case editor.AwaitPublish:
		return "Saving new document; save not confirmed"
	case editor.AwaitFirstRead:
		return "File created; verification pending. Save not confirmed."
	case editor.PublishedUnbound:
		return "Saved file could not be verified. F10 retries verification without saving again."
	case editor.PublicationUncertain:
		return "Save outcome unknown. Saving is disabled; inspect filesystem."
	case editor.NewAnchored:
		return ""
	}
	return ""
}


=== ACTUAL_APPROVED_H71_SEMANTIC_SOURCE cd983c05401a90c1f0c912c60ab6ca23d8bd437b:internal/wp51/native.go ===
// Package wp51 validates the bounded WP 5.1 DOS document outer framing
// described in contracts/native-framing.md. It preserves exact native bytes;
// acceptance establishes neither semantic validity nor safe semantic editing.
package wp51

import (
	"bytes"
	"encoding/binary"
	"fmt"
	"sort"
)

// Safety policies for this reader slice, not original application format limits.
const (
	MaxInputBytes                 = 16 << 20
	MaxIndexBlocks                = 4096
	MaxPacketEntries              = 16384
	OuterFramedPayloadUnvalidated = "outer-framed, payload-unvalidated"
)

// Category distinguishes unsupported variants from malformed input and limits.
type Category string

const (
	UnsupportedFormat     Category = "UnsupportedFormat"
	UnsupportedProduct    Category = "UnsupportedProduct"
	UnsupportedFileType   Category = "UnsupportedFileType"
	UnsupportedVersion    Category = "UnsupportedVersion"
	UnsupportedEncryption Category = "UnsupportedEncryption"
	UnsupportedVariant    Category = "UnsupportedVariant"
	UnsupportedStructure  Category = "UnsupportedStructure"
	InputLimit            Category = "InputLimit"
	TraversalLimit        Category = "TraversalLimit"
	TruncatedInput        Category = "TruncatedInput"
	MalformedPrefix       Category = "MalformedPrefix"
	MalformedBody         Category = "MalformedBody"
)

// Failure reports an absolute byte offset and the violated field or boundary.
// Detail further identifies cases such as Cycle and DelimiterMismatch.
type Failure struct {
	Category      Category
	Offset        int
	Field, Detail string
}

func (e *Failure) Error() string {
	return fmt.Sprintf("wp51: %s at %d (%s): %s", e.Category, e.Offset, e.Field, e.Detail)
}
func fail(c Category, o int, field, detail string) error { return &Failure{c, o, field, detail} }

// Span is an absolute half-open byte range, represented by offset and length.
type Span struct{ Offset, Length int }

// Block describes a checked special index block in native traversal order.
type Block struct {
	Data  Span
	Count int
	Next  uint32
}

// Packet retains its type, index relationship and opaque bounded data span.
type Packet struct {
	Type        uint16
	Index, Data Span
}

// Inspection describes framing only. Packet and function payloads are unvalidated.
// Every slice returned by Inspect is independent of the image.
type Inspection struct {
	Validation      string
	Prefix, Body    Span
	Blocks          []Block
	Packets         []Packet
	OuterTokenCount int
}

// Image owns its complete immutable input. Construct accepted images with Parse.
// Bytes not referenced by metadata remain preserved in this authoritative image.
type Image struct {
	data []byte
	info Inspection
}

// Export returns a fresh, byte-identical no-edit copy, including all gaps/tails.
func (i *Image) Export() []byte { return bytes.Clone(i.data) }

// Inspect returns an independent copy of framing metadata, without byte aliases.
func (i *Image) Inspect() Inspection {
	c := i.info
	c.Blocks = append([]Block(nil), c.Blocks...)
	c.Packets = append([]Packet(nil), c.Packets...)
	return c
}

// Bytes returns a fresh copy of a bounded span, or false for an invalid range.
func (i *Image) Bytes(s Span) ([]byte, bool) {
	if s.Offset < 0 || s.Length < 0 || s.Offset > len(i.data) || s.Length > len(i.data)-s.Offset {
		return nil, false
	}
	return bytes.Clone(i.data[s.Offset : s.Offset+s.Length]), true
}

// Parse checks identity, prefix/index/packet boundaries and body outer framing.
// The size limit is checked before copying. A refusal returns no accepted image
// and never changes caller data. Callers must not mutate input during Parse.
// File acquisition/replacement and semantic mutation belong to other packages.
func Parse(input []byte) (*Image, error) {
	if len(input) > MaxInputBytes {
		return nil, fail(InputLimit, 0, "input", "exceeds 16 MiB")
	}
	if len(input) < 16 {
		return nil, fail(TruncatedInput, len(input), "header", "requires 16 bytes")
	}
	if !bytes.Equal(input[:4], []byte{0xff, 'W', 'P', 'C'}) {
		return nil, fail(UnsupportedFormat, 0, "signature", "expected FF575043")
	}
	if input[8] != 1 {
		return nil, fail(UnsupportedProduct, 8, "product", "expected 1")
	}
	if input[9] != 10 {
		return nil, fail(UnsupportedFileType, 9, "file type", "only document type 10 is supported")
	}
	if input[10] != 0 || input[11] != 1 {
		return nil, fail(UnsupportedVersion, 10, "stored version", "expected 0/1")
	}
	if u16(input, 12) != 0 {
		return nil, fail(UnsupportedEncryption, 12, "password", "encrypted input is unsupported")
	}
	if u16(input, 14) != 0 {
		return nil, fail(UnsupportedVariant, 14, "reserved header", "nonzero reserved value")
	}
	boundary := u32(input, 4)
	if boundary < 16 || uint64(boundary) > uint64(len(input)) {
		return nil, fail(MalformedPrefix, 4, "body offset", "outside [16,EOF]")
	}
	b := int(boundary)
	info := Inspection{Validation: OuterFramedPayloadUnvalidated, Prefix: Span{0, b}, Body: Span{b, len(input) - b}}
	if err := prefix(input, b, &info); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	count, err := body(input, b)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	info.OuterTokenCount = count
	return &Image{data: bytes.Clone(input), info: info}, nil
}
func u16(b []byte, o int) uint16 { return binary.LittleEndian.Uint16(b[o : o+2]) }
func u32(b []byte, o int) uint32 { return binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(b[o : o+4]) }

func prefix(data []byte, b int, info *Inspection) error {
	visited := make(map[int]bool)
	offset := 16
	graphics := false
	done := false
	for !done {
		if visited[offset] {
			return fail(MalformedPrefix, offset, "next block", "Cycle")
		}
		if len(info.Blocks) >= MaxIndexBlocks {
			return fail(TraversalLimit, offset, "index blocks", "exceeds 4096")
		}
		if offset < 16 || offset > b || b-offset < 10 {
			return fail(MalformedPrefix, offset, "block header", "outside prefix or truncated")
		}
		visited[offset] = true
		if u16(data, offset) != 0xfffb {
			return fail(MalformedPrefix, offset, "block type", "expected FFFB")
		}
		count := int(u16(data, offset+2))
		size := int(u16(data, offset+4))
		if count == 0 || size != count*10 {
			return fail(MalformedPrefix, offset+2, "block count/size", "zero count or inconsistent size")
		}
		if size > b-offset {
			return fail(MalformedPrefix, offset+4, "block size", "block crosses body boundary")
		}
		if count != 5 {
			return fail(UnsupportedVariant, offset+2, "block count", "only five-entry document blocks validated")
		}
		next := u32(data, offset+6)
		// Only the followed next pointer is interpreted; an early type 0 ends enumeration.
		block := Block{Data: Span{offset, size}, Count: count, Next: next}
		info.Blocks = append(info.Blocks, block)
		for j := 1; j < count; j++ {
			index := offset + 10*j
			k := u16(data, index)
			if k == 0 {
				done = true
				break
			}
			if graphics {
				return fail(UnsupportedStructure, index, "packet 8 ordering", "graphics index must be last")
			}
			if k == 0xfffb {
				return fail(UnsupportedStructure, index, "packet type", "special header in general entry")
			}
			if len(info.Packets) >= MaxPacketEntries {
				return fail(TraversalLimit, index, "packet entries", "exceeds 16384")
			}
			length, pos := u32(data, index+2), u32(data, index+6)
			if pos < 16 || uint64(pos) > uint64(b) || uint64(length) > uint64(b)-uint64(pos) {
				return fail(MalformedPrefix, index+2, "packet position/length", "outside prefix")
			}
			p := Packet{Type: k, Index: Span{index, 10}, Data: Span{int(pos), int(length)}}
			if k == 8 {
				if length != 2 {
					return fail(MalformedPrefix, index+2, "packet 8 length", "requires two-byte count")
				}
				if u16(data, int(pos)) != 0 {
					return fail(UnsupportedStructure, int(pos), "graphics count", "nonzero graphics extent unvalidated")
				}
				graphics = true
			}
			info.Packets = append(info.Packets, p)
		}
		if done || next == 0 {
			break
		}
		if next < 16 || uint64(next) >= uint64(b) {
			return fail(MalformedPrefix, offset+6, "next block", "outside prefix")
		}
		offset = int(next)
	}
	// Sort temporary spans only. Native metadata and bytes retain original order.
	type extent struct {
		span  Span
		block bool
	}
	ranges := make([]extent, 0, len(info.Blocks)+len(info.Packets))
	for _, block := range info.Blocks {
		ranges = append(ranges, extent{block.Data, true})
	}
	for _, packet := range info.Packets {
		if packet.Data.Length > 0 {
			ranges = append(ranges, extent{packet.Data, false})
		}
	}
	sort.Slice(ranges, func(i, j int) bool { return ranges[i].span.Offset < ranges[j].span.Offset })
	for j := 1; j < len(ranges); j++ {
		prev, cur := ranges[j-1], ranges[j]
		if cur.span.Offset < prev.span.Offset+prev.span.Length {
			if prev.block && cur.block {
				return fail(MalformedPrefix, cur.span.Offset, "index blocks", "overlapping blocks")
			}
			return fail(UnsupportedStructure, cur.span.Offset, "prefix spans", "overlapping packet/index or packet data")
		}
	}
	return nil
}

var fixedWidths = [16]int{4, 9, 11, 3, 3, 5, 6, 7, 4, 5, 6, 6, 8, 10, 10, 12}

func body(data []byte, start int) (int, error) {
	tokens := 0
	for q := start; q < len(data); tokens++ {
		code := data[q]
		remaining := len(data) - q
		if code == 0 || code == 0x7f {
			return 0, fail(MalformedBody, q, "single-byte token", "00 and 7F are not standalone tokens")
		}
		if code < 0xc0 {
			q++
			continue
		}
		if code <= 0xcf {
			width := fixedWidths[code-0xc0]
			if width > remaining {
				return 0, fail(MalformedBody, q, "fixed function", "truncated")
			}
			if data[q+width-1] != code {
				return 0, fail(MalformedBody, q+width-1, "fixed trailer", "DelimiterMismatch")
			}
			q += width
			continue
		}
		if remaining < 4 {
			return 0, fail(MalformedBody, q, "variable header", "truncated")
		}
		length := int(u16(data, q+2))
		if length < 4 {
			return 0, fail(MalformedBody, q+2, "variable length", "requires L>=4")
		}
		if length+4 > remaining {
			return 0, fail(MalformedBody, q+2, "variable length", "span crosses EOF")
		}
		if u16(data, q+length) != uint16(length) || data[q+length+2] != data[q+1] || data[q+length+3] != code {
			return 0, fail(MalformedBody, q+length, "variable trailer", "DelimiterMismatch")
		}
		q += length + 4
	}
	return tokens, nil
}

// CancelPendingMsg is an explicit host operation, never an original key alias.
// Disk IO continues; commands are held until the late receipt is reconciled.
type CancelPendingMsg struct{}

// RetryFirstReadMsg is explicit host safety, not an original-key alias.
// It cannot publish or acquire a replacement directory.
type RetryFirstReadMsg struct{}
type operation struct {
	id, revision uint64
	kind         editor.IntentKind
}
type Model struct {
	ascii             asciiQualification
	menu              menuDisplay
	runtime           *Runtime
	session           editor.Session
	width, height     int
	notice, reconcile string
	unsettled         []operation
	macro             *macroFlow
	macroTicket       *macroTicket
	macroCPU          bool
	macroStatus       MacroStatus
	macroFrozen       *macroPane
	repeatEntry       bool
	characterRepeat   characterRepeat
	uiAuthority       *uiTransition
	macroDeferred     *macroIOReceipt
	firstPending      *firstTicket
}
type State struct {
	MenuBar          MenuBar
	RevealInspection RevealInspection

	Session                   editor.Snapshot
	Width, Height             int
	Notice, Reconciliation    string
	PendingIO                 int
	PhysicalDeliveryQualified bool
	Macro                     MacroStatus
	Profile                   string
	NativeProfile             string
}

func (m Model) State() State {
	profile := ""
	if m.runtime != nil {
		profile = m.runtime.profile
	}
	return State{MenuBar: m.menuState(), RevealInspection: m.inspectionState(), NativeProfile: NativeProfile, Profile: profile, Session: m.session.Snapshot(), Width: m.width, Height: m.height, Notice: m.notice, Reconciliation: m.reconcile, PendingIO: len(m.unsettled) + firstPendingCount(m.firstPending), Macro: m.macroStatus}
}

// Session returns an immutable value for measurements/inspection. Never fork it
// to execute independent intents: this Model owns the sole authoritative chain.
func (m Model) Session() editor.Session { return m.session }
func (m Model) Init() tea.Cmd           { return nil }
func (m Model) reduce(event editor.Event) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
	previousMode := m.session.Snapshot().Mode
	next, intent, err := m.session.Reduce(event)
	m.session = next
	if err == nil && next.Snapshot().Mode == editor.MacroName && previousMode != editor.MacroName {
		m.macroStatus = MacroStatus{}
		m.macroFrozen = nil
	}
	m.notice = ""
	if err != nil {
		m.notice = err.Error()
	}
	if intent != nil {
		if firstKind(intent.Kind) {
			return m.queueFirst(*intent)
		}
		m.unsettled = append(append([]operation(nil), m.unsettled...), operation{intent.Operation, intent.Revision, intent.Kind})
		return m, m.runtime.command(*intent)
	}
	if next.Snapshot().Mode == editor.Exited {
		return m, tea.Quit
	}
	return m, nil
}
func (m Model) Update(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
	if m.runtime == nil {
		m.notice = "Uninitialized host runtime"
		return m, nil
	}
	token, valid := m.runtime.claimUI(m.uiAuthority)
	if !valid {
		m.notice = "Stale UI transition refused; authentic model retains authority"
		return m, nil
	}
	m.uiAuthority = token
	next, cmd := m.update(msg)
	model := next.(Model)
	model.invalidateMenu()
	model.prepareASCIIViewport()
	return model, cmd
}
func (m Model) update(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
	m.invalidateCharacterMessage(msg)
	switch msg.(type) {
	case firstReply, RetryFirstReadMsg, macroIOReply, macroReply, macroDuplicateMsg, macroReadRefusedMsg, HostStopMacroMsg, duplicateDispatchMsg, resultMsg, CancelPendingMsg:
		m.menu = menuDisplay{}
	}
	switch v := msg.(type) {
	case tea.EnvMsg, tea.ColorProfileMsg:
		// Bubble Tea startup administration is not delivered user input.
		return m, nil
	case firstReply:
		return m.firstResult(v.ticket)
	case RetryFirstReadMsg:
		if m.firstPending != nil || len(m.unsettled) > 0 || m.macro != nil {
			m.notice = "Actual IO still pending; Read retry refused"
			return m, nil
		}
		return m.reduce(editor.Event{Command: editor.RetryFirstRead})
	case macroIOReply:
		return m.macroIO(v.receipt)
	case macroReply:
		return m.macroReply(v)
	case macroDuplicateMsg:
		m.notice = "Duplicate macro dispatch refused; authentic continuation retained"
		return m, nil
	case macroReadRefusedMsg:
		return m.macroReadRefused(v)
	case HostStopMacroMsg:
		return m.hostStopMacro(v.Reason)
	}
	if _, ok := msg.(duplicateDispatchMsg); ok {
		m.notice = "Duplicate IO dispatch refused; no additional operation executed"
		return m, nil
	}
	if reply, ok := msg.(resultMsg); ok {
		result := reply.result
		if firstKind(result.Kind) {
			m.notice = "Unbound first-save payload refused; authentic receipt retained"
			return m, nil
		}
		if result.Kind == editor.ReadMacro {
			m.notice = "Unbound macro IO payload refused; authentic receipt retained"
			return m, nil
		}
		remaining := make([]operation, 0, len(m.unsettled))

		for _, op := range m.unsettled {
			if op == (operation{result.Operation, result.Revision, result.Kind}) {

			} else {
				remaining = append(remaining, op)
			}
		}
		m.unsettled = remaining
		if warning := Reconciliation(result); warning != "" {
			m.reconcile = warning
		}
		pending := m.session.Snapshot().Pending
		attached := pending != nil && pending.Operation == result.Operation && pending.Revision == result.Revision && pending.Kind == result.Kind
		if !attached && result.Err == nil && result.Outcome.State == storage.Published {
			m.reconcile = "Detached save published a destination; filesystem reconciliation required"
		}
		return m.reduce(editor.Event{Command: editor.Complete, Result: &result})
	}
	if _, ok := msg.(CancelPendingMsg); ok {
		return m.reduce(editor.Event{Command: editor.CancelOperation})
	}
	event := input.Translate(msg)
	if event.Kind == input.Resize {
		m.menu = menuDisplay{}
		m.width = max(1, min(event.Width, 4096))
		m.height = max(1, min(event.Height, 4096))
		return m, nil
	}
	if event.Kind != input.Press {
		switch event.Kind {
		case input.Paste, input.PasteStart, input.PasteEnd, input.Unknown:
			m.notice = "Unsupported event: " + string(event.Kind) + " (" + event.SourceType + ")"
		}
		return m, nil
	}
	if m.characterRepeat.active {
		return m.characterPress(*event.Key)
	}
	m.invalidateMenu()
	if next, handled := m.menuPress(*event.Key); handled {
		return next, nil
	}
	if m.reconcile != "" {
		m.notice = "Resolve filesystem uncertainty before further commands"
		return m, nil
	}
	if (len(m.unsettled) > 0 || m.firstPending != nil) && m.session.Snapshot().Mode != editor.Waiting {
		m.notice = "Detached IO still pending; awaiting its actual receipt"
		return m, nil
	}
	k := event.Key
	if k.IsRepeat {
		m.notice = "Unsupported repeated key event"
		return m, nil
	}
	// Contextual Linux verification retry, not an original WP command claim.
	if k.Code == tea.KeyF10 && k.Mod == 0 && m.session.Snapshot().NewPhase == editor.PublishedUnbound {
		return m.reduce(editor.Event{Command: editor.RetryFirstRead})
	}
	if next, handled := m.beginCharacterRepeat(*k); handled {
		return next, nil
	}
	if next, cmd, handled := m.macroKey(k.Code, k.Mod, k.Text); handled {
		return next, cmd
	}
	command, ok := resolve(*k, m.session.Snapshot().Mode)
	if !ok {
		m.notice = "Unsupported key (physical delivery unqualified)"
		return m, nil
	}
	return m.reduce(command)
}
func resolve(k input.Key, mode editor.Mode) (editor.Event, bool) {
	// Delivered text keeps its identity. No q/CtrlQ/CtrlS command aliases.
	if k.Mod&^(tea.ModShift|tea.ModCapsLock) == 0 && k.Text != "" {
		switch mode {
		case editor.RetrieveName, editor.SaveName, editor.MacroName, editor.Editing, editor.Waiting:
			return editor.Event{Command: editor.Text, Text: k.Text}, true
		case editor.ReplaceQuestion, editor.ExitSaveQuestion, editor.ExitQuestion, editor.DeleteQuestion:
			if k.Text == "y" || k.Text == "Y" {
				return editor.Event{Command: editor.Yes}, true
			}
			if k.Text == "n" || k.Text == "N" {
				return editor.Event{Command: editor.No}, true
			}
		case editor.HistoryPreview:
			if k.Text == "1" {
				return editor.Event{Command: editor.Restore}, true
			}
			if k.Text == "2" {
				return editor.Event{Command: editor.Previous}, true
			}
		}
	}
	event := editor.Event{}
	switch {
	case k.Code == tea.KeyF10 && k.Mod == tea.ModAlt:
		event.Command = editor.Macro
	case k.Code == tea.KeyF3 && k.Mod == tea.ModShift:
		event.Command = editor.SwitchDocument
	case k.Code == tea.KeyF10 && k.Mod == tea.ModShift:
		event.Command = editor.Retrieve
	case k.Code == tea.KeyF3 && k.Mod == tea.ModAlt:
		event.Command = editor.Reveal
	case k.Code == tea.KeyF4 && k.Mod == tea.ModAlt:
		event.Command = editor.Block
	case k.Mod != 0:
		return event, false
	default:
		switch k.Code {
		case tea.KeyF10:
			event.Command = editor.Save
		case tea.KeyF1:
			event.Command = editor.F1
		case tea.KeyF7:
			event.Command = editor.F7
		case tea.KeyEscape:
			event.Command = editor.Escape
		case tea.KeyHome:
			event.Command = editor.Home
		case tea.KeyUp:
			event.Command = editor.Up
		case tea.KeyRight:
			event.Command = editor.Right
		case tea.KeyLeft:
			event.Command = editor.Left
		case tea.KeyBackspace:
			event.Command = editor.Backspace
		case tea.KeyDelete:
			event.Command = editor.Delete
		case tea.KeyInsert:
			event.Command = editor.Insert
		case tea.KeyEnter:
			event.Command = editor.Enter
		default:
			return event, false
		}
	}
	return event, true
}

// panes uses only bounded authoritative document projections. It never interprets native bytes,
// wraps original pages, computes printed positions or serializes view text.
func (m Model) panes() (normal, reveal string, x int, err error) {
	f := m.visibleFrame()
	return f.normal, f.reveal, f.x, f.err
}
func (m Model) legacyPanes() (normal, reveal string, x int, err error) {
	d := m.session.Document()
	if d == nil {
		return "", "", 0, nil
	}
	s := m.session.Snapshot()
	gap := s.Gap
	if gap > document.MaxWindowTokens {
		return "", "", 0, fmt.Errorf("cursor beyond bounded %d-token presentation", document.MaxWindowTokens)
	}
	count := min(d.TokenCount(), document.MaxWindowTokens)
	p, err := m.session.Present(0, count)
	if err != nil {
		return "", "", 0, err
	}
	elements := p.Elements
	blockStart, blockEnd := -1, -1
	if s.Selection {
		blockStart, blockEnd = s.SelectionStart, s.SelectionEnd
	}
	if s.Mode == editor.HistoryPreview {
		info, e := m.session.Preview(0, 0)
		if e != nil {
			return "", "", 0, e
		}
		if info.TotalTokens > document.MaxWindowTokens {
			return "", "", 0, fmt.Errorf("preview exceeds bounded %d-token presentation", document.MaxWindowTokens)
		}
		overlay, e := m.session.Preview(0, info.TotalTokens)
		if e != nil {
			return "", "", 0, e
		}
		elements = make([]document.Element, len(overlay.Elements))
		for i, v := range overlay.Elements {
			elements[i] = document.Element{TokenIndex: i, Kind: v.Kind, Text: v.Text}
		}
		blockStart, blockEnd = overlay.FragmentStart, overlay.FragmentEnd
	}
	var n strings.Builder
	var lower []revealRun
	nativeActive := !s.Selection && s.Mode != editor.HistoryPreview
	nx, rx := 0, 0
	for i := 0; i <= len(elements); i++ {
		if i == blockStart {
			n.WriteString("[Block]")
			lower = append(lower, revealRun{text: "[Block]", role: revealBase})
			if i < gap {
				nx += 7
				rx += 7
			}
		}
		if i == blockEnd {
			n.WriteString("[Block]")
			lower = append(lower, revealRun{text: "[Block]", role: revealBase})
			if i < gap {
				nx += 7
				rx += 7
			}
		}
		if i == len(elements) {
			break
		}
		e := elements[i]
		rt := e.Text
		switch e.Kind {
		case document.BoldOn:
			n.WriteString("\x1b[1m")
			rt = "[BOLD]"
		case document.BoldOff:
			n.WriteString("\x1b[22m")
			rt = "[bold]"
		case document.ASCII:
			n.WriteString(e.Text)
			if i < gap {
				nx += len(e.Text)
			}
		default:
			return "", "", 0, fmt.Errorf("unsupported token presentation")
		}
		role := revealBase
		if e.Kind == document.BoldOn || e.Kind == document.BoldOff {
			role = revealCode
		}
		// Overlay elements deliberately have no native span. Only an element
		// returned by the authoritative native Present can own active cells.
		if nativeActive && e.TokenIndex == p.CursorGap && e.Span.Length > 0 {
			role = revealActive
		}
		lower = append(lower, revealRun{text: rt, role: role})
		if i < gap {
			rx += len(rt)
		}
	}
	if nativeActive && p.CursorGap == d.TokenCount() {
		lower = append(lower, revealRun{text: " ", role: revealActive})
	}
	// Terminal cell scrolling is view-only, never original pagination or Pos.
	ns, rs := max(0, nx-max(1, m.width/2)), max(0, rx-max(1, m.width/2))
	x = nx - ns
	return ansi.TruncateLeft(n.String()+"\x1b[0m", ns, ""), renderReveal(lower, rs, m.width), x, nil
}
func clip(s string, width int) string {
	out := ansi.Truncate(s, width, "")
	if strings.Contains(s, "\x1b[") {
		out += "\x1b[0m"
	}
	return out
}
func (m Model) View() tea.View {
	s := m.session.Snapshot()
	frame := m.visibleFrame()
	if frame.anchors {
		return m.anchorView(frame, s.Reveal)
	}
	normal, reveal, x, err := frame.normal, frame.reveal, frame.x, frame.err
	lines := []string{"Wordling 5.2 | bounded native editing", normal}
	if err != nil {
		lines[1] = "Unsupported document presentation: " + err.Error()
	}
	if s.Reveal {
		banner := "Press Reveal Codes to restore screen"
		if frame.inspection.Partial {
			banner = "Partial Reveal inspection | HOST ?N = unsupported owner"
		}
		lines = append(lines, banner, reveal)
	}
	prompt := s.Prompt
	if m.characterRepeat.active {
		prompt = "Repeat Value = "
		if m.characterRepeat.count != 0 {
			prompt += string(rune('0' + m.characterRepeat.count))
		}
	} else if m.repeatEntry {
		prompt = "Repeat Value = "
	} else if s.Mode == editor.Editing && s.MacroRepeat == 3 {
		prompt = "Repeat Value = 3"
	}
	prompt = m.menuPrompt(s, prompt)
	lines = append(lines, prompt, s.Notice)
	if line := m.macroLine(); line != "" {
		lines = append(lines, line)
	}
	if s.Screen == 2 {
		lines = append(lines, "Doc2")
	}
	if line := firstStateLine(s); line != "" {
		lines = append(lines, line)
	}
	if s.Mode == editor.Waiting {
		lines = append(lines, "Filesystem operation pending")
	}
	if m.notice != "" {
		lines = append(lines, m.notice)
	}
	if m.reconcile != "" {
		lines = append(lines, m.reconcile)
	}
	history := "unknown"
	if s.HistoryCountKnown {
		history = fmt.Sprint(s.HistoryCount)
	}
	typing := ""
	if s.Typeover {
		typing = "Typeover "
	}
	profileLabel := "local-flat: ASCII basenames only; physical key delivery UNQUALIFIED"
	if m.runtime != nil && m.runtime.profile == MacroCOUT {
		profileLabel = "macro-cout: exact C:\\OUT\\ and flat names; physical key delivery UNQUALIFIED"
	}
	lines = append(lines, fmt.Sprintf("%s | %s | gap %d | %s", s.Mode, s.Name, s.Gap, s.SaveState),
		fmt.Sprintf("%sDirty=%t Block=%t revision=%d history=%s qualified=%t", typing, s.Dirty, s.Selection, s.Revision, history, s.HistoryQualified), profileLabel)
	if m.menuState().Visible {
		lines[0] = renderMenuBar()
	}
	for i := range lines {
		lines[i] = clip(lines[i], m.width)
	}
	if len(lines) > m.height {
		lines = lines[:m.height]
	}
	v := tea.NewView(strings.Join(lines, "\n"))
	v.AltScreen = true
	v.KeyboardEnhancements = tea.KeyboardEnhancements{ReportEventTypes: true, ReportAlternateKeys: true, ReportAllKeysAsEscapeCodes: true, ReportAssociatedText: true}
	if (s.Mode == editor.Editing || s.Mode == editor.DeleteQuestion || s.Mode == editor.HistoryPreview || s.Mode == editor.Waiting) && err == nil && m.height > 1 {
		y := 1
		if y < m.height {
			v.Cursor = tea.NewCursor(min(x, m.width-1), y)
		}
	}
	// Prompt caret refers to the original reducer draft, not document position.
	if (s.Mode == editor.RetrieveName || s.Mode == editor.SaveName || s.Mode == editor.MacroName) && len(lines) > 2 {
		prefix := "Document to be retrieved: "
		if s.Mode == editor.SaveName {
			prefix = "Document to be saved: "
		} else if s.Mode == editor.MacroName {
			prefix = "Macro: "
		}
		y := 2
		if s.Reveal {
			y = 4
		}
		if y < m.height {
			v.Cursor = tea.NewCursor(min(len(prefix)+s.Caret, m.width-1), y)
		}
	}
	if m.characterRepeat.active && m.height > 2 {
		v.Cursor = tea.NewCursor(min(len(prompt), m.width-1), 2)
	}
	if (m.repeatEntry || s.Mode == editor.Editing && s.MacroRepeat == 3) && m.height > 2 {
		x := len("Repeat Value = ")
		if !m.repeatEntry {
			x++
		}
		v.Cursor = tea.NewCursor(min(x, m.width-1), 2)
	}
	return v
}

// anchorEligible observes existing source/UI owners. It grants no command or IO
// authority. The pristine menu uses its qualified legacy base on BOTH toggles.
func (m Model) anchorEligible(s editor.Snapshot, frame macroPane) bool {
	if m.width != 80 || m.height != 25 || m.runtime == nil || frame.err != nil || frame.inspection.Partial ||
		s.Mode != editor.Editing || s.Selection || s.Typeover || !s.HistoryCountKnown || s.Screen != 1 || s.MacroRepeat != 1 ||
		m.characterRepeat.active || s.Pending != nil || s.Prompt != "" || s.Notice != "" || m.repeatEntry || m.notice != "" || m.reconcile != "" ||
		len(m.unsettled) != 0 || m.firstPending != nil || m.macro != nil || m.macroTicket != nil || m.macroCPU ||
		m.macroDeferred != nil || m.macroStatus.Active || m.macroStatus.WorkerPending || m.macroStatus.DisplaySuppressed ||
		s.NewPhase == editor.PublishedUnbound || s.NewPhase == editor.PublicationUncertain ||
		m.menuReason() == "" || m.menu.diagnostic != "" {
		return false
	}
	m.runtime.mu.Lock()
	defer m.runtime.mu.Unlock()
	return !m.runtime.stopping && m.runtime.macro == nil && m.uiAuthority != nil && m.uiAuthority == m.runtime.uiAuthority
}

// A fixed bounded display composition, not original page/ruler/status layout.
// Frozen frames retain this choice even when later live state/size is different.
func (m Model) anchorView(frame macroPane, reveal bool) tea.View {
	lines := make([]string, 25)
	lines[0] = revealBase + frame.normal
	if reveal {
		lines[13] = frame.reveal
		lines[24] = renderReveal([]revealRun{
			{text: "Press ", role: revealBase},
			{text: "Reveal Codes", role: revealCode},
			{text: " to restore screen" + strings.Repeat(" ", 44), role: revealBase},
		}, 0, 80)
	}
	for i := range lines {
		lines[i] = clip(lines[i], m.width)
	}
	lines = lines[:min(len(lines), m.height)]
	v := tea.NewView(strings.Join(lines, "\n"))
	v.AltScreen = true
	v.KeyboardEnhancements = tea.KeyboardEnhancements{ReportEventTypes: true, ReportAlternateKeys: true, ReportAllKeysAsEscapeCodes: true, ReportAssociatedText: true}
	v.Cursor = tea.NewCursor(min(frame.x, m.width-1), 0)
	return v
}
