package tui

import (
	"strings"

	tea "charm.land/bubbletea/v2"
	"github.com/lynncoleart/wordling/internal/document"
	"github.com/lynncoleart/wordling/internal/editor"
	"github.com/lynncoleart/wordling/internal/input"
)

// Value state carries observations, never a document or a reusable authority.
type characterRepeat struct {
	active          bool
	count           uint8
	native, genesis document.Revision
	revision        uint64
	screen          uint8
	gap             int
}

func (m Model) characterContext() bool {
	if m.runtime == nil || m.width != 80 || m.height != 25 || m.repeatEntry || m.notice != "" || m.reconcile != "" || m.menu.shown || m.menu.diagnostic != "" || len(m.unsettled) != 0 || m.firstPending != nil || m.macro != nil || m.macroTicket != nil || m.macroCPU || m.macroDeferred != nil || m.macroFrozen != nil || m.macroStatus != (MacroStatus{}) {
		return false
	}
	s := m.session.Snapshot()
	d := m.session.Document()
	if d == nil || !d.Birth().IsNew() || m.session.Source() != nil || d.TokenCount() != 0 || s.GenesisRevision == (document.Revision{}) || s.GenesisRevision != s.NativeRevision || s.Revision != 1 || s.Gap != 0 || s.Provenance != 0 || s.HomePrefix != 0 || !s.HistoryQualified || !s.HistoryCountKnown || s.HistoryCount != 0 {
		return false
	}
	if s.Screen != 1 || s.Mode != editor.Editing || s.NewPhase != editor.NewUnpublished || s.Typeover || s.Reveal || s.Selection || s.MacroRun != 0 || s.MacroRepeat != 1 {
		return false
	}
	if s.Pending != nil || s.Name != "" || s.Draft != "" || s.OriginalName != "" || s.Prompt != "" || s.Notice != "" || s.PublishedName != "" || s.SaveState != editor.NotSaved || s.PublishedRevision != (document.Revision{}) || s.SavedRevision != (document.Revision{}) || s.FirstPublication != (editor.Receipt{}) || s.Published != (editor.Receipt{}) || s.Last != (editor.Receipt{}) || s.Detached != (editor.Receipt{}) {
		return false
	}
	p := m.characterRepeat
	if p.active && (p.native != s.NativeRevision || p.genesis != s.GenesisRevision || p.revision != s.Revision || p.screen != s.Screen || p.gap != s.Gap) {
		return false
	}
	m.runtime.mu.Lock()
	defer m.runtime.mu.Unlock()
	return !m.runtime.stopping && m.uiAuthority != nil && m.uiAuthority == m.runtime.uiAuthority && m.runtime.macro == nil && m.runtime.issued == 0 && len(m.runtime.results) == 0 && len(m.runtime.executed) == 0 && len(m.runtime.firstTickets) == 0 && len(m.runtime.macroResults) == 0
}

func bareCharacterCommand(k input.Key, code rune) bool {
	return k.Code == code && k.Mod == 0 && k.Text == "" && !k.IsRepeat
}

func (m Model) beginCharacterRepeat(k input.Key) (Model, bool) {
	if !bareCharacterCommand(k, tea.KeyEscape) || !m.characterContext() {
		return m, false
	}
	s := m.session.Snapshot()
	m.characterRepeat = characterRepeat{active: true, native: s.NativeRevision, genesis: s.GenesisRevision, revision: s.Revision, screen: s.Screen, gap: s.Gap}
	return m, true
}

func (m Model) characterPress(k input.Key) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
	valid := m.characterContext()
	p := m.characterRepeat
	m.characterRepeat = characterRepeat{}
	if !valid {
		m.notice = "Character Repeat context unavailable (host policy)"
		return m, nil
	}
	if bareCharacterCommand(k, tea.KeyEscape) || bareCharacterCommand(k, tea.KeyF1) {
		m.notice = "Character Repeat cancelled (host policy)"
		return m, nil
	}
	if !k.IsRepeat && k.Mod&^(tea.ModShift|tea.ModCapsLock) == 0 && len(k.Text) == 1 && k.Code >= 0x21 && k.Code <= 0x7e {
		c := k.Text[0]
		if p.count == 0 && c >= '1' && c <= '9' {
			p.count = c - '0'
			m.characterRepeat = p
			return m, nil
		}
		if p.count >= 1 && p.count <= 9 && c >= 0x21 && c <= 0x7e && (c < '0' || c > '9') {
			d := m.session.Document()
			total := d.TokenCount()
			native, err := d.Export()
			if err == nil && total == 0 && total <= document.MaxMutationTokens && int(p.count) <= document.MaxMutationTokens-total && int(p.count) <= document.MaxMutationBytes && len(native) <= document.MaxMutationBytes-int(p.count) {
				return m.reduce(editor.Event{Command: editor.Text, Text: characterPayload(c, p.count)})
			}
		}
	}
	m.notice = "Unsupported character Repeat input (host policy)"
	return m, nil
}

// Called only after grammar, owner and downstream capacity checks.
func characterPayload(c byte, count uint8) string {
	return strings.Repeat(string([]byte{c}), int(count))
}

func (m *Model) invalidateCharacterMessage(msg tea.Msg) {
	if !m.characterRepeat.active {
		return
	}
	switch msg.(type) {
	case tea.EnvMsg, tea.ColorProfileMsg:
		return
	}
	switch input.Translate(msg).Kind {
	case input.Press, input.Release, input.KeyboardCapabilities, input.Capability, input.TerminalVersion:
		return
	}
	m.characterRepeat = characterRepeat{}
}

package tui

import (
	"strings"

	tea "charm.land/bubbletea/v2"
	"github.com/lynncoleart/wordling/internal/document"
	"github.com/lynncoleart/wordling/internal/editor"
	"github.com/lynncoleart/wordling/internal/input"
)

// MenuBar is copied HOST display diagnostic data, never command or native authority.
type MenuBar struct {
	Visible, Available bool
	Reason             string
}
type menuDisplay struct {
	shown      bool
	diagnostic string
}

const menuUnsupported = "Menu bar display only; Alt+= hides; action unsupported"

func menuKey(k input.Key) bool {
	return k.Code == '=' && k.Mod == tea.ModAlt && !k.IsRepeat && (k.Text == "" || k.Text == "=")
}

// Availability observes the real constructor genesis and existing IO/UI owners.
// It never claims a token, creates a document or changes reducer history.
func (m Model) menuReason() string {
	if m.characterRepeat.active {
		return "Menu bar unavailable: character Repeat"
	}
	if m.runtime == nil {
		return "Menu bar unavailable: uninitialized runtime"
	}
	if m.width != 80 || m.height != 25 {
		return "Menu bar unavailable: requires 80x25"
	}
	if m.macro != nil || m.macroTicket != nil || m.macroCPU || m.macroDeferred != nil || m.macroFrozen != nil || m.macroStatus.Active || m.macroStatus.WorkerPending || m.macroStatus.DisplaySuppressed {
		return "Menu bar unavailable: macro display or work"
	}
	if len(m.unsettled) != 0 || m.firstPending != nil {
		return "Menu bar unavailable: pending IO"
	}
	m.runtime.mu.Lock()
	idle := !m.runtime.stopping && m.uiAuthority != nil && m.runtime.uiAuthority == m.uiAuthority && m.runtime.macro == nil && m.runtime.issued == 0 && len(m.runtime.results) == 0 && len(m.runtime.firstTickets) == 0
	m.runtime.mu.Unlock()
	if !idle {
		return "Menu bar unavailable: stale, closed or busy runtime"
	}
	s := m.session.Snapshot()
	d := m.session.Document()
	if d == nil || m.session.Source() != nil || d.TokenCount() != 0 || s.GenesisRevision == (document.Revision{}) || s.GenesisRevision != s.NativeRevision || s.Revision != 1 || s.Gap != 0 || s.Provenance != 0 {
		return "Menu bar unavailable: requires pristine startup document"
	}
	if s.Screen != 1 || s.Mode != editor.Editing || s.NewPhase != editor.NewUnpublished || s.Typeover || s.Reveal || s.Selection || s.MacroRun != 0 || s.MacroRepeat != 1 || m.repeatEntry {
		return "Menu bar unavailable: requires idle Doc1 Insert"
	}
	if s.Pending != nil || s.Name != "" || s.Draft != "" || s.OriginalName != "" || s.Prompt != "" || s.Notice != "" || m.notice != "" || m.reconcile != "" || s.PublishedName != "" || s.SaveState != editor.NotSaved || s.PublishedRevision != (document.Revision{}) || s.SavedRevision != (document.Revision{}) || s.FirstPublication != (editor.Receipt{}) || s.Published != (editor.Receipt{}) || s.Last != (editor.Receipt{}) || s.Detached != (editor.Receipt{}) {
		return "Menu bar unavailable: application state"
	}
	return ""
}
func (m Model) menuState() MenuBar {
	reason := m.menuReason()
	return MenuBar{Visible: m.menu.shown && reason == "", Available: reason == "", Reason: reason}
}
func (m *Model) invalidateMenu() {
	if m.menu.shown && m.menuReason() != "" {
		m.menu = menuDisplay{}
	}
}
func (m Model) menuPress(k input.Key) (Model, bool) {
	state := m.menuState()
	if state.Visible {
		if menuKey(k) {
			m.menu = menuDisplay{}
		} else {
			m.menu.diagnostic = menuUnsupported
		}
		return m, true
	}
	if menuKey(k) {
		if state.Available {
			m.menu = menuDisplay{shown: true}
		} else {
			m.menu = menuDisplay{diagnostic: state.Reason}
		}
		return m, true
	}
	return m, false
}
func (m Model) menuPrompt(s editor.Snapshot, prompt string) string {
	if m.menu.diagnostic == "" || s.Mode != editor.Editing || s.Reveal || prompt != "" || s.Notice != "" || m.notice != "" || m.reconcile != "" || m.characterRepeat.active || m.repeatEntry || m.macroFrozen != nil || m.macroStatus.DisplaySuppressed {
		return prompt
	}
	return m.menu.diagnostic
}
func renderMenuBar() string {
	text := "File Edit Search Layout Mark Tools Font Graphics Help        (Press F3 for Help)"
	var out strings.Builder
	previous := 0
	for i, c := range text {
		role := 0x71
		if i < 4 {
			role = 0x47
		}
		switch i {
		case 5, 10, 17, 24, 29, 36, 40, 49:
			role = 0x74
		}
		if role != previous {
			switch role {
			case 0x47:
				out.WriteString("\x1b[22;37;41m")
			case 0x74:
				out.WriteString("\x1b[22;31;47m")
			case 0x71:
				out.WriteString("\x1b[22;34;47m")
			}
			previous = role
		}
		out.WriteRune(c)
	}
	out.WriteString("\x1b[0m")
	return out.String()
}

package tui

import (
	"fmt"

	tea "charm.land/bubbletea/v2"
	"github.com/lynncoleart/wordling/internal/editor"
)

// HostStopMacroMsg is explicit host containment, never a physical-key alias or
// qualification of original F1/CANCEL. Actual admitted work/IO is reconciled.
type HostStopMacroMsg struct{ Reason string }

type MacroStatus struct {
	State, Error                                       string
	Run                                                uint64
	Screen                                             uint8
	Source                                             [32]byte
	Steps, Words, AppliedWords                         uint64
	Active, WorkerPending, DisplaySuppressed, Terminal bool
}
type macroPane struct {
	anchors        bool // captured display geometry; never current native or command authority
	inspection     RevealInspection
	normal, reveal string
	x              int
	err            error
}

func (m Model) macroStart() (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
	f, t, cmd, err := m.runtime.startMacro(m.session)
	if err != nil {
		m.notice = err.Error()
		return m, nil
	}
	frame := m.visibleFrame()
	m.macroFrozen = &frame
	m.macro, m.macroTicket, m.macroCPU = f, t, true
	m.macroStatus = MacroStatus{State: "queued", Screen: m.session.Snapshot().Screen, Active: true, WorkerPending: true}
	if source := m.session.MacroSource(); source != nil {
		m.macroStatus.Source = source.Identity().SHA256
	}
	return m, cmd
}
func (m Model) macroQueue(kind string, result *editor.Result) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
	t, cmd, err := m.runtime.nextMacro(m.macro, m.macroTicket, m.session, kind, result)
	if err != nil {
		m.notice = err.Error()
		return m, nil
	}
	m.macroTicket = t
	m.macroCPU = true
	m.macroStatus.WorkerPending = true
	return m, cmd
}
func (m Model) macroReply(reply macroReply) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
	if m.macro != reply.flow || m.macroTicket != reply.ticket {
		m.notice = "Foreign or stale macro worker reply refused"
		return m, nil
	}
	o, ok := m.runtime.deliverMacro(reply.flow, reply.ticket)
	if !ok {
		m.notice = "Duplicate or stale macro worker delivery refused"
		return m, nil
	}
	m.macroCPU = false
	m.session = o.session
	p := o.progress
	m.macroStatus = MacroStatus{State: p.State, Run: p.Run, Screen: p.Screen, Source: p.Source, Steps: p.TotalSteps, Words: p.TotalWords, AppliedWords: p.AppliedWords, Active: !p.Terminal, DisplaySuppressed: p.DisplaySuppressed, Terminal: p.Terminal}
	m.notice = ""
	if o.err != nil {
		m.notice = o.err.Error()
	} else if p.Machine.Err != nil {
		m.notice = p.Machine.Err.Error()
	}
	m.macroStatus.Error = m.notice
	if o.consumed != nil {
		m = m.settle(*o.consumed)
	}
	if p.Terminal {
		m.runtime.releaseMacro(m.macro)
		m.macro = nil
		m.macroTicket = nil
		m.macroFrozen = nil
		if m.macroDeferred != nil {
			p := m.macroDeferred
			m.macroDeferred = nil
			return m.macroIO(p)
		}
		return m, nil
	}
	if o.intent != nil {
		i := o.intent
		m.unsettled = append(append([]operation(nil), m.unsettled...), operation{i.Operation, i.Revision, i.Kind})
		return m, m.runtime.command(*i)
	}
	if p.Machine.NeedProgram != nil {
		if m.macroDeferred != nil {
			p := m.macroDeferred
			m.macroDeferred = nil
			return m.macroIO(p)
		}
		return m, nil
	}
	return m.macroQueue("step", nil)
}
func (m Model) settle(key operation) Model {
	remaining := make([]operation, 0, len(m.unsettled))
	for _, op := range m.unsettled {
		if op != key {
			remaining = append(remaining, op)
		}
	}
	m.unsettled = remaining
	return m
}
func (m Model) macroRead(result editor.Result) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
	pending := m.session.Snapshot().Pending
	attached := pending != nil && pending.Kind == editor.ReadMacro && pending.Operation == result.Operation && pending.Revision == result.Revision
	if m.macro != nil {
		if !attached || m.macroCPU {
			m.notice = "Foreign, duplicate or premature nested macro result refused"
			return m, nil
		}
		return m.macroQueue("resume", &result)
	}
	next, intent, err := m.session.Reduce(editor.Event{Command: editor.Complete, Result: &result})
	m.session = next
	m.notice = ""
	if err != nil {
		m.notice = err.Error()
	}
	// Invalid private acquisition must not consume the authentic pending read.
	if next.Snapshot().Pending == nil {
		m = m.settle(operation{result.Operation, result.Revision, result.Kind})
	}
	if intent != nil {
		m.notice = "Unexpected macro completion intent refused"
		return m, nil
	}
	if err == nil && attached && next.Snapshot().Mode == editor.MacroReady {
		return m.macroStart()
	}
	return m, nil
}
func (m Model) macroReadRefused(reply macroReadRefusedMsg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
	i := reply.request
	p := m.session.Snapshot().Pending
	if p == nil || p.Kind != editor.ReadMacro || p.Operation != i.Operation || p.Revision != i.Revision {
		m.notice = "Detached macro read was not started: " + reply.err.Error()
		return m, nil
	}
	m = m.settle(operation{i.Operation, i.Revision, i.Kind})
	if m.macro != nil {
		m.runtime.cancelMacro(m.macro)
		if !m.macroCPU {
			return m.macroQueue("stop", nil)
		}
		m.notice = "Host macro read refused; waiting for admitted worker"
		return m, nil
	}
	next, _, err := m.session.Reduce(editor.Event{Command: editor.CancelOperation})
	m.session = next
	m.notice = "Host macro read not started: " + reply.err.Error()
	if err != nil {
		m.notice += "; " + err.Error()
	}
	return m, nil
}
func (m Model) hostStopMacro(reason string) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
	if m.macro == nil {
		p := m.session.Snapshot().Pending
		if p != nil && p.Kind == editor.ReadMacro {
			return m.reduce(editor.Event{Command: editor.CancelOperation})
		}
		m.notice = "No active macro for host containment"
		return m, nil
	}
	m.runtime.cancelMacro(m.macro)
	m.notice = "Host containment requested; actual partial effects and pending IO retained: " + reason
	if m.macroCPU {
		return m, nil
	}
	return m.macroQueue("stop", nil)
}
func (m Model) macroKey(kcode rune, kmod tea.KeyMod, text string) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd, bool) {
	if active := m.runtime.activeMacro(); active != nil && m.macro != active {
		m.notice = "Another model owns the active macro; stale command refused"
		return m, nil, true
	}
	if m.macro != nil {
		if kcode == tea.KeyF1 && kmod == 0 {
			m.notice = "Original F1 stop is unsupported by this macro adapter; no cancellation performed"
		} else {
			m.notice = "Macro execution owns command delivery; manual command refused"
		}
		return m, nil, true
	}
	s := m.session.Snapshot()
	if kcode == tea.KeyF10 && kmod == tea.ModAlt && s.Mode == editor.Editing {
		if d := m.session.Document(); d != nil && d.Birth().IsNew() && !m.newMacroHostIdle() {
			if m.notice == "" {
				m.notice = "New macro requires untouched current host context"
			}
			return m, nil, true
		}
	}
	if kcode == tea.KeyF10 && kmod == tea.ModAlt && s.Mode == editor.Editing && (s.Typeover || s.Reveal) {
		m.notice = "Macro invocation in this view/mode is unqualified"
		return m, nil, true
	}
	if m.repeatEntry {
		if text == "3" && kmod&^(tea.ModShift|tea.ModCapsLock) == 0 {
			next, err := m.session.WithMacroRepeat(3)
			if err != nil {
				m.notice = err.Error()
			} else {
				m.session = next
				m.repeatEntry = false
				m.notice = ""
			}
		} else {
			m.notice = "Only the observed Repeat3 then Alt-F10 sequence is qualified"
		}
		return m, nil, true
	}
	if kcode == tea.KeyEscape && kmod == 0 && s.Mode == editor.Editing && m.session.Document() != nil && s.Pending == nil && len(m.unsettled) == 0 && s.MacroRepeat == 1 && !s.Typeover && !s.Reveal {
		candidate, err := m.session.WithMacroRepeat(3)
		if err == nil {
			_, _, err = candidate.Reduce(editor.Event{Command: editor.Macro})
		}
		if err != nil {
			m.notice = err.Error()
		} else {
			m.repeatEntry = true
			m.notice = ""
		}
		return m, nil, true
	}
	if s.Mode == editor.MacroName && s.MacroRepeat == 3 && kcode == tea.KeyF1 && kmod == 0 {
		m.notice = "Cancellation of a Repeat3 macro-name context is unqualified; no reset guessed"
		return m, nil, true
	}
	return m, nil, false
}

// G authenticates the genuine constructor target before MacroName. This guard
// adds only current HOST orchestration facts, once at initial New invocation;
// it is never reapplied to filename, read, worker or effect continuations.
func (m Model) newMacroHostIdle() bool {
	if m.runtime == nil || m.characterRepeat.active || m.repeatEntry || m.notice != "" || m.reconcile != "" || len(m.unsettled) != 0 || m.firstPending != nil ||
		m.macro != nil || m.macroTicket != nil || m.macroCPU || m.macroDeferred != nil || m.macroFrozen != nil || m.macroStatus != (MacroStatus{}) {
		return false
	}
	m.runtime.mu.Lock()
	defer m.runtime.mu.Unlock()
	return !m.runtime.stopping && m.uiAuthority != nil && m.runtime.uiAuthority == m.uiAuthority && m.runtime.macro == nil &&
		m.runtime.issued == 0 && len(m.runtime.results) == 0 && len(m.runtime.executed) == 0 && len(m.runtime.firstTickets) == 0 && len(m.runtime.macroResults) == 0
}

func (m Model) macroLine() string {
	p := m.macroStatus
	if p.State == "" {
		return ""
	}
	return fmt.Sprintf("Macro %s | Doc%d | %d characters", p.State, p.Screen, p.AppliedWords)
}

func (m Model) macroIO(p *macroIOReceipt) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {
	if !m.runtime.freshMacroIO(p) {
		m.notice = "Foreign or duplicate macro IO delivery refused"
		return m, nil
	}
	found := false
	for _, op := range m.unsettled {
		if op == p.key {
			found = true
		}
	}
	if !found {
		m.notice = "Macro IO receipt has no current or detached operation"
		return m, nil
	}
	if m.macroCPU {
		if m.macroDeferred == nil {
			m.macroDeferred = p
		} else if m.macroDeferred != p {
			m.notice = "Additional macro receipt refused while actual worker is pending"
		}
		return m, nil
	}
	result, ok := m.runtime.deliverMacroIO(p)
	if !ok {
		m.notice = "Duplicate macro IO receipt refused"
		return m, nil
	}
	return m.macroRead(result)
}


=== ACTUAL_APPROVED_H73_NORMAL_EVENT01M6CYVMQKVCA2Y5SY7WPYNRE9 5bd5105e9014a839ea95c5b2efd21548ccd8756b:internal/tui/ascii_viewport.go ===
package tui

import (
	"strings"

	"github.com/lynncoleart/wordling/internal/document"
	"github.com/lynncoleart/wordling/internal/editor"
)

// A single copied display fact, not native authority or a payload cache. The
// opaque revision is the authentic owner key; equal bytes do not compare equal.
// Invalidating the slot releases its one previous revision. Persistent Model
// values still own their respective immutable observations.
type asciiQualification struct {
	revision       document.Revision
	total          int
	checked, whole bool
}

const maxASCIIViewportOwners = 16384

func (m Model) ordinaryViewEligible(s editor.Snapshot) bool {
	if m.runtime == nil || s.Mode != editor.Editing || s.Selection || s.Typeover || !s.HistoryCountKnown || s.Screen != 1 || s.MacroRepeat != 1 ||
		s.Pending != nil || s.Prompt != "" || s.Notice != "" || m.characterRepeat.active || m.repeatEntry || m.notice != "" || m.reconcile != "" ||
		len(m.unsettled) != 0 || m.firstPending != nil || m.macro != nil || m.macroTicket != nil || m.macroCPU || m.macroFrozen != nil ||
		m.macroDeferred != nil || m.macroStatus.Active || m.macroStatus.WorkerPending || m.macroStatus.DisplaySuppressed ||
		s.NewPhase == editor.PublishedUnbound || s.NewPhase == editor.PublicationUncertain || m.menuReason() == "" || m.menu.diagnostic != "" {
		return false
	}
	m.runtime.mu.Lock()
	defer m.runtime.mu.Unlock()
	return !m.runtime.stopping && m.runtime.macro == nil && m.uiAuthority != nil && m.uiAuthority == m.runtime.uiAuthority
}

// Only the returned authentic Update chain prepares the fact, after dispatch.
// View/State and stale Update returns never enter this path.
func (m *Model) prepareASCIIViewport() {
	s := m.session.Snapshot()
	if !m.ordinaryViewEligible(s) {
		m.ascii = asciiQualification{}
		return
	}
	d := m.session.Document()
	if d == nil || d.TokenCount() <= document.MaxWindowTokens {
		m.ascii = asciiQualification{}
		return
	}
	if m.ascii.checked && m.ascii.revision == s.NativeRevision {
		return
	}
	m.ascii = qualifyASCII(d, s.NativeRevision)
}
func qualifyASCII(d *document.Document, revision document.Revision) asciiQualification {
	total := d.TokenCount()
	fact := asciiQualification{revision: revision, total: total, checked: true}
	// Preflight total before requesting any catalogue allocation. This is only a
	// HOST view limit, never a native open/edit limit.
	if total <= document.MaxWindowTokens || total > maxASCIIViewportOwners {
		return fact
	}
	rawBytes, textBytes := 0, 0
	previousEnd := -1
	for start := 0; start < total; start += document.MaxWindowTokens {
		count := min(document.MaxWindowTokens, total-start)
		c, err := document.InspectRevealCatalogue(d, revision, start, count)
		if err != nil || c.Revision != revision || c.StartGap != start || c.EndGap != start+count || c.TotalTokens != total || c.TrueEOF != (start+count == total) || len(c.Elements) != count {
			return fact
		}
		for i, e := range c.Elements {
			if e.TokenIndex != start+i || e.Kind != document.ASCII || e.Span.Length != 1 || len(e.Text) != 1 || e.Text[0] < 32 || e.Text[0] > 126 || e.Span.Offset < 0 || (previousEnd >= 0 && e.Span.Offset != previousEnd) {
				return fact
			}
			rawBytes += e.Span.Length
			textBytes += len(e.Text)
			previousEnd = e.Span.Offset + e.Span.Length
			if rawBytes > 1<<20 || textBytes > 128<<10 {
				return fact
			}
		}
	}
	fact.whole = true
	return fact
}
func (m Model) asciiViewportFrame(s editor.Snapshot) (macroPane, bool) {
	f := m.ascii
	if !f.checked || !f.whole || f.revision != s.NativeRevision || !m.ordinaryViewEligible(s) {
		return macroPane{}, false
	}
	start := max(0, s.Gap-m.width/2)
	count := min(m.width, f.total-start)
	if start < 0 || start > s.Gap || s.Gap > f.total || count < 0 || count > document.MaxWindowTokens || (s.Gap < f.total && s.Gap >= start+count) {
		return macroPane{}, false
	}
	p, err := m.session.Present(start, count)
	if err != nil {
		return macroPane{err: err}, true
	}
	var normal strings.Builder
	runs := make([]revealRun, 0, len(p.Elements)+1)
	for _, e := range p.Elements {
		normal.WriteString(e.Text)
		role := revealBase
		if e.TokenIndex == p.CursorGap && e.Span.Length > 0 {
			role = revealActive
		}
		runs = append(runs, revealRun{text: e.Text, role: role})
	}
	if p.CursorGap == f.total {
		runs = append(runs, revealRun{text: " ", role: revealActive})
	}
	// Already an absolute owner window: render from local zero, never scroll again.
	return macroPane{normal: normal.String() + "\x1b[0m", reveal: renderReveal(runs, 0, m.width), x: s.Gap - start, anchors: m.width == 80 && m.height == 25}, true
}


=== ACTUAL_APPROVED_H73_NORMAL_EVENT01M6CYVMQKVCA2Y5SY7WPYNRE9 5bd5105e9014a839ea95c5b2efd21548ccd8756b:internal/tui/reveal_inspection.go ===
package tui

import (
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"strconv"

	"github.com/lynncoleart/wordling/internal/document"
	"github.com/lynncoleart/wordling/internal/editor"
)

// MaxInspectionRenderBytes bounds expanded HOST markers, independently of E's
// native/text limits. This is host policy, not an original WP resource metric.
const MaxInspectionRenderBytes = 64 << 10

// RevealInspection is copied diagnostic data for the VISIBLE partial pane.
// It grants no native presentation, movement, mutation or IO authority.
type RevealInspection struct {
	Available, Partial            bool
	Revision                      document.Revision
	StartGap, EndGap, TotalTokens int
	TrueEOF                       bool
	Elements                      []document.Element
}

func copyInspection(x RevealInspection) RevealInspection {
	x.Elements = append([]document.Element(nil), x.Elements...)
	return x
}

// Merely reading State/View must not claim or advance UI authority.
func (m Model) inspectionEligible(s editor.Snapshot) bool {
	if m.runtime == nil || s.Mode != editor.Editing || !s.Reveal || s.Selection || s.Pending != nil ||
		m.characterRepeat.active || m.repeatEntry || m.notice != "" || m.reconcile != "" || len(m.unsettled) != 0 || m.firstPending != nil ||
		m.macro != nil || m.macroTicket != nil || m.macroCPU || m.macroDeferred != nil ||
		m.macroStatus.Active || m.macroStatus.WorkerPending || m.macroStatus.DisplaySuppressed {
		return false
	}
	m.runtime.mu.Lock()
	defer m.runtime.mu.Unlock()
	return !m.runtime.stopping && m.uiAuthority != nil && m.runtime.uiAuthority == m.uiAuthority
}

func (m Model) visibleFrame() macroPane {
	// Select frozen pixels AND diagnostics before inspecting any live native data.
	if m.macroStatus.DisplaySuppressed && m.macroFrozen != nil {
		frame := *m.macroFrozen
		if !m.session.Snapshot().Reveal {
			frame.inspection = RevealInspection{}
		}
		return frame
	}
	s := m.session.Snapshot()
	if frame, ok := m.asciiViewportFrame(s); ok {
		return frame
	}
	var frame macroPane
	if m.inspectionEligible(s) {
		frame = m.liveInspectionFrame(s)
	} else {
		frame = m.legacyFrame()
	}
	frame.anchors = m.anchorEligible(s, frame)
	return frame
}

// State is an observation, not an implicit normal/virtual Present request.
func (m Model) inspectionState() RevealInspection {
	if m.macroStatus.DisplaySuppressed && m.macroFrozen != nil {
		if !m.session.Snapshot().Reveal {
			return RevealInspection{}
		}
		return copyInspection(m.macroFrozen.inspection)
	}
	s := m.session.Snapshot()
	if m.ascii.checked && m.ascii.whole && m.ascii.revision == s.NativeRevision && m.ordinaryViewEligible(s) {
		return RevealInspection{}
	}
	if !m.inspectionEligible(s) {
		return RevealInspection{}
	}
	return copyInspection(m.liveInspectionFrame(s).inspection)
}
func (m Model) liveInspectionFrame(s editor.Snapshot) macroPane {
	session := m.session
	d := session.Document()
	if d == nil {
		return m.legacyFrame()
	}
	count := min(d.TokenCount(), document.MaxWindowTokens)
	catalogue, err := document.InspectRevealCatalogue(d, s.NativeRevision, 0, count)
	if err != nil {
		return macroPane{err: err}
	}
	// E authenticates/bounds the SAME captured session/window before Present's
	// contiguous raw copy. The session is immutable; no caller wrapper is reused.
	frame := m.legacyFrame()
	var failure *document.Failure
	if !errors.As(frame.err, &failure) || failure.Category != document.UnsupportedToken {
		return frame
	}
	lower, err := inspectionRuns(catalogue)
	if err != nil {
		return macroPane{err: err}
	}
	frame.reveal = renderReveal(lower, 0, m.width)
	frame.inspection = RevealInspection{Available: true, Partial: true, Revision: catalogue.Revision,
		StartGap: catalogue.StartGap, EndGap: catalogue.EndGap, TotalTokens: catalogue.TotalTokens,
		TrueEOF: catalogue.TrueEOF, Elements: catalogue.Elements}
	return frame
}
func (m Model) legacyFrame() macroPane {
	normal, reveal, x, err := m.legacyPanes()
	return macroPane{normal: normal, reveal: reveal, x: x, err: err}
}

// Preflight lengths BEFORE run/marker/result construction. Elements come only
// from the approved E API; H neither reads native bytes nor reclassifies owners.
func inspectionRuns(c document.CatalogueInspection) ([]revealRun, error) {
	if len(c.Elements) > document.MaxWindowTokens {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("inspection render owner limit")
	}
	size := 0
	for _, e := range c.Elements {
		n := len(e.Text)
		if e.Kind == document.Opaque && e.Text == "" {
			if e.TokenIndex < 0 {
				return nil, fmt.Errorf("inspection invalid owner ordinal")
			}
			n = 2
			for value := e.TokenIndex; value >= 10; value /= 10 {
				n++
			}
		}
		if n > MaxInspectionRenderBytes-size {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("inspection expanded render limit")
		}
		size += n
	}
	runs := make([]revealRun, 0, len(c.Elements))
	for _, e := range c.Elements {
		text, role := e.Text, revealBase
		if e.Kind == document.Opaque && text == "" {
			text = "?" + strconv.Itoa(e.TokenIndex)
		} else if e.Kind != document.ASCII {
			role = revealCode
		}
		runs = append(runs, revealRun{text: text, role: role})
	}
	return runs, nil
}


=== ACTUAL_APPROVED_H73_NORMAL_EVENT01M6CYVMQKVCA2Y5SY7WPYNRE9 5bd5105e9014a839ea95c5b2efd21548ccd8756b:internal/tui/README.md ===
# Bounded executable native editing composition (WP18/WP28)

Run `go run ./cmd/wordling -root /absolute/local/directory -profile local-flat`
from a terminal. The operator chooses a validated local filesystem; the app does
not certify a network mount or interpret DOS drives. Only flat ASCII basenames
are supported. Shift+F10 retrieves, Alt+F3 toggles Reveal Codes, F10 invokes the
approved snapshot or native-candidate save workflow, and F7 invokes the original bounded exit prompts.
No global q/Ctrl+Q/Ctrl+S/Escape aliases exist. F1/Escape retain their observed
context-specific reducer meaning. Missing/unobserved branches refuse explicitly.

`Open(Config{Root, Profile: LocalFlat})` returns a directory-owning Runtime and
value Model. Model implements the pinned Bubble Tea v2.1.1 interface. Update
consumes input.Translate and calls the actual editor.Session.Reduce; View consumes
shared document.Presentation token positions. State() and Session() expose
immutable inspection for tests/measurement. Keep one authoritative model chain;
do not execute intents from forked old values. No terminal code serializes native
bytes or decides original page geometry. The normal pane styles supported Bold;
the Reveal pane shows the same tokens and gap. The presentation has a4096-token
safety window and horizontally clips terminal cells; it does not paginate.
Cursor beyond that window and opaque-token windows explicitly refuse presentation
while preserving the complete native snapshot for no-edit storage.

Production uses only the empty rooted prefix. Text Shift/CapsLock modifiers may
supply uppercase filenames; command modifiers remain exact. Paste, releases,
repeats, unknown messages and unsupported keys never replay editing commands.
Unknown/paste states are visible. Resize changes bounded terminal dimensions
(1..4096 cells per axis), never the document. Physical delivery remains UNQUALIFIED
in State and the application view; requesting keyboard enhancements is separate
from actual terminal capability or physical evidence. No original Pos inches,
line/page metrics, DOS raster, help/menu engine or unsupported writer is invented.
Original prompt wording and unmodified/Reveal status cues are preserved; their
row/spacing arrangement follows this bounded Linux presentation, not an accepted
pixel/whole-screen compatibility claim.

Each tea.Cmd captures one complete immutable editor.Intent. Runtime calls the
actual Directory.Read/Observe/Save/PreparePublication/Publish and returns the exact operation/revision/kind.
The reducer validates document/directory/name/native fingerprint and source
identity. Observe and Save are separate operations, never a claimed atomic pair.
Same-source explicit Yes can yield SameFileNoOp; a new name yields
NewNamePublished only after real verified IO. Existing different inodes always
refuse replacement, even for identical bytes. Source is not rebound after Save As.

CancelPendingMsg is an explicitly programmatic host detach, with no physical key
alias or claim of disk cancellation. Further keys are held until its actual late
receipt arrives; detached publication requires filesystem reconciliation and
never becomes current success. Typed cleanup/uncertain failures retain candidate
and destination-may-exist details, block further commands, preserve source/native
state, and never auto-exit. No speculative rollback/unlink/recovery action occurs.
The owner must inspect filesystem state after abnormal termination; there is no
fake acknowledgement button claiming recovery.

Runtime.Close gates new operations under the same lock as worker registration,
drains every started call, then closes the pinned directory. Queued commands
executed afterward refuse before touching descriptors. Each operation envelope
can execute only once. A duplicate dispatch returns a distinct non-completion
notice, checked before shutdown/capacity; it cannot settle the admitted operation
or consume its pending receipt. A first dispatch rejected before admission by
shutdown/capacity returns a definitive failure because no worker owns that
operation. A1024-operation host safety cap bounds receipt/identity
history (not an original limit). Completed() returns copied outcome/error metadata
without retaining loaded native images/destination handles. The actual command
checks terminal restoration, waits for worker drain, checks receipts/residue, and
reports host signals as abnormal rather than original successful exit. SIGKILL,
power-loss durability and automatic startup recovery remain unqualified. Runtime
Close does not promise a time bound on a blocked external filesystem.

Tests use real temporary directories and actual executable PTYs; injectable
completion errors explicitly test the host result boundary, not filesystem fault
claims. Approved storage tests cover lower-level faults. No native framing or
semantic writer is added, so no new native-parser fuzz acceptance is claimed.
Evidence, exact original-capture associations and reproduction are in
`docs/research/continuation/composed-session/README.md`.

## Native editing continuation (WP28)

Printable ASCII including q is document content in Editing/Waiting; q does not
quit. Ctrl+Q/Ctrl+S remain unsupported. Insert toggles typeover, Delete/Backspace
retain direction, Alt+F4 starts the approved forward Reveal block, and selected
Backspace asks the exact block question. Selected forward Delete refuses. Y/N,
F1 and Escape resolve only in the reducer's exact question contexts. Undelete
uses F1, numeric2 Previous, numeric1 Restore and preview F1 cancel. Selection and
preview [Block] display markers are never native bytes. HistoryCountKnown=false
renders as unknown, with the qualified flag separate. Normal/Reveal/preview use
the same immutable revision/gap and real Present/Preview data. The inherited
normal-pane hardware caret remains a terminal text position; the displayed gap
is the semantic authority, including positions adjacent to zero-width codes.
No original physical Pos, screen raster or printed layout is inferred.

Runtime captures independent Directory/Document/Destination wrappers and candidate
bytes before tea.Cmd dispatch. Directory value equality compares its one private
opened capability; copied wrappers are accepted, independent opens of the same
inode are refused. Close still closes the shared capability. PublishCandidate
uses the actual loaded-source PreparePublication followed by Publish with the
exact Operation/Revision PublicationID, never a snapshot Save shortcut or a new
publication constructor. The source guard stays the first loaded source after
later displayed Save As names. No-edit crossdirectory storage Save remains
supported; edited candidate cross-capability requests retain the storage refusal.
Production local-flat exposes one opened root only, not a crossdirectory dialog.

Pending candidate writes permit only reducer-admitted text/mode/navigation;
newer edits keep the old immutable candidate and cannot be cleaned or renamed by
its later result. A successful older publication is visible as a detached receipt.
Host detach is a separate programmatic message; keys wait for its actual receipt,
and detached publication needs reconciliation. Generic host errors paired with
Published retain destination-may-exist uncertainty, just like typed storage failures;
no current success or quit is manufactured. A successful older attached publication
is a known destination effect, not an atomic cancellation or a save of newer edits.

At the WP28 checkpoint, only loaded structurally admitted originals could mutate. Prefix564, unsupported
characters/opaque structures, more than two sequential qualified Bold pairs,
EC045 intermediate/unbrowsed history and wider unqualified lifecycles retain
component refusals. Normal block selection, reversed/partial text blocks, ASCII
Backspace grouping, original repeats, F6 creation and actual overwrite remain
unimplemented. Preserved history fragments do not establish hidden counts or
new interleavings. WP37 subsequently adds the reviewed macro host, and WP48 below adds startup New through the approved constructor. These additions do not widen the remaining editing/history limits.

Reproduction and exact source/native/synthetic-PTY/typed-failure distinctions are
in docs/research/continuation/editable-session/. WP29 must independently exercise
the emitted app files with the original application; this package adds no VM,
original printer/resource qualification or full canonical scenario/FR/M1 claim.

## Startup native creation (WP48)

Fresh command startup now uses the one approved closed historical native profile
through the actual editor constructor. No filename/retrieve is required before
admitted ASCII typing or true empty F10 Save. The existing `-profile local-flat`
and explicit `macro-cout` names still select path namespaces; native serialization
selection is separate and fixed internally. It supplies no fonts/printer runtime
resources or physical layout guarantee. Missing NativeProfile in the existing Go
Config defaults to this same sole profile; an unknown selection refuses.

First Save schedules genuine editor47 PublishNewBuffer, then separately schedules
same-capability BindNewSource Read. Private runtime tickets deduplicate the opaque
issued RequestKey, rather than Operation/Revision counters, and retain37's single
UI-transition authority. The reducer alone commits the source/name/saved revision.
No reducer/Update native IO, template, dummy loaded document or shadow editor is
used. Live New birth/NoLoadedOriginal remains after binding; later SaveAs keeps
the first source guard while display/last-saved receipt may change.

During waiting, Save is not confirmed. If a file was published but its Read could
not verify the original receipt, visible F10 retries verification ONLY against
that same captured capability/receipt. This contextual F10 action is a Linux
safety adaptation, not newly observed original behavior. It never republishes or
opens another root. Publication uncertainty refuses F10/retry/normal successful
exit and preserves data/receipts. Host shutdown remains possible and reports an
unresolved outcome. Programmatic CancelPendingMsg detaches real IO without
cancelling disk effects. Close drains started workers, then closes the shared
capability; later queued commands do no IO and cannot claim clean success.

This route is startup-only. F7 clear still does not make a new document or reset
history; new-target macros and second-screen New remain refused. Existing loaded
macro37, loaded-source publication41, history36/26, unknown count/EC045 and other
unqualified operations retain their guards. Full M1 remains open. Actual executable
PTY/native, public Runtime failures and explicitly instrumented TEST-only storage
ports are separated in docs/research/continuation/new-session/README.md.

WP52 adds an active native-token range in the existing lower Reveal row: one
ASCII cell, all six cells of an actual Bold label, or one display-only blank at
actual EOF. Literal bracket text stays ASCII. The source is the same immutable
Present token index/span and cursor gap as the normal pane; the upper caret still
uses normal text width. The selected original VGA roles map to explicit Linux
ANSI white/blue, bright white/blue and white/red, with resets through clipping.
Physical colors and complete pane geometry remain unqualified. Selection/history
virtual overlays retain their existing markers without claiming native active
spans. Macro DISPLAY OFF retains the already captured styled pane/caret. See
[evidence/wp52/README.md](evidence/wp52/README.md) for original and live-app proof.

## Bounded display anchors (WP69)

At exactly80x25, a current healthy ordinary Insert view places its actual native
text and text-width caret at row0. Reveal places that same document's styled
code stream at row13 and the36-character restore-screen footer plus44 spaces at
row24. Footer logical VGA roles17/1F/17 map through the existing Linux ANSI
profile; physical palette/raster/key delivery and full-screen equality remain
unqualified. Rows11/12 remain neutral HOST omission, not a fabricated ruler or
Pg/Ln/Pos calculation. Existing4096-token and horizontal clipping limits remain.
Implementation diagnostics remain available through State/Session.

This small layout admits no selection, history preview, active Typeover, modal
prompt, genuine notice/error, source-stale UI, partial unsupported inspection,
active/pending/deferred macro or IO, or unresolved publication. Actual unknown
history count is genuine uncertainty: HistoryCountKnown=false retains the legacy
visible history=unknown fallback without changing history knowledge. Other sizes
retain the bounded legacy display. The supported seven-gap loaded native stream
and genuine first ordinary ASCII edit remain reachable anchor positives.

The authentic menu-capable pristine startup context retains its SAME legacy base
whether the menu is hidden or shown. Alt+= overlays only existing row0, preserving
body/caret and full hide restoration. After a real first edit or Retrieve leaves
that pristine context, healthy Insert may use anchors; no state is reset to gain
eligibility. Typeover keeps its visible legacy indicator until actual Insert
returns. Frozen DISPLAY OFF captures source, styles, inspection, caret AND layout
before consulting live state; resize clips that captured layout rather than
choosing geometry from a newer gap. Partial HR/HP/unknown inspection remains the
existing inactive HOST view with upper unsupported error and no native caret.
This adds no page layout, ruler, general scrolling, native API, writer or IO.

## Whole-ASCII follow-cursor view (WP71)

Current healthy Doc1 Insert revisions with4097..16384 owners may use one bounded
HOST horizontal window. After an authentic Update dispatch, at most four existing
E catalogues authenticate the complete immutable revision in contiguous chunks
of4096. Each owner must be one printable ASCII byte with its complete one-byte
native span. This rejects an offscreen code as well as an onscreen one. E checks
owner/raw bounds before copying; its independent text ceiling is checked during
construction. The closed mapping is at most24576 bytes per4096-owner chunk.
Totals16385+ are refused before catalogue allocation; this is a presentation
ceiling, not a native document/open/edit limit. Short sources keep the old path.

One private constant-size copied positive/negative fact is bound to the opaque
native revision. Only the authentic returned Update chain prepares it; View,
State and Session observations never install it. An unchanged eligible revision
reuses its fact across Left/Right/Reveal/resize. New revision, acquisition or
leaving eligibility invalidates it; equal-byte independent acquisitions have
different keys. No source payload, rendered strings, command/IO permit or global
cache is retained. Stale Models still fail their normal source/UI gates.

The window starts at max(0,gap-width/2) and contains min(width,total-start) actual
owners, up to width4096. Both panes use that same Session.Present window and
absolute native token indices; rendering starts at local zero. The real active
owner remains included at width1; only trueEOF gets a display-only active blank.
At exactly80x25, WP69's row0/13/24 anchors and complete footer remain. Other sizes
retain existing legacy composition. This is a HOST policy, not observation of
original WP long-line thresholds, hysteresis or general scrolling/page layout.

All old menu, Typeover, partial inspection, error, source, IO, macro and uncertain
history exclusions remain. Actual clear/retrieve and Doc1/Doc2 switching can make
history unknown; those outcomes deliberately keep visible history=unknown instead
of claiming requalification. Frozen DISPLAY OFF is selected before live facts or
windows. Source/native bytes, reducer/history and save behavior are unchanged.
Prospective classifier/warm allocation limits and actual bounded measurements,
public Model tests, unmodified CLI PTYs and failures are recorded in
[evidence/ascii-viewport](evidence/ascii-viewport/).

Character Repeat is a bounded host composition for untouched constructor New
Doc1 at 80x25: Escape, one explicit digit 1–9, then one nonnumeric printable
ASCII key inserts one atomic Text payload. Escape/F1 cancel; other unsupported
pending input consumes that event and cancels. The next ordinary key has no
inherited count. The legacy row-2 count prompt is host UI. Loaded macro Repeat
remains separate. Only final `xxxxx` has original CMD51-005 screen witnesses;
other counts, cancellation, native conservation and history are host contracts.
General Repeat and full M1 parity remain open.

=== IMMUTABLE_MISSION_AUTHORITY_CONTEXT_NOT_CURRENT_SOURCE_GUARD 066ab94fc3e0340f3881bb80977d3c93c17c149a:kitty-specs/wp51-parity-01M64CVY/spec.md ===
# Mission Specification: WordPerfect 5.1 parity

**Mission Branch**: `feat/wp51-parity`  
**Created**: 2026-10-29  
**Status**: Scope confirmed; discovery checkpoint recorded; detailed parity research remains open
**Mission**: `wp51-parity-01M64CVY` (`01M64CVYYRTDKBCYZ56H6XNTEQ`)  
**Input**: Lynn confirmed two missions: complete WP 5.1 DOS parity first, then
modern conveniences including PDF; original file formats remain unchanged.

## Confirmed intent and boundaries

For writers and original WP 5.1 users, opening or creating a document on Linux
must provide the original application's commands, formatting semantics,
automation, files, and printed results. Reveal Codes means the original editing
function, not merely a visual representation of formatting. Native documents and
companion artifacts must remain interoperable with the original application.

This is M1 of the [two-mission charter](../../.kittify/charter/charter.md).
PDF and newly selected modern conveniences belong to M2 after parity acceptance.
Existing backup, mouse, configuration, and other original functions remain M1.
Intermediate releases are milestones within M1, not a reduced definition of parity.
User confirmation is recorded in `decisions/DM-01M64CWKX056PQDXX2D3QW7QR0.md`.

The [compatibility contract](../../docs/compatibility.md) is binding. The exact
reference executable is now fingerprinted and its Help date confirmed; no
Wordling compatibility is claimed. The [selected primary target](../../docs/decisions/0001-reference-release.md)
is plain 5.1 DOS, U.S. English dated 1992-03-09, without excluding other original
language/character obligations.
The downloaded manuals span several publication dates and must be reconciled.

`main` is the GitHub integration/PR destination. Spec Kitty's `meta.json`
`target_branch` is `feat/wp51-parity`, the mission's planning placement; these are
distinct surfaces in this runtime. No mission merge or implementation acceptance
is requested by starting this specification.

## User Scenarios & Testing *(mandatory)*

Priorities establish sequencing, not optional scope. Source groups and initial
acceptance probes are in the [inventory](../../docs/research/feature-inventory.md).
These user-story scenarios are requirements. Bounded command-level original observations are linked below; no Wordling scenario has been verified.

### User Story 1 — Keep original documents usable (Priority: P1)

A user retrieves, edits, and saves original documents without conversion or loss.

**Independent Test**: Execute the same bounded edits in Wordling and the declared
WP 5.1 reference, inspect native data, then retrieve and print both results in WP.

**Acceptance Scenarios**:

1. **Given** a valid original file, **when** it is opened and saved without edits,
   **then** the output bytes are identical (FC-002).
2. **Given** text containing attributes and an opaque valid structure, **when** a
   supported edit is made elsewhere, **then** unrelated content and relationships
   survive and WP retrieves the file without repair or feature loss (FC-003/004).
3. **Given** a new document, **when** the user saves through the original workflow,
   **then** WP 5.1 retrieves, edits, prints, and saves it, and Wordling reopens it.
4. **Given** a failed or unsafe save, **when** the error occurs, **then** the
   original file remains intact and the user receives an explicit failure.

### User Story 2 — Work through the original editor (Priority: P1)

An experienced user uses the original keys, prompts, menus, selection, search,
Reveal Codes, and editing semantics without relearning the word processor.

**Independent Test**: Replay a recorded reference session and compare command
state, cursor position, code effects, prompts, selections, and saved results.

**Acceptance Scenarios**:

1. **Given** a formatted paragraph, **when** Alt-F3 is used and a code is deleted,
   **then** both panes reflect the same change and the saved file matches its
   documented semantic effect (REF51 pp. 112-113, 560-562).
2. **Given** a code embedded in text, **when** typeover reaches it, **then** the
   code remains and inserted characters follow original behavior (p. 695).
3. **Given** three deletions made with declared commands, **when** the original
   Undelete choices are used, **then** text and code restoration match the
   reference command-specific behavior (p. 697).
4. **Given** nested menus, both document screens, and search prompts, **when**
   original navigation/cancel commands are replayed, **then** document and prompt
   state match the reference, including unchanged state after cancellation.

### User Story 3 — Format and print the same document (Priority: P1)

A user produces the original formatted pages, including advanced structures.

**Independent Test**: Compare page boundaries, glyph positioning, codes, and
printer output with fixed page settings and identical declared resource metrics.

**Acceptance Scenarios**:

1. **Given** fixed printer/font resources and layout settings, **when** margins,
   tabs, columns, headers, notes, and hard/soft breaks are combined, **then**
   pagination, numbering, and printed positions agree with the reference.
2. **Given** a table, equation, and anchored graphics box, **when** they are edited
   and printed, **then** structure, calculations, placement, wrapping, and saved
   representation retain original semantics.
3. **Given** original index/contents/authorities marks and subdocuments, **when**
   generation runs, **then** entries, order, numbering, and references agree.
4. **Given** selected original printer resources, **when** print-to-disk is used,
   **then** the documented original printer-language output is retained.
5. **Given** a graphics-capable reference preview, **when** preview is opened on
   Linux, **then** the original page-inspection functions have a tested Linux
   presentation; terminal limitations cannot silently remove this obligation.

### User Story 4 — Automate and manage work (Priority: P2)

A user reuses original macros, merge files, styles, dictionaries, spreadsheet
links, and utilities without migrating those artifacts to new formats.

**Independent Test**: Run fixtures and keystrokes through both applications,
compare generated files and results, and reopen them in the original app.

**Acceptance Scenarios**:

1. **Given** an original macro or merge workflow, **when** it is executed or
   edited, **then** commands, variables, expressions, prompts, output, and errors
   agree with the declared reference (including native serialization).
2. **Given** text, language resources, sort keys, or a spreadsheet link, **when**
   original language/data tools operate, **then** original results and update
   behavior are reproduced using declared resources.
3. **Given** backup, configuration, utility, and file-management workflows,
   **when** exercised on Linux, **then** original artifact semantics remain and
   platform adaptations are documented and tested.

### Edge Cases

- Truncated prefixes, invalid pointers, cyclic packet chains, bad lengths,
  mismatched delimiters, deeply nested text, and malformed encrypted data.
- Unrecognized valid code/packet types; edits that invalidate opaque references.
- Paired attributes crossing selections, deletion boundaries and subdocuments.
- Typeover at code boundaries, cancellation during prompts, and deletion history.
- Unsupported characters, missing fonts/dictionaries/graphics, and printer changes.
- Hard/soft pages, tables spanning pages, note continuation, and field regeneration.
- Wrong passwords, interrupted writes, disk exhaustion, permissions, and shared
  file conflicts. No failing path may damage the original or report false success.
- Terminal resize, unavailable modified function keys, and graphics presentation.
- DOS paths, 8.3 names and case handling versus Linux paths; explicit mappings
  must be resolved before compatibility claims for those workflows.

### Key Entities *(include if feature involves data)*

- **Native document**: original bytes, text/codes, prefix packets, nested content,
  resources and their relationships; its saved representation obeys FC rules.
- **Formatting code**: a meaningful editable function affecting surrounding or
  nested content, not just a terminal style.
- **Companion artifact**: a macro, merge data source, style/keyboard definition or
  original resource with independently researched format and behavior.
- **Reference installation**: fingerprinted WP 5.1 DOS release and declared resources.
- **Feature obligation**: stable ID, source/version/locator, behavior, and evidence
  tracked separately for documentary study, implementation and verification.
- **Acceptance scenario**: declared starting data, keystrokes/settings, original
  output, expected invariants and durable test result.
- **Adaptation decision**: explicit Linux mapping of an original platform behavior
  with its compatibility implications and owner decisions where scope changes.

## Success Criteria *(mandatory)*

### Measurable Outcomes

- **SC-001**: Every original documented feature and utility is accounted for with
  zero unexplained omissions after full coverage reconciliation.
- **SC-002**: Every M1 obligation has passing original-app comparison evidence;
  100% of accepted native fixtures satisfy FC-001 through FC-009 as applicable.
- **SC-003**: Every declared original workflow produces matching editing, generated
  document and printed semantics under the recorded baseline/resources.
- **SC-004**: Zero unresolved destructive data mismatches, false-success saves or
  undisclosed format substitutions remain at parity acceptance.
- **SC-005**: Every platform adaptation and version discrepancy has a recorded,
  tested disposition; exclusions require an explicit owner-approved scope change.

## Research dependencies and readiness

The expanded inventory contains 91 coverage groups and 236 representative draft
scenarios, not a final command catalogue. A thematic map covers all 36 lessons in
the pinned 1989 workbook; step-level lesson coverage remains incomplete.
Fifty-three bounded original-app recipes have matched expectations in two fresh
runs each, including code-pair repair and Undelete, overwrite refusal, macro
control flow, nested handlers and loops, value/iteration control, file-level
BREAK contexts and merge page settings across nested primary/secondary files. An
independently rebuilt profile passed a menu probe. The setup timestamp variation
is investigated separately without normalizing original files. This is partial
observation, not full scenario verification. [Execution evidence and limits](reference-execution.md).
No Wordling application implementation exists yet. Before detailed
work-package finalization, close these research decisions or provide bounded
investigation packages with explicit outputs:

1. Extend the fingerprinted selected target to historical DOS, graphics-capable
   printer/font and other language/resource profiles; the current FreeDOS and
   mixed-date text-only printer profile has explicit limits.
2. Reconcile the 6/92 reference, 11/89 workbook and 12/93 toolkit with that baseline.
3. Decompose compound groups and cover every command, appendix and workbook lesson.
4. Specify resource formats, encryption, graphics/preview and Linux platform mappings.
5. Define corpus provenance, reproducible comparison procedures and layout tolerances.
6. Establish measured latency/size targets and parser resource/fuzz budgets.

The 2026-10-30 [discovery checkpoint](research.md) consolidates the existing evidence and open investigations for planning. Full research coverage, implementation and parity acceptance remain incomplete. Bounded implementation packages may proceed only on explicit supported contracts; unresolved behavior must receive an investigation package rather than an invented implementation.


=== IMMUTABLE_MISSION_AUTHORITY_CONTEXT_NOT_CURRENT_SOURCE_GUARD 066ab94fc3e0340f3881bb80977d3c93c17c149a:docs/compatibility.md ===
# Permanent compatibility contract

These are project requirements, not claims about an implemented application.
They apply to both missions.

| ID | Obligation | Acceptance evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| FC-001 | Save native documents as original WP 5.1 files. No replacement container, private version, or required sidecar. | Original WP 5.1 opens, edits, prints, and saves Wordling-created files; Wordling reopens the results. |
| FC-002 | An untouched valid supported input saves byte-identically. | SHA-256 and direct byte comparison, including prefix packets and embedded data. Opening must not silently normalize the source. |
| FC-003 | Editing preserves unrelated codes, packet content, embedded resources, and semantic relationships. | Targeted edit fixtures; binary differences explained; original-app retrieval and behavior checked. Offset/length changes are permitted when required, arbitrary normalization is not. |
| FC-004 | Unknown or not-yet-supported structures are preserved. Unsafe edits/saves fail explicitly before replacing the original. | Opaque-code and packet fixtures, reference checks, rejected-edit and failed-save scenarios with original bytes unchanged. |
| FC-005 | Original companion artifacts retain their original formats wherever the app reads/writes them. | Per-format fixtures and original-app checks for macros, styles, merge data, keyboard definitions, and user resources; scope expanded from manual research. |
| FC-006 | Characters preserve their WP character-set identities and round-trip. | Character-set corpus; unsupported input receives an explicit error or choice before modification, never silent replacement. |
| FC-007 | Native saves are independent of conversion through another word processor. | The Go format writer emits original-format data directly; external tools may be independent test witnesses only. |
| FC-008 | Original export/conversion and print-to-disk modes retain their documented formats and semantics. | Per-mode comparison against original output with encoding, resources, and conversion losses declared. These modes do not replace the native save format. |
| FC-009 | A failed save must not corrupt the existing file. | Tests for permissions, interrupted/short writes, full storage, and failed replacement; original backup conventions researched and preserved. |
| FC-010 | PDF is a new, optional M2 export. It never becomes the editable native document format. | M2 PDF acceptance tests against the shared layout output and native-file preservation checks. |

## Fidelity levels

Byte identity is required for no-edit native round-trips. After an edit, the whole
file need not have identical bytes: lengths, pointers, and related state may need
to change. Differences must be confined to the intended edit and required
structural updates, with unrelated data preserved.

Behavioral fidelity includes command sequences, prompts, code/cursor semantics,
selection, error handling, pagination, and rendered/printed results. A matching
screen alone does not establish format or behavioral compatibility.

Exact pagination needs a declared printer, font metrics, resources, settings, and
reference output. Linux printer delivery may differ operationally; document
layout or original print-to-disk bytes must not change without an explicit,
tested adaptation decision.

## Reference baseline and remaining installation work

- Primary documentary target: plain WP 5.1 DOS, U.S. English, 1992-03-09.
  See [ADR 0001](decisions/0001-reference-release.md). Selection does not narrow
  the original language/character compatibility obligations.
- The executable and 158 installed files are fingerprinted in
  [the baseline registry](research/reference-baseline.json). Fifty-three bounded original
  reference recipes have been repeated; Wordling and full scenarios remain unverified.
- The [current reference environment](research/reference-environment/README.md)
  uses FreeDOS 1.4 and a July 1992 text-only printer resource alongside the
  confirmed March program. Historical DOS and graphics/pagination profiles remain
  to be established; this is not an untouched March shipping-set claim.
- OS/emulator, keyboard, display settings, and filesystem behavior.
- Installed printer definitions, fonts, dictionaries, hyphenation resources.
- Repeatable input fixtures and keystroke captures with provenance.
- Differences between the 6/92 reference manual, the 11/89 workbook, and later
  toolkit documentation; record applicability rather than assuming equivalence.

Encrypted documents, graphics preview, legacy printer resources, DOS integration,
and resource licensing remain explicit research obligations. Uncertainty does not
remove them from M1 scope or establish support.
