// Package editor implements a pure, revision-bound session reducer. Native edits
// and history use document; filesystem execution stays with the host adapter.
package editor

import (
	"bytes"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io/fs"
	"strings"

	"github.com/lynncoleart/wordling/internal/document"
	"github.com/lynncoleart/wordling/internal/storage"
)

type Mode string

const (
	Editing          Mode = "Editing"
	RetrieveName     Mode = "RetrieveName"
	SaveName         Mode = "SaveName"
	ReplaceQuestion  Mode = "ReplaceQuestion"
	ExitSaveQuestion Mode = "ExitSaveQuestion"
	ExitQuestion     Mode = "ExitQuestion"
	Waiting          Mode = "Waiting"
	Exited           Mode = "Exited"
	DeleteQuestion   Mode = "DeleteQuestion"
	HistoryPreview   Mode = "HistoryPreview"
	MacroName        Mode = "MacroName"
	MacroReady       Mode = "MacroReady"
	MacroRunning     Mode = "MacroRunning"
)

const MacroError Mode = "MacroError"

type Command uint8

const (
	Text     Command = iota
	Retrieve         // Original Shift+F10.
	Save             // Original F10.
	Reveal           // Original Alt+F3.
	F1
	Escape
	F7
	Home
	Up
	Right
	Left
	Backspace
	Enter
	Yes
	No
	Complete
	CancelOperation // Host safety operation; not an original key alias.
	Insert          // Original Insert mode toggle.
	Delete
	Block          // Original Alt+F4 selection start.
	Previous       // Original Undelete numeric 2.
	Restore        // Original Undelete numeric 1.
	Macro          // Original Alt+F10.
	SwitchDocument // Original Shift+F3; bounded two-screen context.
	RetryFirstRead // Explicit Linux host-safety retry; no original-key alias.
)

type IntentKind string

const (
	Read             IntentKind = "Read"
	Observe          IntentKind = "Observe"
	WriteSnapshot    IntentKind = "WriteSnapshot"
	PublishCandidate IntentKind = "PublishCandidate"
	ReadMacro        IntentKind = "ReadMacro"
)

// Location is a declared rooted profile, not DOS path emulation. Empty Prefix
// accepts flat basenames only. A nonempty exact Prefix is for a separately
// declared comparison/profile binding; it never resolves arbitrary host paths.
type Location struct {
	Prefix    string
	Directory *storage.Directory
}
type Config struct{ Locations []Location }

// Intent is an immutable request value. Host code performs the actual dependency
// call and returns the same Operation, Revision and Kind in its Result.
type Intent struct {
	firstProof             *firstRequest
	macroProof             *macroReadProof
	Operation, Revision    uint64
	Kind                   IntentKind
	Directory              *storage.Directory
	Name                   string
	Document               *storage.Document
	Destination            *storage.Destination
	Decision               storage.Decision
	Candidate              []byte
	NativeRevision         document.Revision
	MacroRun, MacroRequest uint64
	MacroParent            [32]byte
	Repeat                 uint32
	Screen                 uint8
}
type Result struct {
	firstObservation    *firstObservation
	macroMissing        bool
	macroProof          *macroReadProof
	macroObservation    *macroReadObservation
	Operation, Revision uint64
	Kind                IntentKind
	Document            *storage.Document
	Macro               *storage.Macro
	Destination         *storage.Destination
	Outcome             storage.Outcome
	Err                 error
}
type Event struct {
	Command Command
	Text    string
	Result  *Result
}

type SaveState string

const (
	NotSaved         SaveState = "NotSaved"
	SameFileNoOp     SaveState = "SameFileNoOp"
	NewNamePublished SaveState = "NewNamePublished"
	SaveFailed       SaveState = "SaveFailed"
	SaveUncertain    SaveState = "SaveUncertain"
)

// Receipt preserves typed failures/residue, including detached late operations.
// Applied=false never means a filesystem action was rolled back or cancelled.
type Receipt struct {
	Result              Result
	Applied             bool
	DestinationMayExist bool
	Candidate           string
}

type Refusal struct{ Reason string }

func (r *Refusal) Error() string { return "editor: unsupported or invalid transition: " + r.Reason }
func refuse(reason string) error { return &Refusal{reason} }

// Session is a persistent value. All transitions return a copy; dependency
// documents/cursors are immutable. Call New; the zero Session is not initialized.
type Session struct {
	newPhase                  NewPhase
	genesis                   document.Revision
	first                     *firstPublication
	initialized               bool
	transition                *transitionIdentity
	bufferID                  *bufferIdentity
	screen                    uint8
	other                     *Session
	macroOwner                *macroIdentity
	macroDelivery             bool
	macroReady                *macroAcquisition
	newMacro                  *newMacroTarget
	genesisHistory            document.HistoryRevision
	macroRepeat               uint32
	locations                 []Location
	mode, resume              Mode
	source                    *storage.Document
	native                    *document.Document
	cursor                    document.Cursor
	revision, sequence        uint64
	name, draft, original     string
	caret, homes              int
	touched, fromExit         bool
	editedPrefill             bool
	pending                   *Intent
	target                    *storage.Destination
	targetDir                 *storage.Directory
	targetName                string
	lastSave                  SaveState
	published                 Receipt
	last, detached            Receipt
	notice                    string
	history                   document.History
	historyValid, historyUsed bool
	over                      bool
	selection                 bool
	anchor                    document.Cursor
	deletion                  document.Deletion
	preview                   document.Preview
	saved                     document.Revision
	publishedRevision         document.Revision
	publishedName             string
	provenance                Command
}

type Snapshot struct {
	NewPhase                                                        NewPhase
	GenesisRevision                                                 document.Revision
	FirstPublication                                                Receipt
	Screen                                                          uint8
	MacroRun                                                        uint64
	MacroRepeat                                                     uint32
	Mode                                                            Mode
	Prompt, Name, Draft, OriginalName, Notice                       string
	Caret, HomePrefix, Gap                                          int
	Reveal                                                          bool
	Revision                                                        uint64
	Pending                                                         *Intent
	SaveState                                                       SaveState
	Published                                                       Receipt
	Last, Detached                                                  Receipt
	Dirty, Typeover, Selection, HistoryQualified, HistoryCountKnown bool
	SelectionStart, SelectionEnd, HistoryCount, PreviewIndex        int
	SavedRevision, NativeRevision, PublishedRevision                document.Revision
	PublishedName                                                   string
	Provenance                                                      Command
}

func New(config Config) (Session, error) {
	locations := append([]Location(nil), config.Locations...)
	seen := map[string]bool{}
	for i, l := range locations {
		if l.Directory == nil || l.Directory.ID() == (storage.FileID{}) || seen[l.Prefix] {
			return Session{}, refuse("location requires unique prefix and pinned directory")
		}
		seen[l.Prefix] = true
		l.Directory = copyDirectory(l.Directory)
		locations[i] = l
	}
	native, err := document.New(document.NewConfig{Profile: document.HistoricalUS51DosTextStandardPrinter})
	if err != nil {
		return Session{}, err
	}
	cursor, err := native.CursorAt(0)
	if err != nil {
		return Session{}, err
	}
	history, err := document.NewHistory(native)
	if err != nil {
		return Session{}, err
	}
	return Session{initialized: true, transition: &transitionIdentity{1}, bufferID: &bufferIdentity{1}, screen: 1, macroRepeat: 1, locations: locations, mode: Editing, lastSave: NotSaved, native: copyNative(native), cursor: cursor, history: history, historyValid: true, genesisHistory: history.Revision(), newPhase: NewUnpublished, genesis: native.Revision(), revision: 1}, nil
}
func (s Session) Snapshot() Snapshot {
	x := Snapshot{Mode: s.mode, Name: s.name, Draft: s.draft, OriginalName: s.original, Notice: s.notice, Caret: s.caret, HomePrefix: s.homes, Gap: s.cursor.Gap(), Reveal: s.cursor.Reveal(), Revision: s.revision, SaveState: s.lastSave, Last: cloneFirstReceipt(s.last), Detached: cloneFirstReceipt(s.detached)}
	x.NewPhase, x.GenesisRevision = s.newPhase, s.genesis
	if s.first != nil {
		x.FirstPublication = firstReceipt(s.first.result, true, s.first.possible, s.first.candidate)
	}
	x.Dirty = s.dirty()
	x.Typeover, x.Selection = s.over, s.selection
	x.SelectionStart, x.SelectionEnd = s.anchor.Gap(), s.cursor.Gap()
	x.HistoryQualified, x.HistoryCountKnown, x.HistoryCount = s.historyValid && s.history.Qualified(), s.historyValid && s.history.CountKnown(), s.history.Count()
	x.PreviewIndex = s.preview.Index()
	x.NativeRevision, x.SavedRevision, x.PublishedRevision = s.native.Revision(), s.saved, s.publishedRevision
	x.PublishedName, x.Provenance = s.publishedName, s.provenance
	x.Published = receipt(s.published.Result, s.published.Applied)
	x.Pending = copyIntent(s.pending)
	x.Screen = s.screen
	x.MacroRepeat = s.macroRepeat
	if s.macroOwner != nil {
		x.MacroRun = s.macroOwner.run
	}
	switch s.mode {
	case DeleteQuestion:
		x.Prompt = s.deletion.Prompt()
	case HistoryPreview:
		x.Prompt = s.preview.Prompt()
	case MacroName:
		x.Prompt = "Macro: " + s.draft
	case RetrieveName:
		x.Prompt = "Document to be retrieved: " + s.draft + " (List Files)"
	case SaveName:
		x.Prompt = "Document to be saved: " + s.draft
	case ReplaceQuestion:
		x.Prompt = "Replace " + s.draft + "? No (Yes)"
	case ExitSaveQuestion:
		x.Prompt = "Save document? Yes (No)"
		if !s.dirty() {
			x.Notice = "(Text was not modified)"
		}
	case ExitQuestion:
		x.Prompt = "Exit WP? No (Yes) (Cancel to return to document)"
	}
	return x
}
func (s Session) Document() *document.Document { return copyNative(s.native) }
func (s Session) Source() *storage.Document    { return copySource(s.source) }
func (s Session) Present(start, count int) (document.Presentation, error) {
	if s.native == nil {
		return document.Presentation{}, refuse("no retrieved document")
	}
	return s.cursor.Present(start, count)
}
func (s Session) begin(kind IntentKind, dir *storage.Directory, name string) (Session, *Intent, error) {
	if kind == ReadMacro && s.native != nil && s.native.Birth().IsNew() && !s.newMacro.unedited(s) {
		return s, nil, refuse("New macro admission no longer owns naming target")
	}
	s.sequence++
	request := Intent{Operation: s.sequence, Revision: s.revision, Kind: kind, Directory: dir, Name: name, Document: s.source, Destination: s.target, Decision: storage.NoOverwrite}
	request.NativeRevision = s.native.Revision()
	if s.native != nil && kind != ReadMacro {
		request.Candidate, _ = s.native.Export()
	}
	if kind == ReadMacro {
		request.Document = nil
		request.Destination = nil
		request.MacroRun = request.Operation
		request.Repeat = s.macroRepeat
		request.Screen = s.screen
		request.macroProof = newMacroReadProof(request, s.bufferID)
		if s.newMacro != nil {
			request.macroProof.target = s.newMacro
			request.macroProof.issued = &transitionIdentity{1}
			request.macroProof.native = request.NativeRevision
		}
		s.macroRepeat = 1
	}
	s.pending = copyIntent(&request)
	s.resume = s.mode
	s.mode = Waiting
	s.notice = ""
	return s, copyIntent(s.pending), nil
}
func (s Session) resolve(name string) (*storage.Directory, string, error) {
	var best *Location
	for i := range s.locations {
		l := &s.locations[i]
		if strings.HasPrefix(name, l.Prefix) && (best == nil || len(l.Prefix) > len(best.Prefix)) {
			best = l
		}
	}
	if best == nil {
		return nil, "", refuse("filename is outside declared rooted locations")
	}
	leaf := strings.TrimPrefix(name, best.Prefix)
	if leaf == "" || leaf == "." || leaf == ".." || len(leaf) > storage.MaxNameBytes || strings.ContainsAny(leaf, "/\\:\x00") || strings.HasPrefix(leaf, ".wordling-candidate-") {
		return nil, "", refuse("only bounded flat basenames in a declared location")
	}
	return best.Directory, leaf, nil
}
func (s Session) filename(mode Mode) Session {
	s.mode = mode
	s.draft = s.name
	s.original = s.name
	s.caret = 0
	s.touched = false
	s.editedPrefill = false
	s.target = nil
	s.targetDir = nil
	s.targetName = ""
	s.notice = ""
	if mode == RetrieveName || mode == MacroName {
		s.draft = ""
		s.original = ""
	}
	return s
}
func (s Session) clear() Session {
	s.newPhase = ""
	s.first = nil
	s.genesis = document.Revision{}
	s.source = nil
	s.native = nil
	s.cursor = document.Cursor{}
	s.name = ""
	s.draft = ""
	s.original = ""
	s.caret = 0
	s.homes = 0
	s.revision++
	s.mode = Editing
	s.lastSave = NotSaved
	s.historyValid = false
	s.saved = document.Revision{}
	s.selection = false
	s.deletion = document.Deletion{}
	s.preview = document.Preview{}
	s.fromExit = false
	s.target = nil
	s.notice = ""
	return s
}

func (s Session) Reduce(event Event) (Session, *Intent, error) {
	if s.mode == Editing && s.macroRepeat != 1 && event.Command != Macro {
		return s, nil, refuse("qualified Macro repeat setup must be followed by Macro")
	}
	if s.macroOwner != nil && !s.macroDelivery {
		return s, nil, refuse("active macro owns delivery; use execution Step/Resume/HostStop")
	}

	next, intent, err := s.reduce(event)
	if err != nil && event.Command != Complete {
		return s, nil, err
	}
	if err == nil && event.Command != Complete {
		next.provenance = event.Command
		if intent != nil && intent.firstProof != nil {
			next.transition = intent.firstProof.transition
		} else if intent != nil && intent.macroProof != nil && intent.macroProof.target != nil {
			next.transition = intent.macroProof.issued
		} else {
			next = next.advanced()
		}
	}
	return next, intent, err
}
func (s Session) reduce(event Event) (Session, *Intent, error) {
	if !s.initialized {
		return s, nil, refuse("uninitialized session")
	}
	if event.Command == Complete {
		if event.Result == nil || event.Text != "" {
			return s, nil, refuse("completion requires result only")
		}
		return s.complete(*event.Result)
	}
	if event.Result != nil || (event.Command != Text && event.Text != "") {
		return s, nil, refuse("mixed event payload")
	}
	if s.newPhase == PublishedUnbound {
		if event.Command == RetryFirstRead {
			return s.retryFirstRead()
		}
		return s, nil, refuse("published New requires Read-only binding retry")
	}
	if s.newPhase == PublicationUncertain {
		return s, nil, refuse("New publication effect unresolved; host reconciliation required")
	}
	if s.mode == Exited {
		return s, nil, refuse("session exited")
	}
	if s.mode == Waiting {
		if s.pending != nil && (s.pending.Kind == PublishCandidate || s.pending.Kind == WriteSnapshot) && editingCommand(event.Command) {
			old := s
			s.mode = Editing
			next, _, err := s.reduce(event)
			if err != nil {
				return old, nil, err
			}
			next.mode = Waiting
			return next, nil, nil
		}
		if event.Command != CancelOperation {
			return s, nil, refuse("operation pending; host cancellation is separate from original keys")
		}
		if s.pending != nil && s.pending.firstProof != nil {
			p := s.pending.firstProof
			if p.kind == PublishNewBuffer {
				s.newPhase = PublicationUncertain
				s.first = &firstPublication{request: p, result: Result{Kind: p.kind, Operation: p.operation, Revision: p.revision}, possible: true}
				s.lastSave = SaveUncertain
			} else {
				s.newPhase = PublishedUnbound
				s.lastSave = SaveUncertain
			}
		}
		s.pending = nil
		s.newMacro = nil
		s.mode = s.resume
		s.notice = "Operation detached; filesystem completion still requires reconciliation"
		return s, nil, nil
	}
	if s.mode == MacroError {
		if event.Command == F1 {
			s.mode = Editing
			s.notice = ""
			return s, nil, nil
		}
		return s, nil, refuse("missing macro recovery requires observed F1")
	}
	if s.mode == MacroReady && event.Command == CancelOperation {
		s.newMacro = nil
		s.mode = Editing
		s.notice = "Host discarded unstarted macro; source snapshot retained"
		return s, nil, nil
	}
	if event.Command == CancelOperation {
		return s, nil, refuse("no pending operation")
	}
	if s.mode == DeleteQuestion || s.mode == HistoryPreview {
		return s.edit(event)
	}
	if s.mode == RetrieveName || s.mode == SaveName || s.mode == MacroName {
		return s.editName(event)
	}
	switch s.mode {
	case ReplaceQuestion:
		switch event.Command {
		case No:
			s.mode = SaveName
			s.caret = 0
			s.touched = false
			s.target = nil
			return s, nil, nil
		case Yes:
			if s.source == nil {
				return s, nil, &storage.Failure{Kind: storage.UnsupportedReplacement, Stage: "New replacement", Name: s.targetName}
			}

			raw, _ := s.native.Export()
			if !bytes.Equal(raw, s.source.Export()) {
				return s, nil, &storage.Failure{Kind: storage.UnsupportedReplacement, Stage: "edited replacement", Name: s.targetName}
			}
			identity, _ := s.target.Identity()
			if identity.ID != s.source.Identity().ID {
				return s, nil, &storage.Failure{Kind: storage.UnsupportedReplacement, Stage: "session replacement decision", Name: s.targetName}
			}
			next, _, err := s.begin(WriteSnapshot, s.targetDir, s.targetName)
			request := *next.pending
			request.Decision = storage.AllowOverwrite
			next.pending = &request
			return next, copyIntent(next.pending), err
		}
	case ExitSaveQuestion:
		switch event.Command {
		case F1:
			s.mode = Editing
			return s, nil, nil
		case Escape:
			s = s.filename(SaveName)
			s.fromExit = true
			return s, nil, nil
		case No:
			s.mode = ExitQuestion
			return s, nil, nil
		}
	case ExitQuestion:
		switch event.Command {
		case F1:
			s.mode = Editing
			return s, nil, nil
		case No, Escape:
			return s.clear(), nil, nil
		case Yes:
			s = s.clear()
			s.mode = Exited
			return s, nil, nil
		}
	case Editing:
		if s.selection && event.Command != Right && event.Command != Backspace && event.Command != F1 {
			return s, nil, refuse("only forward block movement/Backspace and qualified F1 cleanup")
		}
		if s.homes > 0 && event.Command != Home && event.Command != Up {
			return s, nil, refuse("unobserved Home-prefix interruption")
		}
		switch event.Command {
		case SwitchDocument:
			return s.switchDocument()
		case Macro:
			if s.native == nil || s.selection || s.homes != 0 {
				return s, nil, refuse("macro invocation requires qualified loaded editing context")
			}
			newTarget := s.native.Birth().IsNew()
			if newTarget && !s.pristineNewMacro() {
				return s, nil, refuse("macro requires pristine initial New Doc1")
			}
			if h, err := s.interrupted(); err != nil {
				return s, nil, err
			} else {
				s.history = h
			}
			if newTarget {
				s.newMacro = s.captureNewMacro()
			}
			return s.filename(MacroName), nil, nil
		case Retrieve:
			if s.source != nil || (s.native != nil && s.native.Revision() != s.genesis) {
				return s, nil, refuse("insertion into an existing buffer is unimplemented")
			}
			return s.filename(RetrieveName), nil, nil
		case Save:
			if s.source == nil && s.newPhase != NewUnpublished {
				return s, nil, refuse("new document writer unavailable")
			}
			next, err := s.saveHistoryInterleaving()
			if err != nil {
				return s, nil, err
			}
			s = next
			s.fromExit = false
			return s.filename(SaveName), nil, nil
		case F7:
			if h, err := s.interrupted(); err != nil {
				return s, nil, err
			} else {
				s.history = h
			}
			s.mode = ExitSaveQuestion
			return s, nil, nil
		case Text, Insert, Delete, Backspace, Block, F1, Previous, Restore:
			return s.edit(event)
		case Reveal:
			if s.native == nil {
				return s, nil, refuse("Reveal requires retrieved document")
			}
			if s.selection {
				return s, nil, refuse("view change during block is unqualified")
			}
			if s.historyValid && s.history.Qualified() {
				h, err := s.history.InterruptGrouping()
				if err != nil {
					return s, nil, err
				}
				s.history = h
			}
			s.cursor = s.cursor.WithReveal(!s.cursor.Reveal())
			return s, nil, nil
		case Left, Right:
			if s.native == nil {
				return s, nil, refuse("cursor requires retrieved document")
			}
			direction := 1
			if event.Command == Left {
				direction = -1
			}
			cursor, err := s.cursor.Move(direction)
			if err != nil {
				return s, nil, err
			}
			if s.historyValid && s.history.Qualified() {
				var h document.History
				if event.Command == Right {
					h, err = s.history.Separate(document.RightBoundary)
				} else {
					h, err = s.history.InterruptGrouping()
				}
				if err != nil {
					return s, nil, err
				}
				s.history = h
			}
			s.cursor = cursor
			s.provenance = event.Command
			return s, nil, nil
		case Home:
			if s.native == nil || s.homes == 3 {
				return s, nil, refuse("Home prefix outside observed bounded sequence")
			}
			if h, err := s.interrupted(); err != nil {
				return s, nil, err
			} else {
				s.history = h
			}
			s.homes++
			return s, nil, nil
		case Up:
			if s.homes != 2 && s.homes != 3 {
				return s, nil, refuse("only Home2/3 Up endpoints observed")
			}
			gap := 0
			if s.native.TokenCount() > 0 {
				token, _ := s.native.Token(0)
				if token.Kind == document.Opaque {
					return s, nil, refuse("opaque initial code semantics unknown")
				}
				if s.homes == 2 && token.Kind == document.BoldOn {
					gap = 1
				}
			}
			cursor, err := s.native.CursorAt(gap)
			if err != nil {
				return s, nil, err
			}
			s.cursor = cursor.WithReveal(s.cursor.Reveal())
			s.homes = 0
			return s, nil, nil
		}
	}
	return s, nil, refuse(fmt.Sprintf("command %d in %s", event.Command, s.mode))
}

func (s Session) editName(event Event) (Session, *Intent, error) {
	switch event.Command {
	case Macro:
		if s.mode == SaveName {
			return s, nil, nil
		}
		return s, nil, refuse("macro nesting in this filename prompt unqualified")
	case F1:
		s.newMacro = nil
		s.mode = Editing
		s.fromExit = false
		s.notice = ""
		return s, nil, nil
	case Escape:
		if s.mode == RetrieveName {
			s.mode = Editing
			s.notice = ""
			return s, nil, nil
		}
	case Text:
		if len(event.Text) > storage.MaxNameBytes {
			return s, nil, refuse("filename draft safety bound")
		}
		if event.Text == "" {
			return s, nil, refuse("empty filename text")
		}
		for _, r := range event.Text {
			if r < 0x20 || r > 0x7e {
				return s, nil, refuse("filename requires explicit ASCII profile")
			}
		}
		draft, caret := s.draft, s.caret
		if !s.touched {
			s.editedPrefill = false
			draft = ""
			caret = 0
		}
		if len(draft)+len(event.Text) > storage.MaxNameBytes {
			return s, nil, refuse("filename draft safety bound")
		}
		s.draft = draft[:caret] + event.Text + draft[caret:]
		s.caret = caret + len(event.Text)
		s.touched = true
		return s, nil, nil
	case Right:
		if s.mode == RetrieveName || s.mode == MacroName {
			return s, nil, refuse("Retrieve filename editing unobserved")
		}
		if s.caret >= len(s.draft) {
			return s, nil, refuse("filename caret boundary")
		}
		if !s.touched && s.draft != "" {
			s.editedPrefill = true
		}
		s.caret++
		s.touched = true
		return s, nil, nil
	case Backspace:
		if s.mode == RetrieveName || s.mode == MacroName {
			return s, nil, refuse("Retrieve filename editing unobserved")
		}
		if s.caret == 0 {
			return s, nil, refuse("filename caret boundary")
		}
		s.draft = s.draft[:s.caret-1] + s.draft[s.caret:]
		s.caret--
		s.touched = true
		return s, nil, nil
	case Enter:
		if s.mode == SaveName && s.editedPrefill {
			return s, nil, refuse("prefilled filename edit has only cancel evidence")
		}
		if s.fromExit {
			return s, nil, refuse("save-on-exit completion not observed; F1 can return to document")
		}
		resolve := s.resolve
		if s.mode == MacroName {
			resolve = s.resolveMacro
		}
		dir, name, err := resolve(s.draft)
		if err != nil {
			return s, nil, err
		}
		if s.mode == MacroName {
			return s.begin(ReadMacro, dir, name)
		}
		if s.mode == RetrieveName {
			return s.begin(Read, dir, name)
		}
		if s.source == nil && s.newPhase != NewUnpublished {
			return s, nil, refuse("new document writer unavailable")
		}
		s.lastSave = NotSaved
		return s.begin(Observe, dir, name)
	}
	return s, nil, refuse("unobserved filename command")
}

func receipt(result Result, applied bool) Receipt {
	result = copyResult(result)
	record := Receipt{Result: result, Applied: applied, DestinationMayExist: (!applied || result.Err != nil) && result.Outcome.State == storage.Published}
	var failure *storage.Failure
	if errors.As(result.Err, &failure) {
		copy := *failure
		record.Result.Err = &copy
		// A typed storage failure is authoritative about its transaction stage.
		record.DestinationMayExist = failure.DestinationMayExist
		record.Candidate = failure.Candidate
	}
	return record
}
func (s Session) complete(result Result) (Session, *Intent, error) {
	result = copyResult(result)
	if s.pending != nil && s.pending.firstProof != nil {
		if result.Operation != s.pending.Operation || result.Revision != s.pending.Revision || result.Kind != s.pending.Kind {
			s.detached = receipt(result, false)
			return s, nil, nil
		}
		return s.completeFirst(result)
	}
	if s.pending == nil || result.Operation != s.pending.Operation || result.Revision != s.pending.Revision || result.Kind != s.pending.Kind {
		if s.newMacro != nil && s.pending != nil && s.pending.Kind == ReadMacro {
			return s, nil, refuse("foreign New macro root result")
		}
		s = s.advanced()
		s.newMacro = nil
		s.detached = receipt(result, false)
		return s, nil, nil
	}
	request := *s.pending
	if request.Kind == ReadMacro {
		if s.newMacro != nil || (request.macroProof != nil && request.macroProof.target != nil) {
			if !s.validNewMacroRead(&request) {
				return s, nil, refuse("New macro root is stale or foreign")
			}
		}
		var err error
		result, err = acceptedMacroResult(&request, result, s.bufferID)
		if err != nil {
			return s, nil, err
		}
	}
	if result.Revision != s.revision {
		s.pending = nil
		s.mode = Editing
		s = s.advanced()
		s.detached = receipt(result, false)
		s.notice = "Older revision completed; current edits and name retained"
		return s, nil, nil
	}
	s = s.advanced()
	s.pending = nil
	s.mode = s.resume
	s.last = receipt(result, true)
	if result.Err != nil {
		s.notice = result.Err.Error()
		if request.Kind == ReadMacro {
			s.newMacro = nil
			s.mode = Editing
			s.notice = "Macro acquisition failed: " + result.Err.Error()
			if result.macroMissing {
				display := s.draft
				if !strings.HasSuffix(display, ".WPM") {
					display += ".WPM"
				}
				s.mode = MacroError
				s.notice = "ERROR: File not found -- " + display
			}
		}
		if request.Kind == Read && errors.Is(result.Err, fs.ErrNotExist) {
			s.notice = "ERROR: File not found -- " + s.draft
			s.touched = false
			s.caret = 0
		}
		if (request.Kind == WriteSnapshot || request.Kind == PublishCandidate) || request.Kind == Observe {
			s.lastSave = SaveFailed
			if s.last.DestinationMayExist {
				s.lastSave = SaveUncertain
			}
		}
		return s, nil, nil
	}
	invalid := func(reason string) (Session, *Intent, error) {
		s.notice = reason
		result.Err = refuse(reason)
		s.last = receipt(result, true)
		if (request.Kind == WriteSnapshot || request.Kind == PublishCandidate) || request.Kind == Observe {
			s.lastSave = SaveFailed
		}
		return s, nil, refuse(reason)
	}
	switch request.Kind {
	case ReadMacro:
		macro := result.Macro
		if macro == nil || macro.Name() != request.Name || macro.DirectoryID() != request.Directory.ID() || macro.Image() == nil || macro.Image().SHA256() != macro.Identity().SHA256 {
			return invalid("macro result identity mismatch")
		}
		s.macroReady = &macroAcquisition{request: *copyIntent(&request), source: copyMacro(macro), ready: s.transition, observation: result.macroObservation}
		s.mode = MacroReady
		s.notice = ""
	case Read:
		if result.Document == nil || result.Document.Name() != request.Name || result.Document.DirectoryID() != request.Directory.ID() {
			return invalid("read result identity mismatch")
		}
		native, err := document.Parse(result.Document.Export())
		if err != nil {
			s.notice = err.Error()
			result.Err = err
			s.last = receipt(result, true)
			return s, nil, err
		}
		cursor, err := native.CursorAt(0)
		if err != nil {
			return s, nil, err
		}
		s.newPhase = ""
		s.first = nil
		s.genesis = document.Revision{}
		s.source = copySource(result.Document)
		s.native = copyNative(native)
		if !s.historyUsed {
			h, err := document.NewHistory(native)
			if err == nil {
				s.history = h
				s.historyValid = true
			}
		}
		s.historyUsed = true
		s.saved = native.Revision()
		s.publishedRevision = document.Revision{}
		s.published = Receipt{}
		s.publishedName = ""
		s.cursor = cursor
		s.name = s.draft
		s.mode = Editing
		s.revision++
		s.homes = 0
		s.notice = ""
		s.lastSave = NotSaved
	case Observe:
		target := result.Destination
		if target == nil || target.Name() != request.Name || target.DirectoryID() != request.Directory.ID() {
			return invalid("destination result identity mismatch")
		}
		s.target = copyDestination(target)
		s.targetDir = copyDirectory(request.Directory)
		s.targetName = request.Name
		if target.Exists() {
			s.mode = ReplaceQuestion
			return s, nil, nil
		}
		if s.newPhase == NewUnpublished {
			return s.firstIntent(PublishNewBuffer, request.Directory, request.Name, nil)
		}
		kind := WriteSnapshot
		if s.native != nil {
			raw, _ := s.native.Export()
			if !bytes.Equal(raw, s.source.Export()) {
				kind = PublishCandidate
			}
		}
		return s.begin(kind, request.Directory, request.Name)
	case WriteSnapshot, PublishCandidate:
		out := result.Outcome
		if out.Name != request.Name || out.DirectoryID != request.Directory.ID() || out.Identity.Size != int64(len(request.Candidate)) || out.Identity.SHA256 != sha256.Sum256(request.Candidate) {
			return invalid("save result identity mismatch")
		}
		switch out.State {
		case storage.Unchanged:
			if request.Kind != WriteSnapshot || request.Decision != storage.AllowOverwrite || !request.Destination.Exists() || out.Identity.ID != s.source.Identity().ID {
				return invalid("invalid same-file no-op result")
			}
			s.lastSave = SameFileNoOp
		case storage.Published:
			// A real published candidate has an inode. Device zero can be valid;
			// this structural check cannot authenticate arbitrary host IO claims.
			if out.Identity.ID.Inode == 0 {
				return invalid("published destination has no inode identity")
			}
			if request.Destination.Exists() {
				return invalid("existing destination cannot be published")
			}
			s.lastSave = NewNamePublished
		default:
			return invalid("unknown save outcome")
		}
		s.mode = Editing
		s.name = s.draft
		s.saved = request.NativeRevision
		s.publishedRevision, s.publishedName = request.NativeRevision, s.name
		s.published = receipt(result, true)
		if s.historyValid {
			h, err := s.history.Separate(document.SavedBoundary)
			if err != nil {
				// Publication already happened. Preserve its receipt and native
				// revision, but never retain permission after a failed barrier.
				s.historyValid = false
				s.notice = err.Error()
				return s, nil, err
			}
			s.history = h
		}
		s.notice = ""
	default:
		return invalid("unknown operation result")
	}
	return s, nil, nil
}


=== ACTUAL_APPROVED_H73_UNCHANGED_E_G_READER_SOURCE 5bd5105e9014a839ea95c5b2efd21548ccd8756b:internal/editor/editing.go ===
package editor

import (
	"errors"
	"github.com/lynncoleart/wordling/internal/document"
	"github.com/lynncoleart/wordling/internal/storage"
)

func copyDirectory(p *storage.Directory) *storage.Directory {
	if p == nil {
		return nil
	}
	v := *p
	return &v
}
func copySource(p *storage.Document) *storage.Document {
	if p == nil {
		return nil
	}
	v := *p
	return &v
}
func copyDestination(p *storage.Destination) *storage.Destination {
	if p == nil {
		return nil
	}
	v := *p
	return &v
}
func copyNative(p *document.Document) *document.Document {
	if p == nil {
		return nil
	}
	v := *p
	return &v
}
func copyIntent(p *Intent) *Intent {
	if p == nil {
		return nil
	}
	v := *p
	v.Directory = copyDirectory(p.Directory)
	v.Document = copySource(p.Document)
	v.Destination = copyDestination(p.Destination)
	v.Candidate = append([]byte(nil), p.Candidate...)
	return &v
}
func copyResult(r Result) Result {
	r.Document = copySource(r.Document)
	r.Macro = copyMacro(r.Macro)
	r.Destination = copyDestination(r.Destination)
	// Host-owned arbitrary diagnostic causes cannot be universally deep-cloned.
	switch e := r.Err.(type) {
	case *storage.Failure:
		if e != nil {
			v := *e
			r.Err = &v
		}
	case *document.Failure:
		if e != nil {
			v := *e
			r.Err = &v
		}
	case *Refusal:
		if e != nil {
			v := *e
			r.Err = &v
		}
	}
	return r
}
func (s Session) dirty() bool {
	if s.newPhase == NewUnpublished {
		return s.native.Revision() != s.genesis
	}
	if s.newPhase == AwaitPublish || s.newPhase == AwaitFirstRead || s.newPhase == PublishedUnbound || s.newPhase == PublicationUncertain {
		return true
	}
	return s.native != nil && s.native.Revision() != s.saved
}
func editingCommand(c Command) bool { return c == Text || c == Insert || c == Right || c == Left }

// History and Preview are copied handles over the dependency's private immutable
// state. These handles preserve pre-interleaving evidence; current command
// permission is reported by Snapshot, not by an earlier handle. Unknown
// CountKnown never becomes empty, including after clear/retrieve.
func (s Session) History() document.History { return s.history }
func (s Session) Preview(start, count int) (document.Overlay, error) {
	return s.preview.Present(start, count, s.cursor.Reveal())
}

func (s Session) applyDeletion(r document.Deletion, c Command) Session {
	old := s.native.Revision()
	s.native = r.Document()
	s.cursor = r.Cursor()
	s.history = r.History()
	s.deletion = r
	s.selection = r.BlockActive()
	if s.native.Revision() != old {
		s.revision++
		s.lastSave = NotSaved
	}
	s.provenance = c
	if r.Pending() {
		s.mode = DeleteQuestion
	} else {
		s.mode = Editing
	}
	return s
}
func (s Session) edit(e Event) (Session, *Intent, error) {
	if s.native == nil {
		return s, nil, refuse("command requires a loaded document")
	}
	if s.mode == DeleteQuestion {
		var a document.DeleteAnswer
		switch e.Command {
		case Yes:
			a = document.Yes
		case No:
			a = document.No
		case F1:
			a = document.F1
		case Escape:
			a = document.Escape
		default:
			return s, nil, refuse("answer required for exact deletion question")
		}
		r, err := s.native.AnswerDeletion(s.history, s.deletion, a)
		if err != nil {
			return s, nil, err
		}
		return s.applyDeletion(r, e.Command), nil, nil
	}
	if s.mode == HistoryPreview {
		switch e.Command {
		case Previous:
			p, err := s.preview.Previous()
			if err != nil {
				return s, nil, err
			}
			s.preview = p
		case F1:
			h, err := s.history.CancelPreview(s.preview)
			if err != nil {
				return s, nil, err
			}
			s.history = h
			s.preview = document.Preview{}
			s.mode = Editing
		case Restore:
			r, err := s.native.Restore(s.history, s.preview)
			if err != nil {
				return s, nil, err
			}
			s = s.applyDeletion(r, e.Command)
			s.preview = document.Preview{}
		default:
			return s, nil, refuse("only original Undelete 1/2/F1 are qualified")
		}
		return s, nil, nil
	}
	if s.selection && e.Command == F1 && s.deletion.BlockActive() {
		r, err := s.native.CancelBlock(s.history, s.deletion)
		if err != nil {
			return s, nil, err
		}
		return s.applyDeletion(r, e.Command), nil, nil
	}
	switch e.Command {
	case Text:
		if s.selection {
			return s, nil, refuse("typing in block is unqualified")
		}
		var r document.EditResult
		var err error
		if s.over {
			r, err = s.native.TypeoverASCII(s.cursor, []byte(e.Text))
		} else {
			r, err = s.native.InsertASCII(s.cursor, []byte(e.Text))
		}
		if err != nil {
			return s, nil, err
		}
		h := s.history
		if s.historyValid {
			h, err = h.AfterEdit(r)
			if err != nil {
				return s, nil, err
			}
		}
		old := s.native.Revision()
		s.native = copyNative(r.Document)
		s.cursor = r.Cursor
		s.history = h
		if s.native.Revision() != old {
			s.revision++
			s.lastSave = NotSaved
		}
	case Insert:
		if s.selection {
			return s, nil, refuse("mode toggle during selection unqualified")
		}
		s.over = !s.over
		if s.historyValid && s.history.Qualified() {
			h, err := s.history.InterruptGrouping()
			if err != nil {
				return s, nil, err
			}
			s.history = h
		}
	case Block:
		if !s.historyValid || s.selection || !s.cursor.Reveal() {
			return s, nil, refuse("only forward Reveal block qualification")
		}
		s.selection = true
		s.anchor = s.cursor
		s.deletion = document.Deletion{}
	case Delete, Backspace:
		if !s.historyValid {
			return s, nil, refuse("history is not qualified for this document lifecycle")
		}
		var r document.Deletion
		var err error
		if s.selection {
			if e.Command != Backspace {
				return s, nil, refuse("only observed selected-block Backspace is qualified")
			}
			r, err = s.native.DeleteBlock(s.anchor, s.cursor, s.history)
		} else {
			d := document.ForwardDelete
			if e.Command == Backspace {
				d = document.Backspace
			}
			r, err = s.native.Delete(s.cursor, d, s.history)
		}
		if err != nil {
			return s, nil, err
		}
		return s.applyDeletion(r, e.Command), nil, nil
	case F1:
		if s.native.Birth().IsNew() && s.native.Revision() == s.genesis && s.history.Count() == 0 {
			return s, nil, refuse("startup empty Undelete is not qualified")
		}
		if s.selection {
			return s, nil, refuse("block cancellation requires declined block question")
		}
		if !s.historyValid {
			return s, nil, refuse("history is unknown, not empty")
		}
		p, err := s.history.Open(s.cursor)
		if err != nil {
			return s, nil, err
		}
		if p.Active() {
			s.preview = p
			s.mode = HistoryPreview
		}
	default:
		return s, nil, refuse("unsupported editing command")
	}
	s.provenance = e.Command
	return s, nil, nil
}

// Filename/exit/prefix interleavings do not silently join deletion groups.
// Already unqualified history is retained; native safety is independent.
func (s Session) interrupted() (document.History, error) {
	if !s.historyValid || !s.history.Qualified() {
		return s.history, nil
	}
	return s.history.InterruptGrouping()
}

// saveHistoryInterleaving separates known filename/publication behavior from
// optional hidden-history knowledge. Only Save receives this bounded waiver;
// other interleavings continue to use the dependency's exact guards.
func (s Session) saveHistoryInterleaving() (Session, error) {
	if !s.historyValid {
		return s, nil
	}
	h, err := s.history.InterruptGrouping()
	if err == nil {
		s.history = h
		return s, nil
	}
	var failure *document.Failure
	if !s.history.CountKnown() && errors.As(err, &failure) && failure.Category == document.UnsupportedHistoryEffect {
		// Bounded paired-code observations admit immediate history only; an
		// already-unqualified real history stays unavailable as well.
		// F10 is independently known, but its post-interleaving history is not.
		s.historyValid = false
		return s, nil
	}
	return s, err
}

package editor

import (
	"bytes"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"fmt"

	"github.com/lynncoleart/wordling/internal/document"
	"github.com/lynncoleart/wordling/internal/storage"
)

// NewPhase describes Linux host safety, not additional DOS prompts.
type NewPhase string

const (
	NewUnpublished       NewPhase = "NewUnpublished"
	AwaitPublish         NewPhase = "AwaitPublish"
	AwaitFirstRead       NewPhase = "AwaitFirstRead"
	PublishedUnbound     NewPhase = "PublishedUnbound"
	PublicationUncertain NewPhase = "PublicationUncertain"
	NewAnchored          NewPhase = "NewAnchored"
)
const (
	PublishNewBuffer IntentKind = "PublishNewBuffer"
	BindNewSource    IntentKind = "BindNewSource"
)

// RequestKey is an opaque issued-request identity for once-per-request host
// scheduling. It confers neither IO authority nor transaction idempotence.
type RequestKey struct{ proof *firstRequest }

func (k RequestKey) Valid() bool { return k.proof != nil }
func (i Intent) RequestKey() RequestKey {
	if !i.firstProof.matches(&i) {
		return RequestKey{}
	}
	return RequestKey{i.firstProof}
}

type firstRequest struct {
	transition          *transitionIdentity
	buffer              *bufferIdentity
	birth               document.Birth
	native              document.Revision
	operation, revision uint64
	screen              uint8
	kind                IntentKind
	directory           storage.Directory
	target              storage.Destination
	name, display       string
	candidate           []byte
	publication         *firstPublication
}
type firstPublication struct {
	request   *firstRequest
	result    Result
	possible  bool
	candidate string
}
type firstObservation struct {
	request   *firstRequest
	outcome   storage.Outcome
	source    *storage.Document
	err       *sealedError
	possible  bool
	candidate string
}

// sealedError owns diagnostic values. Unwrap returns fresh typed failures so
// errors.As on a public diagnostic cannot mutate the private observation.
type sealedError struct {
	message  string
	failures []storage.Failure
}

func (e *sealedError) Error() string { return e.message }
func (e *sealedError) Unwrap() []error {
	out := make([]error, len(e.failures))
	for i, f := range e.failures {
		v := f
		out[i] = &v
	}
	return out
}
func freezeFirstError(err error, invoked bool) (*sealedError, bool, string) {
	if err == nil {
		return nil, false, ""
	}
	out := &sealedError{}
	possible, candidate := false, ""
	budget := 64
	var walk func(error, bool)
	walk = func(e error, covered bool) {
		if e == nil {
			return
		}
		budget--
		if budget < 0 {
			possible = true
			return
		}
		if f, ok := e.(*storage.Failure); ok && f != nil {
			v := *f
			v.Cause = nil
			out.failures = append(out.failures, v)
			out.message += fmt.Sprintf("storage: %s during %s (%q); ", v.Kind, v.Stage, v.Name)
			if candidate == "" {
				candidate = v.Candidate
			}
			switch v.Kind {
			case storage.InvalidRequest, storage.UnsafePath, storage.ReadFailed, storage.InputLimit, storage.NativeRefused, storage.StaleSource, storage.DestinationChanged, storage.OverwriteRequired, storage.UnsupportedReplacement, storage.CandidateFailed, storage.PublicationFailed, storage.CleanupFailed:
			default:
				possible = true
			}
			possible = possible || v.DestinationMayExist
			walk(f.Cause, true)
			return
		}
		if many, ok := e.(interface{ Unwrap() []error }); ok {
			for _, child := range many.Unwrap() {
				walk(child, covered)
			}
			return
		}
		if one, ok := e.(interface{ Unwrap() error }); ok {
			walk(one.Unwrap(), covered)
			return
		}
		if !covered {
			possible = true
		}
		out.message += e.Error() + "; "
	}
	walk(err, false)
	if !invoked {
		possible = false
	}
	return out, possible, candidate
}
func (p *firstRequest) matches(i *Intent) bool {
	if p == nil || i == nil || i.firstProof != p || i.Directory == nil {
		return false
	}
	if i.Kind != p.kind || i.Operation != p.operation || i.Revision != p.revision || i.NativeRevision != p.native || i.Screen != p.screen || *i.Directory != p.directory || i.Name != p.name || i.Document != nil || i.Decision != storage.NoOverwrite || i.MacroRun != 0 || i.MacroRequest != 0 || i.MacroParent != ([32]byte{}) || i.Repeat != 0 || i.macroProof != nil {
		return false
	}
	if !bytes.Equal(i.Candidate, p.candidate) {
		return false
	}
	if p.kind == PublishNewBuffer {
		return i.Destination != nil && *i.Destination == p.target
	}
	return i.Destination == nil
}
func (s Session) firstIntent(kind IntentKind, directory *storage.Directory, name string, pub *firstPublication) (Session, *Intent, error) {
	if s.native == nil || !s.native.Birth().IsNew() {
		return s, nil, refuse("first publication requires actual New birth")
	}
	raw, err := s.native.Export()
	if err != nil {
		return s, nil, err
	}
	if s.sequence == ^uint64(0) || s.revision == 0 {
		return s, nil, refuse("first request counter exhausted or invalid")
	}
	s.sequence++
	p := &firstRequest{transition: &transitionIdentity{1}, buffer: s.bufferID, birth: s.native.Birth(), native: s.native.Revision(), operation: s.sequence, revision: s.revision, screen: s.screen, kind: kind, directory: *directory, name: name, display: s.draft, candidate: append([]byte(nil), raw...), publication: pub}
	var target *storage.Destination
	if kind == PublishNewBuffer {
		if s.target == nil || s.target.Exists() {
			return s, nil, refuse("requires observed absent target")
		}
		p.target = *s.target
		target = copyDestination(s.target)
		s.newPhase = AwaitPublish
	} else {
		s.newPhase = AwaitFirstRead
		p.display = pub.request.display
	}
	i := &Intent{firstProof: p, Operation: p.operation, Revision: p.revision, Kind: kind, Directory: copyDirectory(directory), Name: name, Destination: target, Candidate: append([]byte(nil), raw...), NativeRevision: p.native, Screen: p.screen}
	s.transition = p.transition
	s.pending = copyIntent(i)
	s.resume = Editing
	s.mode = Waiting
	return s, copyIntent(i), nil
}
func firstResult(i *Intent, kind IntentKind) (Result, *firstRequest) {
	r := Result{Kind: kind}
	if i == nil {
		r.Err = refuse("nil first request")
		return r, nil
	}
	r.Operation, r.Revision = i.Operation, i.Revision
	p := i.firstProof
	if !p.matches(i) || p.kind != kind {
		r.Err = refuse("first request wrapper differs from accepted authority")
		return r, nil
	}
	return r, p
}

// ExecuteNewPublication performs actual41 IO outside Reduce, on the captured
// opened capability. Public wrapper values can refuse but never redirect IO.
func ExecuteNewPublication(i *Intent) Result {
	r, p := firstResult(i, PublishNewBuffer)
	if p == nil {
		return r
	}
	id := storage.PublicationID{Operation: p.operation, Revision: p.revision}
	prepared, err := p.directory.PrepareNewPublication(&p.target, p.candidate, id)
	invoked := false
	if err == nil {
		invoked = true
		r.Outcome, err = p.directory.PublishNew(prepared, id)
	}
	frozen, possible, candidate := freezeFirstError(err, invoked)
	if r.Outcome.State == storage.Published && err != nil {
		possible = true
	}
	o := &firstObservation{request: p, outcome: r.Outcome, err: frozen, possible: possible, candidate: candidate}
	r.firstObservation = o
	if frozen != nil {
		r.Err = frozen
	}
	return r
}

// ExecuteFirstRead observes the same retained capability/name. It cannot
// publish, re-Observe, choose another root or replace the live native object.
func ExecuteFirstRead(i *Intent) Result {
	r, p := firstResult(i, BindNewSource)
	if p == nil {
		return r
	}
	source, err := p.directory.Read(p.name)
	frozen, _, candidate := freezeFirstError(err, false)
	o := &firstObservation{request: p, source: copySource(source), err: frozen, candidate: candidate}
	r.firstObservation = o
	r.Document = copySource(source)
	if frozen != nil {
		r.Err = frozen
	}
	return r
}
func firstReceipt(r Result, applied, possible bool, candidate string) Receipt {
	return Receipt{Result: copyResult(r), Applied: applied, DestinationMayExist: possible, Candidate: candidate}
}
func cloneFirstReceipt(r Receipt) Receipt { r.Result = copyResult(r.Result); return r }
func (s Session) completeFirst(r Result) (Session, *Intent, error) {
	i := s.pending
	p := i.firstProof
	o := r.firstObservation
	invalid := func() (Session, *Intent, error) {
		return s, nil, refuse("first completion lacks exact current private observation")
	}
	if p == nil || !p.matches(i) || p.transition != s.transition || p.buffer != s.bufferID || p.birth != s.native.Birth() || p.native != s.native.Revision() || p.revision != s.revision || p.screen != s.screen || o == nil || o.request != p || r.Operation != p.operation || r.Revision != p.revision || r.Kind != p.kind || r.Macro != nil || r.Destination != nil || r.macroProof != nil || r.macroObservation != nil || r.Outcome != o.outcome {
		return invalid()
	}
	if o.err == nil {
		if r.Err != nil {
			return invalid()
		}
	} else {
		e, ok := r.Err.(*sealedError)
		if !ok || e != o.err {
			return invalid()
		}
	}
	if (r.Document == nil) != (o.source == nil) {
		return invalid()
	}
	if r.Document != nil && *r.Document != *o.source {
		return invalid()
	}
	s.pending = nil
	s.mode = Editing
	s = s.advanced()
	s.last = firstReceipt(r, true, o.possible, o.candidate)
	if p.kind == PublishNewBuffer {
		if o.err != nil {
			s.notice = o.err.Error()
			s.lastSave = SaveFailed
			s.newPhase = NewUnpublished
			if o.possible {
				s.newPhase = PublicationUncertain
				s.lastSave = SaveUncertain
				s.first = &firstPublication{p, copyResult(r), true, o.candidate}
			}
			return s, nil, nil
		}
		outcome := o.outcome
		if outcome.State != storage.Published || outcome.Name != p.name || outcome.DirectoryID != p.directory.ID() || outcome.Identity.ID.Inode == 0 || outcome.Identity.Size != int64(len(p.candidate)) || outcome.Identity.SHA256 != sha256.Sum256(p.candidate) {
			s.newPhase = PublicationUncertain
			s.lastSave = SaveUncertain
			s.notice = "Invalid publication observation; destination effect unresolved"
			s.first = &firstPublication{p, copyResult(r), true, o.candidate}
			return s, nil, refuse(s.notice)
		}
		s.first = &firstPublication{p, copyResult(r), false, ""}
		s.lastSave = NotSaved
		return s.firstIntent(BindNewSource, &p.directory, p.name, s.first)
	}
	// No Parse: acquisition provides only the permanent first guard.
	pub := p.publication
	if pub == nil || s.first != pub {
		return invalid()
	}
	source := o.source
	if o.err != nil || source == nil || source.Name() != p.name || source.DirectoryID() != p.directory.ID() || source.Identity() != pub.result.Outcome.Identity || !bytes.Equal(source.Export(), p.candidate) {
		s.newPhase = PublishedUnbound
		s.lastSave = SaveUncertain
		s.notice = "Published but first rooted Read did not bind; explicit Read-only retry required"
		return s, nil, nil
	}
	s.source = copySource(source)
	s.newPhase = NewAnchored
	s.saved = p.native
	s.name = pub.request.display
	s.publishedName = s.name
	s.publishedRevision = p.native
	s.published = firstReceipt(pub.result, true, false, "")
	s.lastSave = NewNamePublished
	s.notice = ""
	if s.historyValid {
		h, err := s.history.Separate(document.SavedBoundary)
		if err != nil {
			s.historyValid = false
		} else {
			s.history = h
		}
	}
	return s, nil, nil
}
func (s Session) retryFirstRead() (Session, *Intent, error) {
	if s.newPhase != PublishedUnbound || s.first == nil || s.pending != nil {
		return s, nil, refuse("no published-unbound receipt to retry")
	}
	p := s.first.request
	return s.firstIntent(BindNewSource, &p.directory, p.name, s.first)
}

// Package wp51 validates the bounded WP 5.1 DOS document outer framing
// described in contracts/native-framing.md. It preserves exact native bytes;
// acceptance establishes neither semantic validity nor safe semantic editing.
package wp51

import (
	"bytes"
	"encoding/binary"
	"fmt"
	"sort"
)

// Safety policies for this reader slice, not original application format limits.
const (
	MaxInputBytes                 = 16 << 20
	MaxIndexBlocks                = 4096
	MaxPacketEntries              = 16384
	OuterFramedPayloadUnvalidated = "outer-framed, payload-unvalidated"
)

// Category distinguishes unsupported variants from malformed input and limits.
type Category string

const (
	UnsupportedFormat     Category = "UnsupportedFormat"
	UnsupportedProduct    Category = "UnsupportedProduct"
	UnsupportedFileType   Category = "UnsupportedFileType"
	UnsupportedVersion    Category = "UnsupportedVersion"
	UnsupportedEncryption Category = "UnsupportedEncryption"
	UnsupportedVariant    Category = "UnsupportedVariant"
	UnsupportedStructure  Category = "UnsupportedStructure"
	InputLimit            Category = "InputLimit"
	TraversalLimit        Category = "TraversalLimit"
	TruncatedInput        Category = "TruncatedInput"
	MalformedPrefix       Category = "MalformedPrefix"
	MalformedBody         Category = "MalformedBody"
)

// Failure reports an absolute byte offset and the violated field or boundary.
// Detail further identifies cases such as Cycle and DelimiterMismatch.
type Failure struct {
	Category      Category
	Offset        int
	Field, Detail string
}

func (e *Failure) Error() string {
	return fmt.Sprintf("wp51: %s at %d (%s): %s", e.Category, e.Offset, e.Field, e.Detail)
}
func fail(c Category, o int, field, detail string) error { return &Failure{c, o, field, detail} }

// Span is an absolute half-open byte range, represented by offset and length.
type Span struct{ Offset, Length int }

// Block describes a checked special index block in native traversal order.
type Block struct {
	Data  Span
	Count int
	Next  uint32
}

// Packet retains its type, index relationship and opaque bounded data span.
type Packet struct {
	Type        uint16
	Index, Data Span
}

// Inspection describes framing only. Packet and function payloads are unvalidated.
// Every slice returned by Inspect is independent of the image.
type Inspection struct {
	Validation      string
	Prefix, Body    Span
	Blocks          []Block
	Packets         []Packet
	OuterTokenCount int
}

// Image owns its complete immutable input. Construct accepted images with Parse.
// Bytes not referenced by metadata remain preserved in this authoritative image.
type Image struct {
	data []byte
	info Inspection
}

// Export returns a fresh, byte-identical no-edit copy, including all gaps/tails.
func (i *Image) Export() []byte { return bytes.Clone(i.data) }

// Inspect returns an independent copy of framing metadata, without byte aliases.
func (i *Image) Inspect() Inspection {
	c := i.info
	c.Blocks = append([]Block(nil), c.Blocks...)
	c.Packets = append([]Packet(nil), c.Packets...)
	return c
}

// Bytes returns a fresh copy of a bounded span, or false for an invalid range.
func (i *Image) Bytes(s Span) ([]byte, bool) {
	if s.Offset < 0 || s.Length < 0 || s.Offset > len(i.data) || s.Length > len(i.data)-s.Offset {
		return nil, false
	}
	return bytes.Clone(i.data[s.Offset : s.Offset+s.Length]), true
}

// Parse checks identity, prefix/index/packet boundaries and body outer framing.
// The size limit is checked before copying. A refusal returns no accepted image
// and never changes caller data. Callers must not mutate input during Parse.
// File acquisition/replacement and semantic mutation belong to other packages.
func Parse(input []byte) (*Image, error) {
	if len(input) > MaxInputBytes {
		return nil, fail(InputLimit, 0, "input", "exceeds 16 MiB")
	}
	if len(input) < 16 {
		return nil, fail(TruncatedInput, len(input), "header", "requires 16 bytes")
	}
	if !bytes.Equal(input[:4], []byte{0xff, 'W', 'P', 'C'}) {
		return nil, fail(UnsupportedFormat, 0, "signature", "expected FF575043")
	}
	if input[8] != 1 {
		return nil, fail(UnsupportedProduct, 8, "product", "expected 1")
	}
	if input[9] != 10 {
		return nil, fail(UnsupportedFileType, 9, "file type", "only document type 10 is supported")
	}
	if input[10] != 0 || input[11] != 1 {
		return nil, fail(UnsupportedVersion, 10, "stored version", "expected 0/1")
	}
	if u16(input, 12) != 0 {
		return nil, fail(UnsupportedEncryption, 12, "password", "encrypted input is unsupported")
	}
	if u16(input, 14) != 0 {
		return nil, fail(UnsupportedVariant, 14, "reserved header", "nonzero reserved value")
	}
	boundary := u32(input, 4)
	if boundary < 16 || uint64(boundary) > uint64(len(input)) {
		return nil, fail(MalformedPrefix, 4, "body offset", "outside [16,EOF]")
	}
	b := int(boundary)
	info := Inspection{Validation: OuterFramedPayloadUnvalidated, Prefix: Span{0, b}, Body: Span{b, len(input) - b}}
	if err := prefix(input, b, &info); err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	count, err := body(input, b)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	info.OuterTokenCount = count
	return &Image{data: bytes.Clone(input), info: info}, nil
}
func u16(b []byte, o int) uint16 { return binary.LittleEndian.Uint16(b[o : o+2]) }
func u32(b []byte, o int) uint32 { return binary.LittleEndian.Uint32(b[o : o+4]) }

func prefix(data []byte, b int, info *Inspection) error {
	visited := make(map[int]bool)
	offset := 16
	graphics := false
	done := false
	for !done {
		if visited[offset] {
			return fail(MalformedPrefix, offset, "next block", "Cycle")
		}
		if len(info.Blocks) >= MaxIndexBlocks {
			return fail(TraversalLimit, offset, "index blocks", "exceeds 4096")
		}
		if offset < 16 || offset > b || b-offset < 10 {
			return fail(MalformedPrefix, offset, "block header", "outside prefix or truncated")
		}
		visited[offset] = true
		if u16(data, offset) != 0xfffb {
			return fail(MalformedPrefix, offset, "block type", "expected FFFB")
		}
		count := int(u16(data, offset+2))
		size := int(u16(data, offset+4))
		if count == 0 || size != count*10 {
			return fail(MalformedPrefix, offset+2, "block count/size", "zero count or inconsistent size")
		}
		if size > b-offset {
			return fail(MalformedPrefix, offset+4, "block size", "block crosses body boundary")
		}
		if count != 5 {
			return fail(UnsupportedVariant, offset+2, "block count", "only five-entry document blocks validated")
		}
		next := u32(data, offset+6)
		// Only the followed next pointer is interpreted; an early type 0 ends enumeration.
		block := Block{Data: Span{offset, size}, Count: count, Next: next}
		info.Blocks = append(info.Blocks, block)
		for j := 1; j < count; j++ {
			index := offset + 10*j
			k := u16(data, index)
			if k == 0 {
				done = true
				break
			}
			if graphics {
				return fail(UnsupportedStructure, index, "packet 8 ordering", "graphics index must be last")
			}
			if k == 0xfffb {
				return fail(UnsupportedStructure, index, "packet type", "special header in general entry")
			}
			if len(info.Packets) >= MaxPacketEntries {
				return fail(TraversalLimit, index, "packet entries", "exceeds 16384")
			}
			length, pos := u32(data, index+2), u32(data, index+6)
			if pos < 16 || uint64(pos) > uint64(b) || uint64(length) > uint64(b)-uint64(pos) {
				return fail(MalformedPrefix, index+2, "packet position/length", "outside prefix")
			}
			p := Packet{Type: k, Index: Span{index, 10}, Data: Span{int(pos), int(length)}}
			if k == 8 {
				if length != 2 {
					return fail(MalformedPrefix, index+2, "packet 8 length", "requires two-byte count")
				}
				if u16(data, int(pos)) != 0 {
					return fail(UnsupportedStructure, int(pos), "graphics count", "nonzero graphics extent unvalidated")
				}
				graphics = true
			}
			info.Packets = append(info.Packets, p)
		}
		if done || next == 0 {
			break
		}
		if next < 16 || uint64(next) >= uint64(b) {
			return fail(MalformedPrefix, offset+6, "next block", "outside prefix")
		}
		offset = int(next)
	}
	// Sort temporary spans only. Native metadata and bytes retain original order.
	type extent struct {
		span  Span
		block bool
	}
	ranges := make([]extent, 0, len(info.Blocks)+len(info.Packets))
	for _, block := range info.Blocks {
		ranges = append(ranges, extent{block.Data, true})
	}
	for _, packet := range info.Packets {
		if packet.Data.Length > 0 {
			ranges = append(ranges, extent{packet.Data, false})
		}
	}
	sort.Slice(ranges, func(i, j int) bool { return ranges[i].span.Offset < ranges[j].span.Offset })
	for j := 1; j < len(ranges); j++ {
		prev, cur := ranges[j-1], ranges[j]
		if cur.span.Offset < prev.span.Offset+prev.span.Length {
			if prev.block && cur.block {
				return fail(MalformedPrefix, cur.span.Offset, "index blocks", "overlapping blocks")
			}
			return fail(UnsupportedStructure, cur.span.Offset, "prefix spans", "overlapping packet/index or packet data")
		}
	}
	return nil
}

var fixedWidths = [16]int{4, 9, 11, 3, 3, 5, 6, 7, 4, 5, 6, 6, 8, 10, 10, 12}

func body(data []byte, start int) (int, error) {
	tokens := 0
	for q := start; q < len(data); tokens++ {
		code := data[q]
		remaining := len(data) - q
		if code == 0 || code == 0x7f {
			return 0, fail(MalformedBody, q, "single-byte token", "00 and 7F are not standalone tokens")
		}
		if code < 0xc0 {
			q++
			continue
		}
		if code <= 0xcf {
			width := fixedWidths[code-0xc0]
			if width > remaining {
				return 0, fail(MalformedBody, q, "fixed function", "truncated")
			}
			if data[q+width-1] != code {
				return 0, fail(MalformedBody, q+width-1, "fixed trailer", "DelimiterMismatch")
			}
			q += width
			continue
		}
		if remaining < 4 {
			return 0, fail(MalformedBody, q, "variable header", "truncated")
		}
		length := int(u16(data, q+2))
		if length < 4 {
			return 0, fail(MalformedBody, q+2, "variable length", "requires L>=4")
		}
		if length+4 > remaining {
			return 0, fail(MalformedBody, q+2, "variable length", "span crosses EOF")
		}
		if u16(data, q+length) != uint16(length) || data[q+length+2] != data[q+1] || data[q+length+3] != code {
			return 0, fail(MalformedBody, q+length, "variable trailer", "DelimiterMismatch")
		}
		q += length + 4
	}
	return tokens, nil
}
